### [1] [EvoMisty: Building, Optimizing, and Evolving Capabilities on a Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31341)
*Xiao Wang,Lu Dong,Ifeoma Nwogu,Srirangaraj Setlur,Venu Govindaraju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoMisty是一个基于插件的框架，使编程智能体能够从官方文档构建并持续改进机器人能力，并在物理Misty II机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的方法虽能生成任务程序，但仍依赖人工开发的控制接口和预定义能力，且缺乏根据用户指定指标优化已完成任务、并跨任务复用性能提升的通用机制。

Method: EvoMisty从官方文档构建控制程序，在物理机器人上验证候选能力，将验证后的能力存入原子能力库并与现有技能组合来解题；任务完成后收集执行数据、测量用户指定指标并迭代改进任务程序，再将可复用技能蒸馏到技能库。在Misty II机器人上使用Claude Code和Codex进行评估。

Result: 两个智能体分别构建109和107个已验证原子能力，均完成全部28个AutoMisty基准任务及7个额外任务；优化在五个任务上提升了全部七项评估指标，例如对话响应延迟分别降低52%和71%；复用优化后对话任务的技能使另外三个对话任务延迟降低33%至72%。

Conclusion: EvoMisty能够自动构建、验证并持续改进机器人能力，其技能复用机制可跨任务传播性能提升，代码与实验视频将公开。

Abstract: Social robots face diverse and changing tasks in companionship and education. LLM-based methods can generate task programs, but they still rely on manually developed control interfaces and predefined capabilities. They also lack a general mechanism to optimize completed tasks based on user-specified metrics and reuse performance improvements across this http URL present EvoMisty, a plugin-based framework that enables coding agents to build and continually improve robot capabilities. EvoMisty builds a control program from official documentation and verifies candidate capabilities on a physical robot. Verified capabilities are stored in an atomic capability library and combined with existing skills to solve tasks. After task completion, EvoMisty can collect execution data, measure user-specified metrics, and iteratively improve task programs. It then distills reusable skills from final solutions into a skill library for future this http URL evaluate EvoMisty on a physical Misty II robot using Claude Code and Codex. The two agents construct 109 and 107 verified atomic capabilities, respectively. Both complete all 28 AutoMisty benchmark tasks and seven additional tasks beyond AutoMisty's capabilities. Optimization improves all seven evaluated metrics across five tasks. For example, dialogue response latency decreases by 52% and 71%, respectively. Reusing skills from one optimized dialogue task reduces latency by 33% to 72% in three other dialogue tasks without further optimization. All code and experimental videos will be publicly released through the webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [2] [Ego2Real: Learning Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Visuo-Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32101)
*Ange Bao,Haishan Zhang,Lingzi Xie,Shuang Li,Pei Zhao,Ling Shi,Bidan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ego2Real利用触觉手套为自我中心人类演示增加触觉监督，通过触觉引导的手-物交互重建和接触感知强化学习，将双手灵巧操作技能迁移到拟人机器人手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭视觉难以获取物理接触和施力意图信息，导致手-物交互解释存在歧义，因此需要触觉感知来实现可靠的人类到机器人灵巧技能迁移。

Method: 提出Ego2Real框架，在两个阶段引入触觉监督：(1)触觉引导的手-物交互重建，用触觉信号优化手部姿态与接触几何；(2)接触感知强化学习，将人类接触模式转化为机器人接触奖励，结合精化运动参考与物理接触奖励训练拟人机器人手。

Result: 实验表明，在重建和策略学习中同时引入触觉监督可提高操作成功率。

Conclusion: 用触觉观测丰富自我中心演示能提升其质量与可迁移性，触觉监督对灵巧技能迁移至关重要。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation from egocentric human demonstrations offers a scalable path toward robot skill acquisition. Vision alone, however, provides limited information about physical contacts and the demonstrator's force intent, leading to ambiguities in interpreting hand-object interactions. Capturing human touch via tactile sensing is therefore crucial for reliable human-to-robot dexterous skill transfer. We present Ego2Real, a framework that leverages egocentric demonstrations and tactile gloves to transfer bimanual dexterous manipulation skills to robotic hands. Ego2Real incorporates tactile supervision at two complementary stages: (1) tactile-guided hand-object interaction reconstruction, which uses measured tactile signals to refine hand poses and contact geometry; and (2) contact-informed reinforcement learning, which translates human contact patterns into robotic contact rewards. By combining refined motion references with physical contact rewards during training, Ego2Real guides anthropomorphic robotic hands to emulate demonstrated contact behaviors while adapting to their own morphology and actuation. Experiments show that incorporating tactile supervision into both reconstruction and policy learning yields higher manipulation success rates, underscoring the importance of enriching egocentric demonstrations with tactile observations to improve their quality and transferability.

</details>


### [3] [ActiveBridge: Scaling Active Prior for Robots through Egocentric Perception-Interaction Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32098)
*Zixiao Gu,Qiren Qin,Yu He,Zhengyang Liu,Da Huang,Hongquan Zhang,Simo Wu,Peng Zhou,Guangnan Ye,Yunbo Wang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ActiveBridge训练框架，从人类第一视角视频中学习主动感知先验，通过世界-动作建模迁移至机器人，实现单头部相机下主动控制视角的操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型在非结构化场景中仅被动响应观测，忽略了第一视角视频中人类主动获取信息的先验，导致性能受限。

Method: 设计仅用单头部相机、通过头部动作主动控制视角的机器人；训练指令条件模型联合预测未来第一视角视频与同步的头-腕动作轨迹，以头部动作提供主动感知的显式监督。

Result: 在主动操作任务上ActiveBridge优于基线，消融实验表明头-腕联合动作监督带来收益。

Conclusion: 学习并迁移人类主动感知先验可提升机器人在非结构化场景中的操作性能，头部动作监督是关键。

Abstract: Robotics foundation models are now widely used for manipulation, and egocentric videos offer rich data for scaling. However, existing methods overlook the human active priors captured in egocentric videos, which limits performance in unstructured scenes. They act on given observations instead of actively seeking information as humans do. We propose ActiveBridge, a training framework that learns these human active priors and transfers them to robots through world-action modeling. We design a robot that actively controls its viewpoint through head actions to acquire the visual information needed for manipulation using a single head camera, without wrist cameras. During human training, an instruction-conditioned model jointly predicts future egocentric videos and synchronized head-wrist action trajectories, with head actions providing explicit supervision for active perception. Experiments show that ActiveBridge outperforms baselines on active manipulation tasks, while ablations demonstrate the benefit of joint head-wrist action supervision.

</details>


### [4] [MARGIN: A Risk-Aware Agent for Robot Program Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32093)
*Hongbo Duan,Fan Du,Haipeng Luo,Bin Qian,Zichao Nie,Yilin Mi,Yaopeng Tian,Jiake Tian,Siyuan Liu,Yan Gao,Fangming Liu,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARGIN是一种风险感知机器人编程智能体，利用近失裕度信号将成功执行转换为可操作证据，以优先修复脆弱程序并保留执行余量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人编程智能体通过反复测试与修复提升可执行控制策略，但二元成功指标饱和，导致在未见场景中执行风险仍然存在。

Method: 提出近失裕度指标，衡量最慢成功开发执行中剩余执行预算的比例；用该信号优先处理脆弱程序、引导定向修复并选择在保持开发成功的同时增加执行余量的修订；采用精确安全竞速规则加速基于成功的候选评估。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-90和Robosuite上评估：80任务LIBERO-Pro复现中78个程序达到完美开发成功但存在未见种子失败；近失裕度以AUC 0.774预测留出失败，低裕度程序失败风险高2.97倍；两轮独立评估中执行控制步数分别减少10.7%和15.0%，LIBERO-90和Robosuite减少18.0-20.0%；安全竞速在保持相同选择下将固定序列候选评估回合减少53.5%；配对执行探针通过识别隐藏回归改进接受决策。

Conclusion: 近失证据是风险感知、预算高效机器人程序细化的有效基础。

Abstract: Robot-programming agents improve executable control policies through repeated testing and repair, yet binary success saturates while execution risk persists across unseen scenes. We introduce MARGIN, a risk-aware agent that converts successful robot rollouts into actionable evidence for program refinement. Its near-miss margin measures the fraction of the execution budget remaining in the slowest successful development rollout. The agent uses this signal to prioritize fragile programs, guide targeted repairs, and select revisions that preserve development success while increasing execution headroom. An exact safe-racing rule accelerates success-based candidate evaluation. We evaluate MARGIN across LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-90, and Robosuite. In our 80-task LIBERO-Pro reproduction, 78 programs attain perfect development success while accounting for substantial failures on unseen seeds. The near-miss margin predicts held-out failures with an AUC of 0.774, and low-margin programs exhibit 2.97 times higher failure risk. Across two independent evaluation rounds spanning 37 unique LIBERO-Pro tasks, MARGIN reduces executed control steps by 10.7% and 15.0%, with reductions of 18.0-20.0% on LIBERO-90 and Robosuite. Safe racing reduces fixed-sequence candidate-evaluation episodes by 53.5% while retaining identical selections, and paired execution probes improve acceptance decisions by identifying hidden regressions. Together, these results establish near-miss evidence as an effective basis for risk-aware, budget-efficient robot program refinement.

</details>


### [5] [KeyMemVLA: Subtask-Guided Key Memory for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32081)
*Yongwei Zhang,Tao Chen,Sicheng Ma,Yuanzhe Xing,Pengwei Zhang,Yongen Zhao,Yaoqing Hu,Zhikun She,Ce Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KeyMemVLA，一种基于子任务引导的关键记忆框架，通过两级标注挑选关键帧并联合学习子任务、动作与关键帧预测，在有限记忆预算下解决长时程操作中的历史依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖的长时程操作要求VLA策略推断任务进度并回忆当前观测中缺失的证据；在有限记忆预算下，需保留能消除阶段歧义并支持后续决策的历史信息。

Method: 设计两级标注流程，选取满足进度指示或决策证据保留条件的子任务，并将其最终帧标为关键帧以监督在线记忆写入；共享视觉-语言骨干联合学习子任务、动作和关键帧预测；推理时先根据指令、视觉上下文和记忆预测当前子任务，再以该语义条件预测动作与未来记忆写入位置，执行中在选定时刻保留实际观测；同时提出KMBench基准评估跨子任务的主动记忆保留。

Result: 在RMBench-$M(n)$、RoboTwin-MeM、KMBench及真实世界操作任务上，平均成功率高于对比基线；消融实验与表征分析支持关键记忆和显式任务进度语义的作用。

Conclusion: 将稀疏关键记忆与在线进度推断相结合，对历史依赖控制具有重要价值。

Abstract: Memory-dependent long-horizon manipulation requires vision-language-action (VLA) policies to infer task progress and recall evidence absent from current observations. Under a limited memory budget, the challenge is to retain history that resolves stage ambiguity and supports subsequent decisions. We present KeyMemVLA, a subtask-guided key memory framework based on progress indication and decision-evidence retention. A two-level annotation process selects subtasks satisfying either criterion and labels their final frames as keyframes to supervise online memory writes. A shared vision-language backbone jointly learns subtask, action, and keyframe prediction. At inference, the policy first predicts the current subtask from the instruction, visual context, and memory, then uses this semantic condition to predict actions and future memory-write positions. Actual observations are retained at the selected times during execution. We introduce KMBench to evaluate active memory retention for decisions across subtasks. Evaluations on RMBench-$M(n)$, RoboTwin-MeM, KMBench, and real-world manipulation tasks show higher average success rates than the compared baselines. Ablation studies and representation analysis support the roles of key memory and explicit task-progress semantics. These results highlight the value of coupling sparse key memory with online progress inference for history-dependent control.

</details>


### [6] [RoboHarness-RL: Agentic Reinforcement Learning for VLM-Based Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32016)
*Liyang Li,Mingyu Liu,Xiaoman Li,Zhiyue Zhao,Jin-Chuan Shi,Canyu Zhao,Haokai Zhang,Jinyuan Tian,Linhao Zhong,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboHarness-RL是一个智能体强化学习框架，通过工具调用提升紧凑开源VLM的机器人操作能力，无需VLA或世界动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿VLM可通过智能体工具框架操控机器人，但紧凑开源VLM难以达到可比性能；现有方法依赖视觉-语言-动作模型或世界动作模型。

Method: 先在成功教师演示上做监督微调，再用任务结果奖励进行多轮强化学习；提出LiteHarness，利用分层记忆保持交互上下文紧凑以支持高效多轮训练。

Result: Qwen3.5-9B在RoboTwin 2.0成功率从31.2%升至44.0%，LIBERO-Pro从57.8%升至73.3%；随机化RoboTwin场景从26.4%升至38.0%；行为分析显示重复调用率最多降低4倍、成功执行更短、负反馈恢复能力增强。

Conclusion: 多轮强化学习可在不依赖专门动作模型的情况下显著提升紧凑开源VLM的机器人操作性能与行为效率。

Abstract: Agentic harnesses enable frontier vision-language models (VLMs) to manipulate robots through perception, planning, and control tools, yet achieving comparable performance with compact open-weight VLMs remains challenging. We present RoboHarness-RL, an agentic reinforcement learning framework for improving VLM-based robot manipulation through tool calls. Starting from supervised fine-tuning on successful teacher demonstrations, the VLM further improves through multi-turn reinforcement learning with task-outcome rewards, without relying on vision-language-action models or world action models. To support efficient multi-turn training, we develop LiteHarness, which uses hierarchical memory to keep interaction contexts compact while preserving task performance. With Qwen3.5-9B, RL raises success from 31.2% to 44.0% on RoboTwin 2.0 and from 57.8% to 73.3% on LIBERO-Pro; these gains extend to randomized RoboTwin scenes unseen during training (26.4% to 38.0%). Behavioral analysis further shows that RL reduces repetitive-call rates by up to $4\times$, shortens successful executions, and improves recovery from negative feedback. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [7] [Learning When a Robot Should Ask For Help: Adaptive Intervention Requests in Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31925)
*Yanran Jiang,Cécile Paris,Dana Kulić,Leimin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于离线强化学习的人类干预请求策略，使机器人在人机团队中自适应地决定何时请求帮助，以平衡干预收益与操作员负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自主导航失败时需要人类及时干预，但频繁或不恰当的请求会增加操作员负担并降低团队效率，而延迟请求可能导致长时间停机或不可恢复的故障；同时人类、机器人和任务上下文也影响干预效果，因此需要学习自适应的人类干预请求时机。

Method: 通过离线强化学习训练帮助寻求策略，基于过去干预的有用性和自主进展来学习警报策略，使机器人仅在干预结果可能有益时选择性地请求人类帮助；在多种任务环境和不同用户专业水平的仿真中评估，随后进行物理用户研究。

Result: 学习到的策略提高了探索效率，减少了不必要的警报，并增强了对新手用户的支持，同时未损害专家在实时人机团队任务中的表现。

Conclusion: 基于离线强化学习的人类干预请求策略能够有效改善人机团队协作中的干预时机选择，在减少操作员负担的同时提升整体任务效率与用户体验。

Abstract: Robots operating in complex environments occasionally encounter situations where autonomous navigation fails. Requesting and receiving a timely intervention from their human supervisor can be critical for failure prevention or recovery. However, requesting help too frequently or at inappropriate times can increase operator workload and reduce team efficiency, while delayed requests may lead to prolonged downtime or unrecoverable failures. In addition to alert timing, the human, robot and task context also influences the intervention outcomes. This paper proposes an algorithm for learning to generate adaptive human intervention requests during human-robot teaming. A help-seeking policy is trained via offline reinforcement learning based on past intervention helpfulness and autonomous progress. This learned alert policy enables a robot to selectively request human help when the intervention outcome is likely to be beneficial. We evaluate the proposed policy in different task environments and user expertise levels in simulation, followed by evaluation in a physical user study. Our findings demonstrate that the learned policy improves exploration efficiency, reduces unnecessary alerts, and enhances novice user support without compromising expert performance in real-time human-robot teaming missions.

</details>


### [8] [PreWAM: Exploring the Design of Latent Future Prediction for World-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31901)
*Wan Chi,Sen Li,Zihang Cui,Chuqin Huang,Yunlong Wang,Nanshan Deng,Yanjun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PreWAM世界-动作模型，利用预训练DINO特征构建预测空间，通过残差世界预测和时间尺度预测增强动作生成的泛化性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型融合动作生成与未来预测，但不同预测空间对泛化性和运动信息可恢复性的影响缺乏系统研究，需要找到更优的预测空间以提升策略学习效果。

Method: 通过受控实验发现空间结构化语义特征在视觉扰动下泛化更好且运动信息更易恢复；据此构建PreWAM，以预训练DINO特征定义预测空间，采用残差世界预测联合学习源相对变化与未来状态，并采用时间尺度预测根据时间距离条件化世界查询，预训练与下游策略学习阶段均与动作监督联合优化共享视觉-语言骨干。

Result: 在LIBERO基准系列、SimplerEnv、RoboTwin 2.0及真实机器人操作实验中，PreWAM展现出强操作性能，并在多种分布偏移下具有更好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以预训练DINO特征构建结构化语义预测空间并结合残差与多时间尺度预测目标，可有效提升世界-动作模型的整体泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: World-action models combine action generation with future prediction, allowing visual transitions to serve as supervision for policy learning. Our controlled study of prediction spaces shows that spatially structured semantic features support better overall generalization under visual perturbations and make motion information more readily recoverable from learned predictive representations. Building on these observations, we introduce PreWAM, a world-action model that organizes future supervision around state changes in a prediction space defined by pretrained DINO features. Specifically, Residual World Prediction jointly learns source-relative changes and their resulting future states to emphasize dynamic information while preserving future-state semantics. Temporal-Scale Prediction conditions world queries on temporal distance to capture both short-term local motion and longer-horizon state evolution. These predictive objectives jointly shape a shared vision-language backbone with action supervision during pretraining and downstream policy learning. Experiments across the LIBERO benchmark family, SimplerEnv, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world robotic manipulation demonstrate strong manipulation performance and improved generalization and robustness under diverse distribution shifts.

</details>


### [9] [RESET: One Demonstration, One Verified World, Self-Improved Skills Across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31894)
*Zheng Sun,Zhuo Li,Zhihao Li,Hengyi Sim,Ge Wang,Ziyue Zhu,Mingkai Jia,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RESET从单次真实演示重建接触丰富的仿真世界并导出冻结成功判据，通过开环回放检验世界保真度，以每节点失败证据自进化模块化任务图与技能代码，最终冻结代码零LLM调用部署到真实机器人并跨机器人迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码智能体依赖仿真基准中的真值成功评分器和可重置练习环境，而真实任务两者皆无，导致无法直接自我改进机器人操作技能。

Method: RESET包含五步：Rebuild从单次演示重建接触丰富世界并导出只读判据；Examine通过开环回放和轮廓一致性检查世界；Self-improve依据每节点失败证据进化白盒任务图与技能代码；Execute将冻结代码部署到真实机器人且运行时零LLM调用；Transfer将验证过的世界与判据共享给未见机器人而无需新演示。

Result: 在8个各由单次真实演示重建的操作任务上，RESET在仿真未见布局中平均成功率达97%，而零样本代码为27%、逐任务微调pi0.5策略为31%、Diffusion Policy为50%；注入重建缺陷表明练习容忍多数重建误差，几何误差仍可致停滞或误导，检查能标记多数此类误差且物理随机化覆盖其余；无目标演示时3种异构机器人均习得相同任务技能，相似臂机器人95%技能函数原样复制，不同臂手机器人自行编写；真实机器人实验中程序在4台机器人280次试验中成功80%。

Conclusion: 单次真实演示可同时充当场景几何、终止成功判据与物理参考轨迹，支撑无需真值评分器和可重置环境的真实世界机器人技能自改进、验证与跨机器人迁移。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents can iteratively self-improve robot manipulation skills in simulation benchmarks, yet real-world tasks provide neither ground-truth success scorers nor resettable practice environments. Our key observation is that a single real demonstration simultaneously specifies scene geometry, defines the terminal success criterion, and serves as a physical reference trajectory for verifying world fidelity---all decoupled from the source robot's kinematics. We present {RESET}: we reconstruct a contact-rich world and derive a frozen read-only criterion from one demonstration ({R}ebuild); check that world against the demonstration by open-loop replay and silhouette consistency ({E}xamine); evolve modular white-box task graphs and skill code from per-node failure evidence ({S}elf-improve); deploy frozen code on real robots with zero runtime LLM calls ({E}xecute); and share the verified world and criterion with unseen robots without new demonstrations ({T}ransfer). On 8 manipulation tasks, each rebuilt from one real demonstration, RESET achieves 97% average success on unseen layouts in simulation, whereas zero-shot code reaches 27%, per-task fine-tuned pi0.5 policies 31%, and Diffusion Policy 50%; injecting reconstruction defects shows that practice tolerates many reconstruction errors, that geometric errors can still stall or mislead it, and that the check flags most of them while physical randomization covers the rest. Without any target demonstrations, 3 heterogeneous robots acquire the same task skills in the shared world: a robot with similar arms copies 95% of its skill functions unchanged from the source robot, while robots with different arms and hands write their own. In real-robot experiments, the resulting programs run on 4 robots and succeed in 80% of 280 trials.

</details>


### [10] [CALVIN-MAX: When Does a Mid-Execution Change Hurt a Robot Policy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31868)
*Sajjad Pakdamansavoji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CALVIN-MAX基准（7,828个案例），分离“世界变化成本”与“变化时机效应”，发现十种策略在受影响指令上损失4.4–9.9个百分点，且成本会延续到后续指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作只表明任务中发生一次变化会降低所有策略的成功率，但未区分变化发生时机的影响与变化后世界本身的成本，也未测量变化后的持续代价。

Method: 在CALVIN五指令链上构建7,828个案例，每个案例对物体、相机、机器人或指令做一次变化；每例比较三次运行（无变化、从受影响指令开始即有变化、机器人运动中出现变化），通过差值分别估计变化世界成本与中途遭遇效应；在1,526个留出案例上预先固定假设评估十种已发布策略。

Result: 八类核心变化下，十种策略在受影响指令上损失4.4–9.9个百分点；改变机器人所见的变化在中途出现时比从一开始出现少损失5.4个百分点，而场景或机器人自身变化无可检测时机效应；将机器人回退到指令开始会消除该收益，预先固定的更严格检验（回退后运行与第二运行匹配）仅三种策略通过；变化成本会延续：仍完成该指令的策略完成下一条指令的概率低5.0–16.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 变化对机器人策略的成本既取决于变化类型也取决于发生时机，并且会跨指令持续；评估持续工作中的鲁棒性需同时考虑变化内容、时机与后续影响。

Abstract: Robots must often keep working after the world changes, as when a block moves or the camera shifts while the robot still acts on what it saw before. Recent work showed that one change during a task lowers success for every policy tested, but did not separate the effect of its timing from the cost of the changed world, or measure what it costs afterwards. We introduce CALVIN-MAX, a benchmark of 7,828 cases on CALVIN's five-instruction chains, each with one change to the objects, the camera, the robot, or the instruction. Each case compares three runs that are identical until the affected instruction begins: one without the change, one with it in place from that moment, and one in which it arrives while the robot is moving. The second run against the first gives the cost of the changed world, and the third against the second the effect of meeting it mid-motion. On 1,526 held-out cases per policy, with hypotheses fixed in advance, each of ten released policies loses 4.4 to 9.9 percentage points on the affected instruction over the eight core changes. Whether timing matters depends on the change: one that alters what the robot sees costs 5.4 points less mid-motion than from the start, whereas a change to the scene itself or to the robot shows no detectable effect. Rewinding the robot to the start of the instruction removes the benefit for every policy; the stricter test we fixed in advance, that the rewound run then matches the second run, passes for three. The cost also outlasts the affected instruction: the change stays in place, and a policy that still completes that instruction is 5.0 to 16.8 points less likely to complete the next one.

</details>


### [11] [Ada-BFM: Adapting Behavior Foundation Models for Humanoid Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31863)
*Furui Xu,Weishuai Zeng,Zirui Wang,Kangning Yin,Jiacheng Bao,Huayi Wang,Yingying Qin,Yufei Xue,Yang Tang,Zenghao Tang,Kailin Li,Jingbo Wang,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Ada-BFM后训练框架，通过轻量级适配器将预训练行为基础模型（BFM）适配到人形机器人场景交互任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BFM在大规模行为数据预训练后具备全身协调能力，但难以直接迁移到需要适应场景相关几何约束与接触动力学的场景交互任务。

Method: 在预训练BFM架构上增加轻量级适配器，实现交互相关调整并复用预训练控制能力；每个交互域训练一个共享模型而非为每个任务单独训练控制器。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，Ada-BFM支持多样化场景交互行为，相比从零训练加速学习，并基本保留预训练行为胜任力。

Conclusion: Ada-BFM是一种有效且高效的方法，可将BFM能力扩展到预训练设定之外，同时保留其在通用全身控制方面的优势。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose humanoid control through pretraining on large-scale behavioral data. However, their proficiency in whole-body coordination does not readily extend to scene interaction, which requires adapting motion to scene-dependent geometric constraints and contact dynamics. To bridge this gap, we introduce Ada-BFM, a post-training framework that adapts pretrained BFMs for humanoid scene interaction. Ada-BFM augments the original architecture with a lightweight adapter, enabling interaction-specific adjustments while leveraging pretrained control capabilities. Rather than producing a separate controller for each task, the framework yields a shared model for each interaction domain, supporting diverse tasks involving terrain or object interaction. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that Ada-BFM enables diverse scene interaction behaviors, accelerates learning compared with training from scratch, and largely preserves pretrained behavioral competence. These findings establish Ada-BFM as an effective and efficient approach to extending the capabilities of BFMs beyond their pretraining settings while retaining their strengths in general whole-body control.

</details>


### [12] [BendGS: Observation-Conditioned Gaussian Self-Modeling for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31844)
*Jeong Woon Lee,Doungjun Kim,Namho Kim,Seungheon Chae,Jongwoo Kim,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BendGS利用从观测中估计的虚拟骨干驱动高斯变形，实现无关节角测量的连续体机器人下一状态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续体机器人自模型无法同时从观测身体预测下一时刻的3D形状、外观和尖端位姿；且基于3D高斯的方法依赖关节角驱动变形。

Method: 从多视图图像前馈重建机器人3D高斯，将骨干（坐标帧链）拟合到反投影深度点估计弯曲状态；在过渡中，网络根据相对电机指令预测骨干变化以变形绑定高斯，渲染下一图像和3D形状，远端帧给出尖端位姿；仅用RGB-D图像和掩码训练，无需弯曲或尖端标签。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上，BendGS在对比方法中取得最低Chamfer距离、最佳图像质量和最低尖端误差。

Conclusion: BendGS无需关节角测量即可实现连续体机器人的下一状态预测，验证了虚拟骨干驱动高斯变形方法的有效性。

Abstract: Visual self-models learn from images how a robot moves under motor commands. Continuum robots need them even more, as they bend continuously without measured joint angles or fixed link geometry. Existing continuum self-models predict point clouds, image-space curves, or future images, but not the next 3D shape, appearance, and tip pose together from an observed body. 3D Gaussians offer a renderable representation of shape and appearance, yet articulated Gaussian self-models rely on joint angles to drive deformation. In this work, we propose BendGS, which uses a virtual backbone estimated from observation to drive command-conditioned Gaussian deformation, enabling next-state prediction for continuum robots without measured joint angles. In Gaussian modeling, a feed-forward network reconstructs the robot as 3D Gaussians from multi-view images, and the backbone, a chain of coordinate frames, is fitted to back-projected depth points to estimate the bending state. In transition, a network predicts its change under a relative motor command to deform the bound Gaussians. The deformed Gaussians render the next images and the 3D shape, and the distal frame gives the tip pose. Training losses use RGB-D images and masks, without bending or tip labels. On simulated and real robots, BendGS achieves the lowest Chamfer distance, best image quality, and lowest tip errors among the compared methods.

</details>


### [13] [Co-DRIS: Physics-Informed Predictive Belief and Variable-Impedance Learning for Reactive Catching under Coupled Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31843)
*Yonglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Co-DRIS+SuperMimic感知-动作协同设计框架，解决多源不确定性耦合下的反应式机器人操作鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧框架DRIS在孤立不确定性下鲁棒性强（86.4%），但感知与执行不确定性共存时性能骤降至27.6%，根源在于无记忆编码和固定高刚度PD控制。

Method: （i）惯性世界坐标系物理信息世界模型，结合精确弹道运动学与零初始化接触残差，滚动前推缺失观测，并通过零成本反事实dropout一致性加速训练；（ii）动作条件双流因果GRU信念编码器，解决去噪与滞后的矛盾；（iii）相位感知变阻抗软接策略，在力矩层将7-DoF Franka Research 3机械臂切换至过阻尼状态（zeta/zeta0约2.26倍）。

Result: 在未见投掷的3种子尾部评估（每场景15,360次投掷）下，标准基准达89.6±0.4%（4-sigma噪声下提升10.5%至70.5%），10个耦合/丢帧压力场景达66.6±0.5%（较DRIS提升39.0%；50%丢帧下提升30.4%）；验证了阻抗解耦、+0.65秒飞行时间偏移下的因果接触触发，以及跨任务迁移至钢板乒乓弹跳（87.5%至100.0%）和FR3+Hand抓取（4倍噪声下提升8.4%，峰值冲击力降低6-13%）。

Conclusion: 感知-动作协同设计可有效克服多源耦合不确定性，显著提升反应式操作鲁棒性并具备跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Reactive robotic manipulation is acutely sensitive to simultaneous sensing noise, measurement dropouts, joint execution errors, and contact parameter variations. While single-frame frameworks like Domain-Randomized Instance Set (DRIS) achieve strong robustness under isolated uncertainties (86.4%), they degrade severely when perception and actuation uncertainties co-occur (27.6% across 10 coupled stress scenarios) due to memoryless encoding and fixed high-stiffness PD control. We propose Co-DRIS + SuperMimic, a perception-action co-design framework coupling: (i) an inertial world-frame physics-informed world model combining exact ballistic kinematics with a zero-initialized contact residual to roll forward missing observations and accelerate training via zero-cost counterfactual dropout consistency; (ii) an action-conditioned dual-stream causal GRU belief encoder resolving the denoising-vs-lag deadlock; and (iii) phase-aware variable-impedance soft-catching that transitions the 7-DoF Franka Research 3 arm into an overdamped regime (zeta/zeta0 ~ 2.26x) at the torque layer. Under unbiased 3-seed tail evaluation on unseen throws (15,360 throws/scenario), our method achieves 89.6 +/- 0.4% on standard benchmarks (+10.5% on 4-sigma noise to 70.5%) and 66.6 +/- 0.5% across 10 coupled/dropout stress scenarios (+39.0% over DRIS; +30.4% on 50% dropout). We further validate impedance decoupling, causal contact triggering under +0.65 s flight-time shifts, and cross-task transfer to steel-plate ping-pong bouncing (87.5% to 100.0%) and FR3+Hand catching (+8.4% under 4x noise with 6-13% lower peak impact force).

</details>


### [14] [OmniAct: One Policy, One Million Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33158)
*Xiang Fan,Yuheng Wang,Jiayuan Yan,Bohan Fang,Wisdom Oluchi Ikezogwo,Ainaz Eftekhar,Max Argus,Jason Ren,Jiafei Duan,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniAct 是一个跨本体操作策略，以机器人运动学图为条件，通过流匹配预测相对关节运动，并利用百万级程序化生成的本体与仿真轨迹训练，在仿真和真实机器人上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一策略控制多种机器人时，不仅需要跨本体迁移任务知识，还需要让行为适配每个机器人的能力；现有共享动作表示未必能刻画机器人形态差异如何影响操作策略。

Method: 将每个机器人特定场景中的运动学表示为运动学图（关节位置、运动轴、与视觉观测的连接），以此直接条件化策略；用流匹配策略为每个节点预测相对关节运动，支持不同自由度与运动学配置；程序化设计并筛选一百万个体上可行的机器人本体，生成仿真轨迹，并在多样仿真操作任务上训练。

Result: 在 2,000 个本体-场景留出评估回合中成功率达 63.0%，对比 pi 0.5 的 17.8% 和最强基线的 57.9%；在 GPT-6 Astra 评估的任务族上为 64.7% 对 41.1%；在未见过的真实机器人上成功率接近次优方法 MolmoAct2 的两倍。

Conclusion: OmniAct 能学习利用不同形态能力并规避其局限的差异化操作策略，实现有效的跨本体操作控制。

Abstract: A single policy that controls diverse robots must not only transfer task knowledge across embodiments, but also adapt its behavior to each robot's capabilities. Existing approaches facilitate transfer through shared action representations, but these representations do not necessarily capture how differences in robot morphology should shape manipulation strategies. We introduce OmniAct, a cross-embodiment manipulation policy that conditions directly on each robot's kinematics in a specific scene to produce embodiment-specific behavior. This kinematic graph represents the robot's joint positions, motion axes, and connectivity to its visual observation, allowing the policy to account for how its embodiment related to objects and surfaces in the scene. A flow-matching policy predicts relative joint motions for each node, enabling control across robots with different degrees of freedom and kinematic configurations. To learn how physical structure shapes behavior at scale, we procedurally design and filter one million physically feasible robot embodiments; next, we develop algorithms to generate robot trajectories in simulation for all embodiments; finally, we train across diverse simulated manipulation tasks. On 2,000 embodiment-scene-held-out evaluation episodes, OmniAct achieves 63.0% success, compared to 17.8% for pi 0.5 and 57.9% for the strongest baseline; on the task families GPT-6 Astra was evaluated on, it achieves 64.7% against GPT-6 Astra's 41.1%. On a previously unseen real-world robot, it achieves nearly twice the success rate of the next best method, MolmoAct2. Beyond task success, we observe that OmniAct learns distinct manipulation strategies that exploit the capabilities and accommodate the limitations of different morphologies. Project: this https URL

</details>


### [15] [PyRUA-Dex: Learning Programmatic Policies for Dexterous Bimanual Manipulation with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33144)
*Feixiang Ruan,Guodong Lv,Kewei Zhang,Wenbo Huang,Yintianrun Zhang,Qiang Wang,Ruiyang Si,Shunyu Yang,Shulong Jiang,Weicheng Duan,Rui Ni,Jianxin Bi,Lekai Wang,Yuze Fan,Haobo Li,Zeliang Fu,Jiankun Zhang,Duomin Wang,Enze Xie,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PyRUA-Dex是一个无需演示数据的框架，利用编码智能体通过程序化策略开发灵巧操作能力，在仿真和真实环境中均取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习受限于高质量演示的稀缺：从人类视频重定向不精确、遥操作无触觉反馈、外骨骼采集难以扩展，因此需要一种无需演示的策略开发方法。

Method: 框架分两阶段：探索阶段，多个智能体并行探索任务技能及其组合方式，一个蒸馏智能体将成功尝试浓缩为任务库和单一程序；精炼阶段，通过随机化布局提升程序的鲁棒性和执行效率。

Result: 在仿真环境中，PyRUA-Dex在Bench2Dex上取得SOTA分数72.9，在完全随机化的DexJoCo上达到81.0%成功率，均领先所有训练策略；在双臂22自由度手平台上，高效学习弹钢琴、写书法等任务，从乐谱学习新曲目最快仅需30分钟。

Conclusion: PyRUA-Dex是缺乏高质量灵巧操作演示时，利用编码智能体开发灵巧操作策略的有效框架。

Abstract: Learning for dexterous manipulation is limited by the scarcity of high-quality demonstrations: retargeting from human video is imprecise, teleoperation gives no force feedback to the operator, and exoskeleton-based collection is hard to scale up. We present PyRUA-Dex, a framework that enables coding agents to develop dexterous manipulation through programmatic policy, without the need of demonstrations. The programmatic policy is developed in two stages: during exploration, multiple agents explore in parallel for task skills and ways to compose them, and a distilling agent condenses the successful attempts into a task library and one program. During refinement, this program is improved through randomized layouts for enhanced robustness and execution efficiency. In simulated environments, PyRUA-Dex achieves SOTA on Bench2Dex with a score of 72.9 and reaches 81.0% success on DexJoCo under full randomization, ahead of every trained policy on both benchmarks. In real world experiments on a bimanual platform with two 22-DoF hands, PyRUA-Dex learns to play piano, write calligraphy, and other tasks efficiently: learning a new piece from sheet music takes as little as 30 minutes. These results suggest that PyRUA-Dex is an effective framework for developing dexterous manipulation policies with coding agents in the absence of high quality dexterous manipulation demonstrations.

</details>


### [16] [ROAM-Bench: Evaluating Embodied Active Spatial Intelligence through Evidence Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33143)
*Xingjian Ran,Xiaoye Mo,Sihao Liu,Li Luo,Taiqiang Wu,Ji Zhao,Wenqi Shao,Hongyang Li,Jiaqi Wang,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ROAM-Bench基准，在机器人运动与碰撞约束下评估具身空间智能，区分正确结果与任务要求完成度，并诊断视觉语言模型的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法充分区分正确结果与任务要求完成情况，需要更严格评估具身智能体在探索交互中的空间推理与行动能力。

Method: 构建ROAM-Bench，包含5组11类共250个经人工验证的任务、27个室内环境；利用仿真状态和回合记录进行独立检查，对几何、覆盖或放置施加更强检查；在100个抽样任务上评估人类与7个前沿视觉语言模型，并进行oracle输入诊断。

Result: 人类评估者在100个抽样任务上达到84%严格任务成功率，且所有正确结果均满足必要检查；7个视觉语言模型表现出两种互补失败模式：结果正确但任务要求未满足，以及通过更强检查但结果错误；oracle输入诊断表明更完整的目标观察和校准反馈能改善最终空间判断。

Conclusion: ROAM-Bench能更细粒度地揭示具身空间智能的缺陷，强调结果与过程要求并重，并为改进视觉语言模型的空间推理提供诊断方向。

Abstract: Embodied spatial intelligence requires agents to gather information through exploration and interaction, then use it to reason and act. Existing benchmarks insufficiently distinguish correct outcomes from completion of these task requirements. We introduce ROAM-Bench to evaluate these abilities under robot motion and collision constraints. Independent checks use simulation state and episode records to assess outcomes and task requirements, with stronger checks of geometry, coverage, or placement. It comprises 250 manually validated tasks across 11 types in five groups and 27 indoor environments, covering object and room search, counting, geometric judgments, direction reasoning, and relocation. Human evaluators achieve 84% strict task success on 100 sampled tasks, with every correct outcome satisfying the required checks. Across seven frontier vision-language models, we identify two complementary failure modes: correct outcomes with unmet task requirements, and incorrect outcomes despite passing stronger checks. Oracle-input diagnostics show that more complete target observations and calibrated feedback improve final spatial judgments.

</details>


### [17] [iWAM: Efficient Action Prediction from World Models without Explicit Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33141)
*Vansh Kapoor,Max Sobol Mark,Sreyas Venkataraman,Max Simchowitz,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: iWAM通过单次视频模型前向传播提取中间特征来条件化动作策略，避免了完整未来帧生成，在保持泛化能力的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型依赖预训练视频生成模型预测场景演变来指导动作，但生成未来场景计算昂贵，难以满足实时控制需求；需要一种高效代理，且不能牺牲动作预测质量。

Method: 利用流匹配模型的可逆动力学，将每个训练未来反转为潜在噪声向量；学习双向流映射来摊销未来生成计算，使单次模型评估即可提供有效条件特征；推理时从先验采样潜在噪声向量，仅用一次视频骨干前向传播条件化策略。

Result: 在模拟基准和真实机器人上，iWAM恢复了世界动作模型的泛化优势，同时大幅降低推理成本。

Conclusion: 动作策略无需完整生成未来帧，仅需单次视频模型评估的中间特征即可有效区分可能未来，从而实现高效且泛化的世界动作建模。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) build policies on pretrained video-generation models, on the premise that predicting how a scene evolves helps determine how to act. However, generating future scenes is expensive for real-time control. This motivates efficient proxies for future imagination, but how to train such proxies without sacrificing action-prediction quality remains unclear. An effective proxy need not generate a high-resolution frame; it only needs to provide enough information to distinguish between possible futures. This leads to the design principle behind iWAM: rather than denoising an entire future frame, can we condition the action policy on intermediate features from a single video-model evaluation? We show that the answer is yes. Exploiting the reversible dynamics of flow-matching models, we invert each training future into the corresponding latent noise vector. However, a single velocity evaluation at this latent does not necessarily yield informative features for action prediction. We therefore learn a bidirectional flow map that amortizes future-generation computation, enabling a single model evaluation to provide effective conditioning features. At inference, we sample a latent noise vector from the prior and condition the policy using just one video-backbone forward pass. Across simulated benchmarks and real robots, iWAM recovers the generalization benefits of WAMs while substantially reducing its inference cost.

</details>


### [18] [Exploratory Midtraining for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33130)
*Kshitiz,Zheyuan Hu,Robyn Wu,Yizhou Li,Yunchu Zhang,Vattanary Tevy,Kavya Tayal,Shawn Yin,Aykut Onol,Kushal Arora,Dhruv Shah,Abhishek Gupta,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出β-Data数据集，通过对抗遥操作构建连接性行为覆盖，用于机器人策略的中期训练，提升下游适应与在线改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略主要从人类演示的熟练行为中学习，缺乏支撑自主改进所需的低层行为覆盖。

Method: 采用对抗遥操作收集数据：人类对抗者在不重置的情况下可逆地扰动环境，操作员持续完成任务；用自然语言局部结果重标注轨迹片段，并以近期动作历史为条件微调预训练策略。

Result: 在仿真操作任务和三个真实机器人平台上，相比标准演示训练，方法获得更优的数据缩放规律、更好的零样本泛化和更有效的额外执行时间利用；在线微调时需更少rollout即可收集成功经验并达到更高成功率。

Conclusion: 中期训练可同时为机器人准备更鲁棒的任务执行能力和持续自主改进能力。

Abstract: Robot policies predominantly learn from human-collected data emphasizing competent behavior, limiting coverage of the low-level behaviors needed to support autonomous improvement. We study how a form of midtraining can be used to build coverage over diverse low-level behaviors that connect challenging states of the robot and its environment to states from which further progress is possible ("connected behavioral coverage"). We also examine how this coverage supports downstream adaptation and online improvement. To this end, we introduce $\beta$-Data, a dataset collected via adversarial teleoperation, where a human adversary reversibly perturbs the environment while a human operator continues toward completion without resetting. We relabel trajectory segments with local outcomes in natural language and finetune a pretrained policy conditioned on recent action history. The resulting policy exhibits sustained use of more low-level behaviors. Across manipulation tasks in simulation and on three real-robot platforms, our approach yields more favorable data scaling laws, better zero-shot generalization, and more effective use of additional rollout time than training on standard demonstrations. During online finetuning, these policies need fewer rollouts to collect successful experience and reach higher success rates, suggesting that midtraining can prepare robots for both more robust task execution and continued autonomous improvement. Our project page and robot videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [19] [Neural Implicit Representation for Bathymetry Estimation using Sidescan Sonar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33118)
*Tianxiang Lin,Sacchin Sundar,Fenghao Liu,Onur Bagoren,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微神经渲染框架，用于从侧扫声呐（SSS）测量中有效估计海底地形（水深）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多波束测深仪（MBES）在深水区空间分辨率受限，而侧扫声呐（SSS）可搭载于自主水下航行器（AUV）靠近海底作业，实现高分辨率声学成像；但SSS测量存在固有模糊性和观测覆盖有限的问题，导致现有方法性能下降。

Method: 构建了基于SSS几何的测量模型，显式考虑其宽仰角孔径及相关模糊性，并集成到可微神经渲染框架中，实现从SSS测量估计海底地形。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上评估，与替代方法相比，所提方法在鲁棒性和准确性上均有提升。

Conclusion: 可微神经渲染框架结合显式SSS测量模型，能有效克服SSS测量模糊性和覆盖限制，提升水下地形估计性能。

Abstract: Bathymetry measurement and estimation is critical for understanding the structure of underwater environments. While multibeam echosounders (MBES) provide accurate depth measurements, their deployment on surface vessels limits spatial resolution in deep water. Sidescan sonar (SSS), in contrast, can be deployed on autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to operate closer to the seafloor, enabling high-resolution acoustic imaging. However, the inherent ambiguity in measurements and the limited observation coverage of sonar datasets degrade the performance of state-of-the-art methods for underwater bathymetry estimation from SSS. In this paper, we propose a differentiable neural rendering framework for SSS that enables effective estimation of bathymetry from SSS measurements. Our approach incorporates a measurement model based on SSS geometry, explicitly accounting for its wide elevation aperture and associated ambiguities. We evaluate our method on both synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating improved robustness and accuracy compared to alternative approaches.

</details>


### [20] [EmbodiSafety: Benchmarking Risk Understanding and Response in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33054)
*Erfei Cui,Shengjia Zhao,Yan Qin,Kaixuan Wang,Junming Chen,Ping Luo,Tianxing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EmbodiSafety基准，从短时快照与长时轨迹两个维度评估具身基础模型的安全决策，并构建4万条安全对齐数据提升小模型安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身基础模型评估偏重任务成功率，物理安全评估局限于规则遵循与二元判断，无法衡量模型是否理解环境危险并做出安全决策。

Method: 构建包含844个视觉场景、1092个快照任务和540个轨迹任务的基准，覆盖直接执行、拒绝、澄清、受限执行、自主恢复与人工升级六种响应模式，从模式正确性、风险识别和可操作处理三方面评估，并要求全部检查通过才算完整决策正确；同时开发数据构建流程并整理4万条安全对齐样本。

Result: Claude-Opus-5.5在参考模型中完整决策准确率最高，快照任务56.1%、轨迹任务61.5%；对Qwen3.5-2B和Qwen3.5-4B进行监督训练后完整决策准确率提升。

Conclusion: EmbodiSafety将安全自主边界作为显式评估目标，弥补具身模型物理安全评估空白，并证明所构建数据可用于提升模型安全决策能力。

Abstract: Progress in embodied foundation models is largely measured by task success, while the physical safety of their decisions remains underexamined. Safety evaluations centered on rule following and binary judgments do not adequately assess whether embodied models understand environmental hazards and make safe decisions. We introduce EmbodiSafety, comprising 1,092 snapshot tasks and 540 trajectory tasks across 844 visual scenarios to assess safety over short and long horizons. Six response modes cover direct execution, refusal, clarification, constrained execution, autonomous recovery, and human escalation. Distinguishing recovery from human intervention makes the boundary of safe autonomy an explicit evaluation target. Our protocol assesses mode correctness, risk identification, and actionable handling, with complete decision accuracy requiring all applicable checks to pass. Claude-Opus-5.5 achieves the highest complete decision accuracy among reference models, reaching 56.1% on snapshots and 61.5% on trajectories. We further develop a data construction pipeline and curate 40k examples for safety alignment. Supervised training on this corpus raises the complete decision accuracy of Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3.5-4B.

</details>


### [21] [At the Limit: Autonomous Vehicle Drifting under Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33040)
*Jie He,Meixun Qu,Cheng Hu,Lei Xie,Ezio Bartocci,Radu Grosu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合SDE采样与值函数优化的在线强化学习流策略方法，用于自主漂移控制，支持从零训练和专家演示预训练微调，在F1TENTH平台上实现实时漂移控制并提升跟踪精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主漂移面临轮胎力饱和与高度非线性车辆动力学的挑战，需要敏捷控制；现有方法在探索效率与策略优化方面存在不足。

Method: 提出在线强化学习流策略方法，将随机微分方程（SDE）采样用于探索，并与基于值的策略优化相结合；支持从零训练和基于专家演示预训练策略的微调；训练后可用SDE或常微分方程（ODE）生成控制动作。

Result: 在F1TENTH数字孪生平台的圆形和八字形漂移实验中，两种训练策略均实现有效的实时漂移控制，跟踪更紧、成功率更高，并在噪声条件下具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结合SDE采样与值函数优化的在线强化学习流策略方法能够有效实现自主漂移控制，且预训练微调与从零训练均可行，ODE推理可满足实时性需求。

Abstract: Autonomous drifting requires agile control despite tire-force saturation and highly nonlinear vehicle dynamics. We propose an online reinforcement learning method for flow policies that combines stochastic differential equation (SDE) sampling for exploration with value-based policy optimization. This method supports both training from scratch and fine-tuning a policy pretrained on expert demonstrations. After training, control actions can be generated using either SDEs or ordinary differential equations (ODEs). Experiments on circular and eight-shaped drifting in an F1TENTH digital twin platform show that both training strategies can achieve effective real-time drift control, with tighter tracking, higher success rates, and better robustness under noisy conditions.

</details>


### [22] [CAPER: PartGround and PlanRefine for Reliable Manual-Guided Robotic Assembly Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32986)
*Hanzhao Xi,Zhonghao Li,Suxuan Liu,Shanghan Zhou,Zesheng Chen,Qiufeng Wang,Yaran Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAPER是一个面向机器人装配的框架，通过PartGround和PlanRefine两个模块提升视觉语言模型在零件识别与装配规划中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在机器人任务规划中可从手册推导装配流程，但会误判零件身份、预测不存在的Part ID或在不同装配步骤中产生不一致的零件。

Method: 提出CAPER框架：PartGround将步骤级手册证据与产品库存及零件参考信息对齐，识别准确的Part ID与数量，并以共享的Assembly IR表示；PlanRefine依据有限库存审计PartGround的分配，移除置信度最低的分配，不确定的身份案例在Assembly IR中标记待审。

Result: 在IKEA Assembly in the Wild数据集上，CAPER达到83.33%的有限库存一致性率，未观察到超出库存的Part ID；PlanRefine将库存过度使用对从9降至0、多余单元从51降至0。

Conclusion: PartGround和PlanRefine提升了零件可靠性，同时改善了有限库存一致性和局部预测准确率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Models (VLMs) used in robot task planning to derive assembly procedures from existing manuals may correctly interpret individual assembly actions yet confuse exact part identities, predict unsupported Part IDs, or produce inconsistent parts across assembly steps. Thus, we propose CAPER (Capacity-Aware Part-grounding and Evidence Refinement), a manual-guided robotic assembly framework that enables VLMs to generate assembly procedures with two modules--PartGround and PlanRefine. PartGround aligns step-level manual evidence with product-specific inventory and part-reference information to identify exact Part IDs and quantities, transforming free-form hardware prediction into evidence-guided assignments represented in a shared Assembly Intermediate Representation (Assembly IR). PlanRefine audits PartGround assignments against finite inventory by removing the lowest-confidence assignments. Uncertain identity cases remain marked for review in the Assembly IR. We evaluate CAPER on the IKEA Assembly in the Wild (IAW) dataset. Experiment results show that CAPER reaches a Finite-inventory Consistency Rate of 83.33% and produces zero observed out-of-inventory Part-ID occurrences. At the plan level, PlanRefine removes all observed inventory-overuse pairs (9 -> 0) and excess units (51 -> 0). Overall, the results indicate that PartGround and PlanRefine improve part reliability, while also enhancing finite-inventory consistency and local prediction accuracy. Project page and video: this https URL

</details>


### [23] [DynaMimic: A Physics-Informed World Model for Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32944)
*Chaoyue Xing,Linyi Huang,Yiting He,Yixuan Yin,Siqi Hou,Yi Zhang,Liyuan Pan,Miaomiao Liu,Daqi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaMimic 通过显式嵌入共享物理的可微世界模型提升人形机器人全身运动跟踪的精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无模型强化学习方法在将运动专家蒸馏为单一策略时损失性能，而直接训练通用策略需大规模算力；SuperMimic 虽用可微世界模型降低计算成本，但其隐式物理建模在支撑切换处误差会随想象 rollout 累积。

Method: 提出 DynaMimic，一种在状态预测中显式融入共享物理的可微世界模型：网络为每个候选支撑连杆预测接触力旋量与残余广义力，再通过闭式拉格朗日前向动力学计算下一状态。

Result: 在 60 个完整 LAFAN1 运动上，MPJPE 相对 SuperMimic 降低 18.6%；地形跟踪中，相对地形条件化 SuperMimic，A3 Parkour 上 MPJPE 从 66.15 mm 降至 56.28 mm（降低 14.9%），GRAIL 地形片段从 141.88 mm 降至 126.57 mm（降低 10.8%）；在真实 A3 人形机器人上学到的策略可完成倒地恢复、侧手翻和箱体翻越等技能。

Conclusion: 显式物理建模的可微世界模型能有效减少支撑切换误差累积，在保持计算效率的同时提升运动跟踪精度，并支持真实人形机器人多样化技能。

Abstract: Whole-body motion tracking enables humanoid robots to reproduce diverse skills from human demonstrations. Existing model-free reinforcement learning methods either distill motion specialists into a single policy at the cost of performance or train a generalist policy directly using cluster-scale computation. SuperMimic reduces this computational cost through a differentiable world model that implicitly learns the physics shared across motions by predicting the next state from the current state and action. However, this implicit formulation may not fully capture the underlying physical processes. Errors around changes of support can accumulate over imagined rollouts used to train the tracking policy. To address this challenge, we introduce DynaMimic, a differentiable world model that explicitly incorporates shared physics into state prediction. Specifically, its network predicts a contact wrench at each candidate support link and a residual generalized force. DynaMimic then uses these predictions to compute the next state through closed-form Lagrangian forward dynamics. Across 60 full LAFAN1 motions, DynaMimic reduces mean per-joint position error (MPJPE) by 18.6% relative to SuperMimic. For terrain tracking, we give both methods the same local height maps. Relative to terrain-conditioned SuperMimic, DynaMimic reduces MPJPE by 14.9% on A3 Parkour (66.15 to 56.28~mm) and by 10.8% on GRAIL terrain clips (141.88 to 126.57~mm). On a real A3 humanoid, the learned policies can perform diverse skills, such as floor recovery, cartwheel and a box vault.

</details>


### [24] [Self-Sensing Compliant Anthropomorphic Hand for Speed-Dictated Shared Force Control in Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32937)
*Huijiang Wang,Yu Zhang,Rui Chen,Tristan Bonato,Jaemin Eom,Kai Junge,Cheng Pan,Benhui Dai,Yue Xie,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 柔性手肌腱中的串联弹簧配合电位计可实现无外部传感器的关节状态与指尖力自感知，并据此构建接触感知共享控制框架用于VR遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉遥操作缺乏力反馈，操作者难以调节抓取力；加装触觉传感器又增加布线、成本和集成复杂度，因此需要一种无需外部传感器的力感知方案。

Method: 利用腱驱动拟人手肌腱路径中的串联弹簧，由前臂电机箱内电位计读取弹簧形变，估计关节状态与手指力；在此基础上设计VR遥操作共享控制框架：接触前按操作者手部运动学映射控制，接触后切换为指尖力控制，设定值由操作者抓取速度决定，并可将控制权分配给单指。

Result: 实验验证了该系统的力估计能力，并在抓取易碎和重物时相比直接遥操作提高了抓取成功率。

Conclusion: 柔顺性不仅可用于被动适应，还可作为物理人机协作中的内在感知资源。

Abstract: In vision-based teleoperation of robotic hands, the operator receives no haptic feedback and therefore struggles to regulate the grasp force, while adding tactile sensors to the fingers increases wiring, cost and integration complexity. Compliant hands offer an alternative, as their mechanical compliance, usually treated only as a passive property for safe and adaptive grasping, can also serve as a source of information. Here, we show that the elastic transmission of a compliant, tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand can itself act as the sensor for shared-control telemanipulation. Series springs in the tendon routing are read by potentiometers in a forearm-mounted motor box, from which the hand estimates its joint states and finger forces without any external sensors. Building on this self-sensing, we develop a contact-aware shared-control framework for VR-based teleoperation. The robot hand is controlled through a mapping from the operator's hand kinematics until a contact is detected proprioceptively. Control then switches to a finger-level force controller whose set point is determined by the operator's grasping speed, and control authority can be allocated to individual fingers. Experiments validate the system's force estimation and increased grasp success rate compared with direct teleoperation for fragile and heavy objects. These results show that compliance can serve not only for passive adaptation but also as an intrinsic sensing resource for physical human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [25] [What Does a Skill Vocabulary Cost? A Closed-Loop Decomposition over Demonstrated Motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32858)
*Maximilian Xiling Li,Nils Blank,Paul Mattes,Pankhuri Vanjani,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MPQ将演示动作库置于训练好的策略之后而非内部，用最近库元素替换预测动作块并在界内校正，无需训练参数，可实现动作量化与失败归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散技能策略将技能词表与选择器、解码器联合学习，闭环失败时无法区分是词表、技能选择还是执行的问题。

Method: 提出Movement Prototype Quantization（MPQ）：在训练好的策略后放置演示动作库，将每个预测动作块替换为最近库元素，并在限定范围内向预测校正，不训练任何参数；在六种架构（含7B VLA模型）上验证等价性，并通过逐组件替换选择器组件进行归因分析。

Result: 包裹后策略与原策略统计等价；在LIBERO-90上，量化无损失，技能选择最多损失2分，选择器自身的放置与校正贡献剩余差距的近全部，最多达12分；离线重建无法预测这些差异，并误判库大小、设计与跨基准迁移。

Conclusion: MPQ提供了同库上任意选择器的参考基线，使失败可归因于词表、选择或执行，且离线重建指标不可靠。

Abstract: Discrete skill policies execute motions drawn from a finite vocabulary. In most current methods, this vocabulary is a latent codebook learned jointly with a selector and a decoder. When such a policy fails in closed loop, the failure cannot be traced to the vocabulary, to the choice of skill or to its execution. We present Movement Prototype Quantization (MPQ), which places a library of demonstrated motions after a trained policy instead of inside it. MPQ replaces each predicted action chunk with its nearest library element, corrects that element toward the prediction within a bound, and trains no parameters. Across six architectures, including a 7B vision-language-action model, the wrapped policy is statistically equivalent to the original. A reference policy executed through the library is therefore a baseline for any selector over the same library. Substituting a selector's components one at a time attributes its shortfall to them. On LIBERO-90, with selectors on three backbones, quantization costs nothing and the choice of skill costs at most 2 points under the reference's placement and correction. The selectors' own placement and correction account for nearly all of the remaining gap, up to 12 points. Offline reconstruction predicts none of these differences and misjudges library size, design and transfer across benchmark suites. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [26] [Atomic Policy: Composing Atomic Manipulation Skills with Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32813)
*Kaidi Zhang,Pengyuan Guo,Zhengtong Xu,Caiyi Zhang,Huayi Gou,Guanlin Huang,Aaron Lim,Anas Rafei,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Atomic Policy框架，将VLM、PDDL规划与视觉运动策略结合，通过语言/视觉指向引导与执行反馈实现长时序操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序操作中VLM具备语义空间推理但缺乏细粒度控制，视觉运动策略控制精细但对目标与动作依赖理解不足，二者集成面临目标模糊与动作依赖难题。

Method: VLM估计符号状态与目标，PDDL求解器生成技能计划；agentic harness通过语言、视觉指向或两者引导每个技能，并验证结果以驱动阶段转换与重规划；统一的阶段级接口支持专用策略与通用动作模型。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人实验中，Atomic Policy提升了任务遵循能力，并支持复杂长时序操作中的恢复。

Conclusion: 原子策略式组合学习技能、结合符号规划与执行反馈，可有效改进长时序操作的任务遵循与失败恢复。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation requires robots to translate task intent into physical interactions as the scene changes. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer semantic and spatial reasoning, while visuomotor policies provide fine-grained control. Yet ambiguous goals and dependencies between actions make their integration challenging. We introduce Atomic Policy, a framework that composes learned manipulation skills through grounded steering and execution feedback. A VLM estimates the symbolic state and goal, and a PDDL solver constructs a skill plan. An agentic harness steers each skill through language, visual pointing, or both, and verifies its outcome to guide phase transitions and replanning. A common phase-level interface supports specialized policies and generalist action models. Experiments in simulation and on a real robot show how Atomic Policy improves task following and supports recovery in complex, long-horizon manipulation. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [27] [MRBench: Benchmarking Physical Necessity Reasoning in Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32803)
*Yanzhe Chen,Zhijun Cao,Ruihe An,Chang Gong,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出多机器人操作中的物理必要性推理问题，并构建MRBench基准与MRScissors方法，以识别完成任务所需的最小可行机器人团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人协调方法虽能完成任务，但无法判断哪些机器人真正必要；当机器人能力变化、资源不可用或被动物体可替代机器人支撑时，所需团队会不同，因此需要物理必要性推理。

Method: 构建MRBench基准，包含八个具有不同并发与可达约束的任务，每个任务在五种配置下评估（改变所需资源但保持任务目标）；并提出MRScissors，将任务需求与实测物理能力分离，通过约束搜索识别最小可行团队。

Result: 在两个模型骨干上，MRScissors的团队选择准确率分别达到85.4%和91.2%，比最强基线分别高出10.4和9.5个百分点；现有协调智能体在参考团队下表现良好，但在机器人形态变化或被动物体替代机器人支撑时表现不佳。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了协调机器人与理解其物理必要性之间的关键差距，表明可靠的多机器人操作需要物理必要性推理。

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-robot manipulation have shown how robots can collaborate to accomplish complex tasks. However, successful coordination does not reveal which robots are actually needed. The same task may require different teams as robot capabilities change, resources become unavailable, or objects replace robotic support. We argue that reliable multi-robot manipulation requires physical necessity reasoning: understanding what a task demands, which resources can fulfill those demands, and which are truly necessary. We introduce MRBench, a benchmark for physical necessity reasoning in heterogeneous multi-robot manipulation. It comprises eight tasks with distinct concurrency and reach constraints, each evaluated under five configurations that change the required resources while preserving the task goal. Our evaluation reveals that existing coordination agents perform well with reference teams but struggle when robot embodiments change or passive objects can replace robotic support. To address this gap, we propose MRScissors, which separates task requirements from measured physical capabilities and identifies minimal feasible teams through constrained search. Across two model backbones, MRScissors achieves 85.4% and 91.2% team-selection accuracy, outperforming the strongest baselines by 10.4 and 9.5 points, respectively. Together, these findings expose a critical gap between coordinating robots and understanding their physical necessity.

</details>


### [28] [Supply Geometry, Learn Execution: Screw Attention for Zero-Shot Policy Transfer in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32797)
*Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Screw Attention Policy，一种以几何为中心的操控框架，显式提供任务几何并仅学习执行，仅需30K可学习参数，在仿真中训练即可泛化到新布局、零样本迁移到不同机械臂并直接部署真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模操控策略学习到的许多内容其实是几何结构，而这些结构可以被显式提供而非从数据中学习。

Method: 策略基于感知和机器人模型提供的物体位姿、相对SE(3)变换和关节螺旋轴运行，仅需30K可学习参数，在单机械臂仿真中训练。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到96%成功率，布局变化下85.8%，跨五种未见机械臂80.2%，真实机器人24次试验成功21次。

Conclusion: 结果表明，操控策略的大部分几何结构可以显式提供，从而以极少参数实现强泛化和零样本迁移。

Abstract: We present the Screw Attention Policy, a geometry-centric manipulation framework that supplies task geometry explicitly and learns only execution. The policy operates on object poses, relative SE(3) transforms, and joint screw axes provided by perception and robot models. The policy requires only 30K learned parameters. Trained entirely in simulation on a single robot arm, the policy generalises to new scene layouts, transfers zero-shot across robot embodiments, and deploys directly on a real robot, without adaptation or fine-tuning. It achieves 96% success on LIBERO, 85.8% under layout changes, 80.2% across five unseen robot arms, and 21 of 24 trials on a real robot. These results suggest that much of what manipulation policies learn at scale is geometric structure that can instead be supplied.

</details>


### [29] [Keypose or Dense? A Controlled Comparison of Action Representations for Stage-Structured Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32788)
*Haizhou Ge,Mengshuo Wang,Zhixing Chen,Lei Han,Chen Wang,Lu Shi,Guyue Zhou,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 关键姿态动作表示与密集动作表示在模仿学习中的对比：关键姿态对演示变化更鲁棒，但对执行器与演示的匹配更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿策略多采用密集动作表示，而阶段式操作任务中关键姿态表示可能更合适，需系统比较两者优劣。

Method: 在多个仿真操作任务和真实机器人上，以相同块长度、匹配的演示、观测、策略架构和更新预算，比较密集与关键姿态表示；仿真中通过调优场景选择各自执行规则，并测试演示放缓、暂停、改道及物体尺寸位置偏移等扰动。

Result: 仿真中演示受扰动时密集成功率骤降而关键姿态基本持平，跨三种策略架构成立；干净演示下关键姿态仅在演示稀缺时领先，否则接近或落后；真实机器人开抽屉任务中密集成功率更高，关键姿态仅在调整执行器旋转和前进速度匹配演示后大幅提升。

Conclusion: 关键姿态以对演示变化的敏感性换取对执行偏离演示的敏感性。

Abstract: Most imitation policies that predict chunks of future actions use the dense action representation, in which the chunk holds one control target per step. In stage-structured manipulation, where a task can be divided into stages with definable endpoints, the keypose action representation instead sets each step of the chunk to the endpoint of the stage that step belongs to, and a speed-capped servo supplies the motion. We compare the two in several simulated manipulation tasks and on a real robot, with the same chunk length and matched demonstrations, observations, policy architectures and update budgets; in simulation, each one's execution rule is selected on tuning scenes. When the simulated demonstrations are slowed, paused and rerouted, dense success falls sharply while keypose stays roughly level, across three policy architectures and with endpoints extracted from the recorded demonstrations; keypose also leads under object-size and position shift. With clean demonstrations, keypose leads only when they are scarce and is otherwise close to or behind dense across the simulated tasks, provided that the demonstrations and execution rule suit its executor; when they do not, it falls far below dense. The same dependence on the executor appeared on a real robot learning to open a drawer from human demonstrations: dense succeeded more often, and keypose improved substantially only after its executor's rotation and forward speed were adjusted to the demonstrations. These results suggest that keypose trades sensitivity to such variation in the demonstrations for sensitivity to execution that departs from them.

</details>


### [30] [Simulator-as-Proxy World Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32786)
*Tingting Liao,Mingjia Huo,Hao Li,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出SimPWM，一种以模拟器为代理的世界模型，在仅给定校准相机和首帧时，用3D机器人模型与可接触物体的3D点表示场景，由无训练参数的规则模拟器在动作下移动它们，再由视频模型渲染，从而可冻结模拟物体以检验视频是否真正遵循模拟状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视频世界模型通常以模拟或渲染的场景代理为条件，并假设视频会显示代理所示内容；验证该假设需要一个状态显式且可扰动的代理。

Method: SimPWM从首帧构建机器人的3D模型和可抓取物体的3D点，使用无训练参数的规则模拟器根据动作移动它们，再由基础视频模型渲染，返回真实感视频及模拟状态；通过冻结模拟物体而按记录移动机械臂，检验视频是否仍显示物体被抬起。

Result: 在未见设置的DROID片段上，三种基础视频模型的机械臂均跟随代理（机器人IoU 0.75-0.78，端到端模型至多0.69）；模拟物体将漏检抓取从54%降至38%；冻结刚性物体使MiniMax-H3抬升率从42%降至10%，而LTX-2.3仅从57%降至49%；MiniMax-H3剩余抬升中超半数为被模拟器刚性处理的布料和绳索。

Conclusion: 模拟器的显式状态能记录模拟行为；在扰动动作下，与模拟状态不一致的视频表明该视频忽略了模拟状态，且不同基础模型对模拟物体状态的遵循程度存在差异。

Abstract: Video world models for robots are increasingly conditioned on a simulated or rendered proxy of the scene, on the assumption that the video will show what the proxy shows. Testing this assumption requires a proxy whose state is explicit and can be perturbed. We present SimPWM (Simulator-as-Proxy World Model): given a calibrated camera, it represents, from the first frame alone, the robot by its 3D model and the objects the gripper may touch as 3D points; a rule-based simulator with no trained parameters moves them under the actions, and a video model renders the result. SimPWM returns the realistic video together with the simulated state. We then freeze the simulated objects while the arm moves as recorded and check whether the video still shows the object lifted. On DROID episodes from unseen setups, the answer differs for the arm and for the objects. With each of three base video models, the arm follows the proxy (robot IoU 0.75-0.78, against at most 0.69 for end-to-end models); rendering the robot alone already gives this. What the simulated objects add is the object state: against a robot-only proxy, they cut missed pick-ups from 54% to 38%, and the video follows them only with some base models: on rigid objects, freezing them lowers the lift rate of MiniMax-H3, fine-tuned on episodes whose simulation agrees with the real video, from 42% to 10%, but that of LTX-2.3, trained on the same episodes, only from 57% to 49%, as if it inferred the grasp from the arm motion; more than half of the remaining lifts of MiniMax-H3 are of cloth and rope, which the simulator carries rigidly. With any base model, the simulated state records what the simulation did; under perturbed actions, a video that disagrees with it reveals that the video ignores the simulated state.

</details>


### [31] [Risk-Informed Path Planning: Collision and Grounding Avoidance Application for Autonomous Surface Vessels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32760)
*Emir Cem Gezer,Ozan Kaya,Ingrid B. Utne,Roger Skjetne*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合贝叶斯信念网络（BBN）在线风险模型的RRT*路径规划器，用于海上自主水面船舶的在线风险评估与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MASS需在运营前和运营中证明安全性，因此需要在线风险评估来指导海上决策。

Method: 将BBN作为在线风险模型，评估危险事件的概率与严重度分布并聚合为有界标量风险指数；在RRT*搜索中沿候选边采样点评估风险，优化由路径距离、航向变化惩罚和累积风险组成的复合代价函数，并施加风险接受限值的硬约束；每个搜索树顶点存储BBN后验，使树成为可解释的风险地图；同时定义BBN设计需求并引入运行时模型，将重规划截止时间转化为单次风险计算时间的严格限制。

Result: 在包含碰撞和搁浅危险的模拟港口进入场景中演示了规划器，实测运行时间验证其在运行时模型预测的单次重规划周期计算边界内运行。

Conclusion: 该风险知情RRT*规划器能够在线整合BBN风险评估，在计算约束内实现安全路径规划，并具备可解释性。

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) must demonstrate safety before and during operations, necessitating online risk assessment to guide decision-making at sea. This paper presents a risk-informed Rapidly Exploring Random Tree Star (RRT*) path planner that integrates a Bayesian Belief Network (BBN) as an online risk model. The BBN evaluates the probability and severity distribution of hazardous events, which are aggregated into a bounded scalar risk index. During the search, this risk index is evaluated at sample points along candidate edges. The planner optimizes a composite cost function comprising path distance, heading change penalties, and accumulated risk, subject to a hard constraint enforcing a risk acceptance limit. Each search tree vertex stores the BBN posterior at its state, enabling the tree to function as an explainable risk map for individual hazards. We define design requirements for the BBN to serve as the risk model in this context and introduce a runtime model that translates replanning deadlines into strict limits on single risk calculation times. The planner is demonstrated in a simulated harbor entry scenario involving collision and grounding hazards. Measured run times validate that the planner operates within the computational bounds predicted by the runtime model for a single replanning cycle.

</details>


### [32] [Support2Sim: Visually Faithful Real2Sim with Stable Object Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32727)
*Samarth Chopra,Ankit Prabhu,Akshayan Nagarajah,Sumer Murthy,Pratik Chaudhari,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Support2Sim从单张RGB图像重建可物理仿真场景，通过支撑图、接触引导位姿初始化和粗到精物理仿真优化，在保真度、支撑实现与稳定性上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像重建场景用于物理仿真时，需恢复空间布局和支撑关系，并在实际时间预算内最大化源保真度、支撑实现和稳定性。

Method: 推断支撑图；接触引导位姿初始器为多支撑物体生成候选并按几何拟合与源图像一致性排序；粗到精精化器在受支撑物体间分配固定搜索预算，用物理rollout评估位姿更新，接受后以更细平移与旋转尺度从新位姿继续搜索。

Result: 在四个数据集共50个场景上，相比最强基线，10秒后源掩码IoU提升21.4个百分点，意图支撑实现率87.1%对73.2%，平均物体漂移从79.6 mm降至45.3 mm。

Conclusion: Support2Sim能高效地从单图重建仿真就绪场景，在保真度、支撑实现和稳定性方面均显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Reconstructing a scene for physics simulation requires recovering its spatial layout and the support relationships that govern its behavior. We present Support2Sim, a Real2Sim system that reconstructs simulation-ready scenes from a single RGB image. Our design objective is to maximize source fidelity, support realization, and stability within a practical time budget for scene generation. Support2Sim infers a support graph, which a contact-guided pose initializer uses to propose poses for objects with multiple supports. The initializer generates and ranks candidates by geometric fit and source-image agreement. A coarse-to-fine refiner allocates a fixed search budget across supported objects and evaluates pose updates with physics rollouts. After an accepted update, it searches from the new pose at a finer translation and rotation scale. We benchmark our method on 50 scenes spanning four datasets. Compared with the strongest baseline, Support2Sim improves source-mask IoU after 10 seconds by 21.4 pp, realizes 87.1% versus 73.2% of intended supports, and reduces mean object drift from 79.6 to 45.3 mm. See our project page at this https URL for more details.

</details>


### [33] [AG-WAM: Attention-Guided World Action Model for Accurate and Efficient Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32722)
*Hongcheng Zhang,Lei Sun,Songxuan Liu,Tengteng Pan,Yuqiang Zhai,Xianglong Xu,Jianya Guo,Xiao Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AG-WAM，通过三个即插即用的显式注意力引导模块改进世界动作模型的内部注意力范式，在多个机器人操作基准上取得最优性能并提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）在隐式学习注意力分配时缺乏显式的动作相关注意力引导，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出统一原则——显式注意力引导，构建端到端框架AG-WAM，包含三个即插即用模块：内容自适应预测注意力模块、引导点视觉注意力模块和状态条件文本注意力模块，直接重构WAMs内部的注意力范式。

Result: 在LIBERO-X、LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin-2.0等公开机器人操作基准上达到新的最优性能，显著优于其他方法（包括使用大量私有具身训练数据的方法），同时明显提升训练和推理效率。

Conclusion: 显式注意力引导是释放WAMs强大能力的有效统一原则，AG-WAM无需外部插件模块和私有训练数据即可实现性能与效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Recently developed World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and robot actions, providing predictive context for control. However, existing WAMs typically learn implicit attention allocation with limited explicit action-relevant attention guidance, leading to limited performance. To unleash strong capabilities of WAMs, we introduce explicit attention guidance as a unifying principle and propose our Attention-Guided WAM (AG-WAM), an end-to-end framework with three plug-and-play attention guidance modules that directly reformulate internal attention paradigm in WAMs: the Content-Adaptive Predictive Attention module, the Guidance-Point Visual Attention module, and the State-Conditioned Text Attention module. Without any external plugin module adopted, our end-to-end framework with the unifying principle of explicit attention guidance boosts the accuracy and efficiency of WAMs. Without using any private embodied training data, our AG-WAM establishes new state-of-the-art performance on multiple public robotic manipulation benchmarks including LIBERO-X, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin-2.0, significantly outperforming other methods, even those using massive private embodied training data by a notable margin, meanwhile notably improving training and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [34] [Seeing to Drive on a Single Board: Attention-Free Depth for Onboard Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32718)
*Itai Mizlish,Boaz Ben-Moshe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文在树莓派5与Hailo-8L整数加速器上部署深度基础模型，发现量化会因注意力softmax权重低于int8分辨率而严重损失，遂改用知识蒸馏训练无注意力ResNet-34 U-Net学生模型，实现27.1 FPS实时深度估计并支撑完整导航栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型地面机器人仅能携带单相机和数瓦算力，而可作距离传感器的深度基础模型多为面向GPU的ViT，难以在边缘设备上运行；直接int8量化又导致深度图大面积缺失，需探索替代部署方案。

Method: 分析Depth Anything V2量化失败原因：449个token时91%的softmax权重低于一个int8步长，却承载17–33%注意力质量。提出蒸馏而非量化：以Depth Anything V2-Large为教师，训练24.5M参数的无注意力ResNet-34 U-Net学生模型并在Hailo-8L上以int8运行；利用障碍物与地面交线及相机高度测距，在Pi CPU上运行建图、hybrid-A*规划与SLAM，并在高斯泼溅重建场景中做硬件在环测试。

Result: 学生模型在芯片上达27.1 FPS，int8输出与浮点孪生高度一致（Pearson 0.99993）；在仿真场地留出场景上AbsRel 0.269，优于其他片上网络（0.354–0.530）；在真实户外驾驶1000个立体校验点上与厂商Depth Anything V2-Small无显著差异且更快（27.1 vs 21.6 FPS）。硬件在环测试中，10个场景7.9±1.1无接触到达目标（精确障碍物为9.2±0.4），自身位姿加模拟轮式里程计为7.7±0.7（真实Pi与Hailo-8L在环、五个种子为8.2±0.4）。

Conclusion: 对边缘加速器应优先蒸馏而非直接量化深度基础模型；无注意力学生网络可在低功耗整数硬件上实现实时、准确的单目深度估计，并支撑完整的感知—规划—导航栈。

Abstract: A small ground robot can carry one camera and a few watts of compute, but the depth foundation models that could turn that camera into a range sensor are vision transformers sized for GPUs. We bring one to a Raspberry Pi 5 with the Hailo-8L, an accelerator that runs every layer in integer arithmetic, attention included. Quantised for it, Depth Anything V2 loses large parts of the scene, and we measure why: at 449 tokens, 91% of softmax weights fall below one int8 step while carrying 17-33% of the attention mass. Our answer is to distil, not quantise. An attention-free ResNet-34 U-Net student of Depth Anything V2-Large (24.5M parameters) runs at 27.1 FPS on the chip, its int8 output matches its float twin (Pearson 0.99993), and on held-out scenes of its simulated yard it is the most accurate depth network on the chip (AbsRel 0.269 against 0.354-0.530). On 1,000 stereo-verified points of a real outdoor drive it is not resolvably different from the vendor's Depth Anything V2-Small build, while running faster (27.1 vs 21.6 FPS). Ranging obstacles by where they meet the floor needs only the camera height, so mapping, hybrid-A* planning and SLAM run on the Pi's own CPU. In real-time hardware-in-the-loop tests in a Gaussian-splat reconstruction of a real yard, the stack reaches the goal without contact in 7.9$\pm$1.1 of 10 scenes, against 9.2$\pm$0.4 with exact obstacles, and in 7.7$\pm$0.7 on its own pose with simulated wheel odometry (ten seeds each; 8.2$\pm$0.4 with the real Pi and Hailo-8L in the loop, five seeds). The source code, pre-trained models and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [35] [Semidefinite Programming for Cold-Start GNSS Doppler-Based Alignment: A Comparison with Nonlinear Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32715)
*Baoshan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在统一的极大似然多普勒目标下系统比较NLS与SDP，并提出将模块化对偶SDP证书器附加到流形NLS求解器上，以低延迟获得全局精度并保留原生协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器融合导航需要局部导航坐标系与ECEF坐标系的初始姿态对齐；GNSS多普勒对齐虽无需载波相位模糊度固定或多天线硬件，但该非凸问题用局部NLS易陷局部极小，直接SDP松弛则延迟高且不输出局部协方差。

Method: 在统一的极大似然多普勒目标函数下，通过蒙特卡洛仿真和三个真实道路驾驶数据集系统比较NLS与SDP；并将模块化对偶SDP证书器附加到标准流形NLS求解器，利用拉格朗日对偶验证固定NLS候选解是否达到全局下界。

Result: 该方案在0.001度以内恢复直接SDP的全局精度，将真实场景95百分位偏航误差从超过86度降至2.88至4.70度，中位求解时间最多缩短一个数量级，同时保留原生雅可比协方差。

Conclusion: 候选驱动的证书机制桥接了局部估计与全局优化：SDP松弛扩大可行域并保持提升后的代价，使对偶可验证全局下界而无需完整SDP求解；且该架构作为非侵入模块可直接接入现有导航流程，保留切空间协方差输出和低典型延迟。

Abstract: Initial attitude alignment between local navigation frames and the Earth-centered Earth-fixed (ECEF) frame is a critical prerequisite for multi-sensor integrated navigation, such as Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)/inertial/odometry fusion, before global and local measurements can be jointly processed. While GNSS Doppler-based alignment eliminates the need for carrier-phase ambiguity resolution or multi-antenna hardware, solving this non-convex problem via local nonlinear least squares (NLS) suffers from local minima, whereas direct semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation incurs high latency and omits local covariance outputs. This study systematically compares NLS and SDP on a unified maximum-likelihood Doppler objective across Monte Carlo simulations and three real-world driving datasets. We demonstrate that attaching a modular dual SDP certifier to a standard manifold NLS solver recovers the global accuracy of direct SDP within 0.001 degrees, reduces real-world 95th-percentile yaw errors from over 86 degrees down to 2.88 degrees to 4.70 degrees, and cuts median solve times by up to an order of magnitude while preserving native Jacobian covariances. We conclude that candidate-driven certification bridges local estimation and global optimization due to two structural advantages: (1) our theoretical comparison reveals that SDP relaxation enlarges the feasible region while preserving the lifted cost, allowing Lagrangian duality to verify whether a fixed NLS candidate attains the global lower bound without invoking a full SDP solver, and (2) the candidate-driven architecture acts as a non-intrusive module that plugs directly into existing navigation pipelines without replacing the local estimator, preserving native tangent-space covariance outputs and low typical latency.

</details>


### [36] [DeCoNav-Act: Benchmarking Collective Physical Capability in Multi-Robot Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32683)
*Yunzi Wu,Sunyao Zhou,Xiaotao Zhang,Yucheng Deng,Yuehao Huang,Lin Qian,Xuanrui Cui,Xinhai Li,Chenjia Bai,Hongqing Chu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeCoNav-Act基准，用于评估语言引导的多机器人移动操作中的集体物理能力，发现现有方法在物理依赖与交接任务上表现极差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队需要共享世界中可用的物理状态，但现有基准侧重任务分配与技能排序，很少测试生成的物理状态是否可用。

Method: 构建含500个任务（350同构、150异构）的共享物理仿真基准，任务经联合参考执行验证，覆盖并行工作负载与物理依赖两种协调机制，并测量路径效率、交接覆盖率与成功率，以及基于验证的前交接状态的诊断指标；评估ASPIRE、RAX的传感器适配版本及自研CoMoMa。

Result: 使用GPT-5.6-luna时无方法完成任务，依赖达成率最高8.8%，即使从前交接状态出发也无交接完成；使用GPT-6.1-sol时CoMoMa在26.5%试验中建立条件，但仅4.1%成功。

Conclusion: 现有方法在多机器人物理依赖与交接场景中能力严重不足，DeCoNav-Act揭示了集体物理能力评估的必要性。

Abstract: Robot teams can split workloads or combine complementary bodies, but only if each robot leaves the shared world in a state its teammate can use. Existing multi-robot benchmarks emphasize task allocation and skill sequencing, rarely testing whether the resulting physical states are usable. To evaluate collective physical capability, we introduce DeCoNav-Act, a benchmark for language-guided multi-robot mobile manipulation in a shared physics simulation. Its 350 homogeneous and 150 heterogeneous tasks, each verified by joint reference execution, span two coordination mechanisms: parallel workloads in shared space and physical dependencies in which one robot establishes a condition for a teammate to use. Beyond team success, DeCoNav-Act measures path efficiency and handoff coverage and success, and adds a diagnostic from verified pre-handoff states. Evaluating sensor-grounded adaptations of ASPIRE and RAX and our agent CoMoMa, we find that with GPT-5.6-luna no method completes a task, at most 8.8% of dependencies are reached, and even from pre-handoff states no handoff is completed; with GPT-6.1-sol, CoMoMa establishes the condition in 26.5% of trials, but only 4.1% succeed.

</details>


### [37] [DiffGR00T: Execution-Aware Differentiable Training for Humanoid Whole-Body Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32665)
*Sichen Wu,Qian Sun,Mengfan Liu,Weikai Qin,Bike Zhu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffGR00T 提出一种端到端可微分训练框架，将 VLA 生成器与预训练全身控制基础模型 SONIC 耦合，并用可学习的世界模型替代不可微仿真器，使执行感知梯度回传至 VLA 动作头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形分层 VLA 将动作生成与物理执行解耦，导致跟踪与平衡误差无法直接优化 VLA 生成器，难以满足人形全身控制对动态平衡与全身协调的要求。

Method: 将 GR00T N1.7 与预训练全身行为基础模型 SONIC 耦合；用动作条件的学习型世界模型替代不可微仿真器，使多步身体执行损失可经由冻结的全身控制器与流匹配采样器反向传播至 VLA 动作头。

Result: 在 HumanoidArena 四个仿真任务上，DiffGR00T 相比监督基线 VLA 平均任务成功率提升 12.5 个百分点（59.2% 提升至 71.7%）。

Conclusion: 执行感知梯度能有效将高层动作生成与闭环全身动力学对齐，验证了端到端可微分训练在人形全身 VLA 控制中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong results in arm-centric manipulation, yet humanoid whole-body control (WBC) requires dynamic balance and tightly coupled full-body coordination. Existing hierarchical humanoid VLAs typically decouple motion generation from physical execution, leaving tracking and balance errors unable to directly optimize the VLA generator. We propose DiffGR00T, an end-to-end differentiable training framework that couples GR00T N1.7 with SONIC, a pretrained whole-body behavior foundation model. To enable execution-aware differentiable training, we replace the non-differentiable simulator with a learned, action-conditioned world model, allowing multi-step body-execution losses to backpropagate through the frozen whole-body controller and the flow-matching sampler directly into the VLA action head. On four HumanoidArena simulation tasks, DiffGR00T improves average task success by 12.5 percentage points over the supervised base VLA (59.2% to 71.7%), demonstrating that execution-aware gradients effectively align high-level motion generation with closed-loop whole-body dynamics.

</details>


### [38] [DexWAC: A Unified World-Actor-Critic Model for Dexterous Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32654)
