On six continuous control tasks with TD3, POISE reduces the smoothness score on every task by $5.9\times$ on average ($4.5\times$ geometric mean), compared with $2.2\times$ ($1.9\times$) for the strongest baseline, and achieves the lowest score on five tasks. Paired return intervals include zero on four tasks, with a 14% increase on Ant and an 11% decrease on Hopper.

</details>


### [74] [VG-GP: Uncertainty-Gated Visuo-Geometric Memory for Negative-Transfer-Resistant Grasp Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31826)
*Yonglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VG-GP是一种即插即用的视-几何高斯过程记忆框架，用于闭环SE(2)残差抓取迁移，在无需重训练的情况下将预训练抓取策略在22个未见物体上的物理抓取成功率从38.6%提升至53.4%，且无性能回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练对跖抓取网络在规则形状上泛化良好，但在质量分布不规则、薄边缘或陌生手柄的新物体上零样本迁移常失败。情节记忆虽可适应冻结基策略，但朴素检索存在视觉外观偏差、覆盖有效基抓取的负迁移以及闭环采集退化等问题。

Method: 融合颜色不变表面法线DINOv2特征、规范SE(2)夹爪对齐3D局部高度图和接触几何描述子，采用乘积核并仅在源物体上学习ARD长度尺度；对基抓取与候选SE(2)替代方案建联合后验，仅当联合协方差下置信界(LCB)超过风险敏感阈值时才覆盖基策略；用单记录必要性与充分性干预的在线反事实筛选门隔离导致负迁移的源经验；通过等预算SE(2)探测上的贝叶斯期望改进(EI)闭环采集。

Result: 在ManiSkill3的42个不相交YCB物体（20源、12开发、10留出测试）上，VG-GP包裹预训练GR-ConvNet基策略，将全部22个未见物体的物理抓取率从38.6%(34/88)提升至53.4%(47/88)，零回退（+13次挽救，0次损失；物体聚类95% CI [+6.8%, +22.7%]，精确McNemar p=0.00024）。在10个留出物体上，EI主动采集达45.0%(18/40，基策略32.5%，达预言机余量5/7)，相对被动回退实现55.0%探测分歧，并单调降低认知不确定性。

Conclusion: VG-GP通过视-几何高斯过程记忆与风险敏感覆盖机制，有效实现零样本残差抓取迁移，消除负迁移并提升闭环采集效率，且不损害基策略性能。

Abstract: Pretrained antipodal grasping networks generalize well to canonical shapes but frequently fail zero-shot on novel objects with irregular mass distributions, thin rims, or unfamiliar handles. While episodic experience memory adapts a frozen base policy without retraining, naive retrieval suffers from visual appearance bias, negative transfer from overriding working base grasps, and degenerate closed-loop acquisition. We present VG-GP, a plug-and-play Visuo-Geometric Gaussian Process memory framework for closed-loop $SE(2)$ residual grasp transfer. First, we fuse color-invariant surface-normal DINOv2 features, canonical $SE(2)$ gripper-aligned 3D local heightmaps, and contact-geometry descriptors under a product kernel with Automatic Relevance Determination (ARD) lengthscales learned strictly on source objects. Second, we model the joint posterior over the base grasp and candidate $SE(2)$ alternatives, overriding the base policy only when the joint-covariance Lower Confidence Bound (LCB) clears a risk-sensitive threshold. Third, an online counterfactual curation gate using single-record necessity and sufficiency interventions quarantines source experiences causing negative transfer. Fourth, we close the acquisition loop via Bayesian Expected Improvement (EI) over equal-budget $SE(2)$ probes. Across 42 disjoint YCB objects in ManiSkill3 (20 source, 12 development, 10 held-out test), VG-GP wraps a pretrained GR-ConvNet base policy and raises physical lift rate across all 22 unseen objects from 38.6% (34/88) to 53.4% (47/88) with zero regressions (+13 rescues, 0 losses; object-clustered 95% CI [+6.8%, +22.7%], exact McNemar $p = 0.00024$). On the 10 held-out objects, EI active acquisition reaches 45.0% (18/40 vs. 32.5% base, 5/7 of oracle headroom), achieves 55.0% probe divergence over passive fallback, and monotonically reduces epistemic uncertainty.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond the Benchmark: Localizing Released Autonomous Driving Models to Real Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31814)
*Morui Zhu,Deyuan Qu,Qi Chen,Qing Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出R2R部署定位方法，识别并仅修正发布模型与源系统耦合的组件，结合HA-RL和冻结感知权重的传感器适配，实现新实车部署前的有效准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发布的自动驾驶模型在基准测试中表现优异，但部署到新实车仍困难，原因是模型即使经过常规适配，仍与源感知、数据和执行过程的假设耦合。

Method: 提出Released-to-Real（R2R）部署定位方法，识别源系统耦合并仅修正受影响组件而保留原模型；针对数据与行为适配，发现轨迹级优化与滚动时域执行的不匹配通过自车状态而非观测进入，提出Human-Anchored Reinforcement Learning（HA-RL），在记录的目标驾驶数据上修正，无需生成式闭环RL的高成本rollout；针对物理传感器适配，R2R在冻结感知权重下定位并修正源相关的空间和时间表示，无需目标标注或重训练。

Result: HA-RL将已发布扩散规划器的闭环成功率从82.2%提升至95.2%；跨多个已发布检测器和三个真实传感器平台的传感器适配，使已发布多相机检测器在五相机目标平台上的车辆mAP从0.041提升至0.420。

Conclusion: 这些结果为在新实车部署前准备已发布模型提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Released autonomous-driving models achieve strong benchmark performance, yet deploying them on a new real vehicle remains challenging. We study this deployment gap and find that released models can remain coupled to assumptions from their source sensing, data, and execution processes even after conventional adaptation. We introduce Released-to-Real (R2R), a deployment-localization methodology that identifies such source-system coupling and corrects only the affected components while preserving the released model. For data and behavior adaptation, we find that the mismatch between trajectory-level optimization and receding-horizon execution enters through the ego state rather than the observation, and introduce Human-Anchored Reinforcement Learning (HA-RL), which corrects it on recorded target driving without the costly rollouts of generative closed-loop reinforcement learning (RL). For physical sensor adaptation, R2R localizes and corrects source-dependent spatial and temporal representations with frozen perception weights, requiring neither target annotations nor retraining. HA-RL improves the closed-loop success rate of a released diffusion-based planner from 82.2% to 95.2%, while sensor adaptation across multiple released detectors and three real sensor rigs improves the vehicle mean average precision (mAP) of a released multi-camera detector from 0.041 to 0.420 on a five-camera target rig. These results provide a practical path for preparing released models before deployment on new real vehicles. Our project page is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [76] [Many Senses, One World: Learning Hierarchical Multimodal World Representations for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31782)
*Jin Yang,Youlin Wang,Ping Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HiMoDex两阶段框架，利用层次化多模态世界状态表示提升双臂灵巧操作策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧操作需融合视觉与非视觉物理交互线索，如何有效整合多模态输入进行策略学习是难点。

Method: 第一阶段在119万同步多模态时间步数据上预训练层次化表示网络，融合视觉、触觉、力/力矩和音频为统一世界状态嵌入；第二阶段冻结该网络，用其嵌入条件化共享世界-动作Transformer，联合学习动作生成与动作条件未来状态预测。

Result: 在五个真实任务上平均成功率达78.7%，比最强基线绝对提升32.0%；消融验证层次化表示学习和两阶段训练的优势，失败分析解释不同变体性能差异。

Conclusion: HiMoDex通过层次化多模态世界状态表示与两阶段训练有效提升双臂灵巧操作性能。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous manipulation relies on multimodal inputs that capture visual context and non-visual physical interaction cues for precise and coordinated control. To address the challenge of effectively integrating these modalities for policy learning, we propose HiMoDex, a two-stage framework centered on hierarchical multimodal world-state representations. In Stage 1, we pretrain a hierarchical representation network on the collected dataset of 1.19 million synchronized multimodal time steps. The network integrates visual context with tactile, force-torque, and audio cues into unified world-state embeddings. In Stage 2, we freeze the pretrained network and use its embeddings to condition a shared world-action transformer. The transformer jointly learns action generation and action-conditioned future-state prediction. Experiments across five real-world tasks show that HiMoDex achieves an average success rate of 78.7\%, representing an absolute improvement of 32.0\% over the strongest evaluated baseline. Extensive ablations validate the benefits of hierarchical representation learning and two-stage training. Failure analysis helps explain performance differences across variants. Code, data, and demos will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [77] [DAA for an Autonomous Delivery Drone: Maneuver Selection Policies for Near-Collision Risk Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31729)
*Franco Callari,Ishay Kamon,Anthony Narkawitz,Pradeep Yarlagadda,Amir Navot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对自主无人机在共享空域中的探测与规避（DAA）问题，比较了两种机动选择策略：纯几何策略与考虑不确定性策略，并通过大规模仿真评估发现后者性能显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DAA架构面向有人驾驶飞机设计，而自主无人机面临感知距离近（亚公里级）、决策时间短、CPA预测不确定性大、低空机动包线受限等挑战，需要专门研究机动选择策略。

Method: 提出并比较两种策略：（1）纯几何方法，在CPA处朝远离入侵者的方向转向；（2）考虑入侵者位置不确定性，在CPA处远离一个预期包含入侵者位置分布的平面转向。利用大规模仿真近碰撞遭遇数据集，以风险比指标评估性能。

Result: 两种策略均可从机载航迹估计中闭式求解，具备可行性；第二种考虑不确定性的策略性能显著优于第一种纯几何策略。

Conclusion: 在自主无人机DAA机动选择中，显式考虑不确定性可显著提升规避性能，是应对近距感知与短决策时间挑战的有效途径。

Abstract: Detect-and-avoid (DAA) systems for autonomous drones operating in shared airspace must select avoidance maneuvers under conditions that differ markedly from those assumed by conventional DAA architectures designed for crewed aircraft. Limited on-board sensing capabilities restrict detection of collision risk to near distances (sub-km), the time available for decision-making is severely limited, uncertainty in the predicted closest point of approach (CPA) and the time to reach it is substantial, and the maneuver envelope of a small uncrewed aircraft at low altitude is tightly constrained. This paper presents and compares two maneuver-selection strategies that address these challenges: (1) a purely geometric approach, steering in the direction away from the intruder at CPA, and (2) a method that also takes into account the known sources of uncertainty in the intruder's location, and steers away from a plane expected to contain the intruder's location distribution at CPA. Performance is evaluated using the risk ratio metric on a large dataset of simulated near-collision encounters with crewed intruders. Results indicate that both strategies are feasible (i.e., computable in closed form from an onboard track estimate), and that the second strategy substantially outperforms the first.

</details>


### [78] [Sparks of Weight-Space Programming for Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31715)
*Kyle Coelho,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用权重空间编程直接修改机器人基础模型参数，无需额外数据或训练即可纠正重复性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型在特定情境下会反复陷入相同的可识别卡死状态，但重新收集数据或微调成本高，因此探索能否通过极小的参数编辑触发纠正行为。

Method: 采用权重空间编程（WSP），由语言模型代理直接读写预训练视觉-语言-动作策略的神经网络参数；核心设计是复用动作专家中少数近静默神经元作为写入位置，使编辑仅在失败相关情境下激活并产生直接指定的纠正动作。

Result: 在仿真中对pi0.5策略，仅编辑约0.0002%的参数即可提升重复性失败的成功率，包括已微调过的策略；反向和随机编辑实验表明增益来自预期行为。

Conclusion: 该方法在失败情境可识别且存在简单纠正措施时效果最佳，为无需额外数据或训练的机器人策略纠错提供了可行途径。

Abstract: This paper demonstrates an approach for correcting failures of robot foundation models (RFMs) without additional data collection, demonstrations, or training. We apply weight-space programming (WSP), in which a language-model agent directly reads and writes a neural network's parameters, to a large, pre-trained vision-language-action policy. The idea is simple: many policy failures are not random but repeatedly land in the same recognizable stuck state, so if that state is visible in the model's internal activations, a small edit could trigger a corrective movement while leaving the rest of the model's parameters unchanged. A core design choice is where to write the edit: repurposing a few near-silent neurons in the action expert lets the edit switch on in situations associated with the failure and produce a corrective movement, such as lowering the gripper toward the target, that is specified directly rather than learned through training. Empirically, when applied to the pi0.5 policy in simulation, we find that edits to roughly 0.0002% of the parameters can improve success on recurring failures, including for a policy that was already fine-tuned on its task. Reversed and random edits indicate that the gains come from the intended behavior, and the approach works best when the failure situation is recognizable and a simple correction can address it.

</details>


### [79] [FoveaWAM: Role-Aware 4D World-Action Modeling for Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31705)
*Jiaqi Yin,Baoyue Zhang,Jiyuan Zhang,Wenxuan Liu,Zhaofei Yu,Yue Deng,Tiejun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoveaWAM提出一种原生4D世界-动作模型，按角色分配预测精度：目标姿态用度量SE(3)轨迹精确建模，周围环境用稀疏3D流表示，静态部分依赖当前帧；在单张RTX 5090上推理仅15.1 ms，比视频世界-动作基线快约10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作运动物体时，策略需预测动作执行瞬间的场景，但预测耗时会使该时刻不断后移；场景级预测计算昂贵，而目标中心预测又会遗漏影响接触的邻近物体。

Method: 核心思路是按角色解耦需预测的内容：仅目标位姿需毫米级精度以决定接触，周围环境主要提供间隙与运动信息。模型将目标过去与未来运动表示为度量SE(3)轨迹，周围环境表示为稀疏3D流，静态内容依赖当前帧，形成紧凑表示。

Result: 在单张RTX 5090上推理耗时15.1 ms，约为视频世界-动作基线的1/10；在自建的OnTimeBench动态操作基准上成功率达82.1%，比最强基线高20.8个百分点，并在DOMINO和真实机器人任务上领先。

Conclusion: 按角色分配预测精度可在保证操作精度的同时大幅降低推理延迟，使策略在场景持续演化时仍能及时执行动作，验证了该紧凑4D表示的优越性。

Abstract: To manipulate a moving object, a policy must predict the scene at the moment its action executes, yet every millisecond spent predicting pushes that moment further away. Scene-level forecasts are computationally expensive, whereas target-centric forecasts can omit nearby objects that affect contact. Our key insight is that what a policy must predict decouples by role: only the target's pose, which determines contact, requires millimeter precision, while the surroundings matter primarily through the clearance they leave and the motion they impart. We present FoveaWAM, a native 4D world-action model that allocates precision according to these roles. Specifically, it models the target's past and future motion as a metric SE(3) trajectory, the surroundings as sparse 3D flow, and relies on the current frame for everything static. This compact representation lets FoveaWAM act in 15.1 ms on one RTX 5090, about 10 times faster than video world-action baselines, leaving substantially less time for the scene to evolve before action execution. To evaluate each policy under its own latency, we introduce OnTimeBench, a dynamic manipulation benchmark in which the scene keeps moving while the policy computes. FoveaWAM reaches 82.1% success on OnTimeBench, 20.8 points above the strongest baseline, and also leads on DOMINO and real-robot tasks. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [80] [CATWAY: Cost-sensitive agentic three-way robot decisions with calibrated language-model confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31694)
*Erik Derner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出成本敏感三向决策门CATWAY，融合投票与多级评分，在多个域显著降低决策损失且零误执行不安全指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人代理需判断指令执行、质疑或拒绝，不安全执行代价远超不必要提问；现有门控未从错误成本导出阈值、未使用校准概率、也未跨域冻结验证。

Method: 构建四类指令条件（清晰、模糊、不可行、不安全）上的损失矩阵，在概率单纯形上划定执行、询问、拒绝区域；概率由十样本投票与模糊性、不可行性、风险分级评分经逻辑回归融合；在家庭指令上拟合后冻结，测试三个小模型文本决策、两个新机器人域及TRUST-NAV基准。

Result: 每次决策11代对比1代时，在全部18个家庭比较中显著更便宜（平均每项损失0.28对0.57-0.83），新域9/18、TRUST-NAV 10/18显著更优（Holm校正），从未显著更贵；142条不安全测试指令零执行；一致投票下最小期望损失规则将校准简化为单一损失导出阈值，导致六个模型配置中两个仅凭投票的门控从不执行清晰指令，TRUST-NAV每项损失0.49对CATWAY的0.13和0.09，融合估计在所有集合上避免此问题。

Conclusion: CATWAY以成本敏感三向决策和融合概率实现跨域鲁棒的低损失指令门控，显著优于直接提示、成本感知提示和无门控代理，并杜绝不安全执行。

Abstract: A language-model robot agent must decide whether to execute, question or refuse an instruction, and an unsafe execution costs far more than an unneeded question. No prior robot gate derives all its thresholds from error costs or acts on calibrated probabilities, and none was fitted in one domain and evaluated unchanged in another. We propose CATWAY, a cost-sensitive three-way decision gate: a loss matrix over four instruction conditions (clear, ambiguous, infeasible, unsafe) fixes execute, ask and refuse regions on the probability simplex, the question being the information-gathering step. Its probabilities fuse a ten-sample vote with graded scores of ambiguity, infeasibility and risk by logistic regression. Fitted on household instructions and frozen, CATWAY was tested on text-level decisions of three small models in-domain, in two new robot domains and on the external TRUST-NAV benchmark. At eleven generations per decision against one, it was significantly cheaper than a direct prompt, a cost-aware prompt and an unguarded agent in all 18 household comparisons (mean loss per item 0.28 against 0.57-0.83), in 9 of 18 in the new domains and in 10 of 18 on TRUST-NAV (Holm per set), and never significantly costlier. It executed none of the 142 unsafe test instructions. On a unanimous vote, the minimum-expected-loss rule reduces calibration to one loss-derived threshold, so for two of six model configurations a gate on the vote alone never executed a clear instruction, costing 0.49 per item on TRUST-NAV against CATWAY's 0.13 and 0.09; the fused estimate avoided this on every set.

</details>


### [81] [Exact Collision Gradients for Ellipsoids in Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31676)
*Shamak Dutta,Biswadip Dey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对椭球碰撞的可微仿真，提出基于Perram-Wertheim接触函数的连续碰撞检测并对接触条件求导，从而在任意步长下获得精确梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微仿真在碰撞瞬间梯度最不可靠：固定步长仿真器仅在物体重叠后才检测到碰撞，现有线性估计碰撞时刻的方法存在梯度误差，且忽略自旋会引入额外误差。

Method: 证明线性估计的梯度误差随碰撞倾斜度、接触曲率和时间步长增大，并量化忽略自旋的误差；随后用连续碰撞检测在Perram-Wertheim接触函数上求碰撞时刻，并对接触条件而非搜索过程求导。

Result: 在最优控制和系统辨识两个问题中，精确梯度能恢复最优控制和真实参数，而线性估计（即使考虑自旋）不能。

Conclusion: 通过对接触条件求导可获得任意步长下的精确碰撞梯度，显著优于线性碰撞时刻估计，适用于椭球碰撞的可微仿真优化。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation enables gradient-based optimization in learning and control problems involving collisions, but its gradients are least reliable at the moment of impact, when the velocities jump. A fixed-step simulator detects a collision only after the bodies overlap, and some differentiable simulators correct for this with a linear estimate of the time of impact. In this paper, we study impacts between ellipsoids. We show that the gradient error of this estimate grows with the obliqueness of the collision, the curvature at contact, and the time step, and that ignoring spin adds a further error. Subsequently, we find the time of impact with continuous collision detection on the Perram Wertheim contact function and differentiate through the touching condition rather than the search. This gives exact gradients at any step size. We demonstrate our method on an optimal control problem and a system identification problem. In both, exact gradients recover the optimal control and the true parameters, while the linear estimate does not, even when it accounts for spin.

</details>


### [82] [Exteroceptive Sensor Faults in Autonomous Vehicles: A Cross-Modal Taxonomy and Fault Injection Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31667)
*Davide Possenti,Daniele Cianca,Stefano Arrigoni,Francesco Braghini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文综述了自动驾驶外感知传感器故障，提出跨模态、以故障起源为导向的五类分类法（硬件、环境、标定、软件、协同），并发布含94种故障注入例程的开源工具包，关联ISO 26262与ISO 21448安全分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机、LiDAR、雷达等外感知传感器受硬件老化、环境干扰、标定误差、嵌入式软件错误及组合因素影响而退化，直接威胁感知质量与安全；现有综述与基准多针对单一传感器、孤立条件或整车级后果，缺乏跨三种模态统一描述故障起源的共同术语体系。

Method: 综述外感知传感器故障文献，构建跨模态、起源驱动的五类故障分类法（硬件、环境、标定、软件、协同），将每类映射到物理或逻辑机制、可观测表现及受影响子系统，并与ISO 26262和ISO 21448安全分析相关联；为落地分类法，发布开源故障注入工具包，含94个症状级例程（相机41、LiDAR 27、雷达26）、四级严重度、可复现突发调度及逐帧标注（故障类别、受影响子系统、严重度）。

Result: 在Boreas数据集上的示例展示了各类故障如何改变相机图像、LiDAR点云和雷达扫描；工具包公开可用，提供统一参考与受控测试平台。

Conclusion: 该分类法与工具包为研究传感器可靠性、开发与基准测试故障检测与诊断方法、构建带故障标注的数据集以支持感知系统安全验证提供了共享参考和受控测试环境。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles rely on cameras, LiDAR, and radar to perceive their surroundings, yet these exteroceptive sensors can be degraded by hardware aging, environmental interference, miscalibration, embedded software errors, and combinations of these causes, with direct consequences for perception quality and safety. Existing surveys and benchmarks mostly address individual sensors, isolated conditions, or vehicle-level consequences, and no common vocabulary relates fault origins across the three modalities. This paper reviews the literature on exteroceptive sensor faults and proposes a cross-modal, origin-driven taxonomy that organizes them into five categories, namely hardware, environmental, calibration, software, and synergistic faults, mapping each entry to its physical or logical mechanism, observable manifestation, and affected subsystem, and relating the categories to the safety analyses of ISO 26262 and ISO 21448. To make the taxonomy operational, we release an open-source fault injection toolkit with 94 symptom-level routines (41 camera, 27 LiDAR, and 26 radar), four graded severity levels, reproducible burst scheduling, and per-frame labels recording the fault category, affected subsystem, and severity. Examples on the Boreas dataset illustrate how each category alters camera images, LiDAR point clouds, and radar scans. Together, the taxonomy and the toolkit provide a shared reference and a controlled testbed for studying sensor reliability, developing and benchmarking fault detection and diagnosis methods, and building fault-annotated datasets for the safety validation of perception systems. The toolkit is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [83] [Collision Rendering: GPU-Accelerated Trajectory Planning on Sparse Voxel Radiance Fields with Risk-Bounded Collision Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31658)
*Byoungkwon Yoon,Han Gyeom Lee,Hojun Lee,Yuseop Sim,Daniel Sungwoo Bae,Martin Byung-Guk Jun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Collision Rendering框架，首次在稀疏体素辐射场上实现GPU加速、风险有界的碰撞检测与可微碰撞代价，用于轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射场虽能提供详细场景表示，但在轨迹规划中缺乏将学习到的空间基元转化为可靠碰撞准则的公认方法。

Method: 利用稀疏体素辐射场（SVR）的结构化体积密度，将渲染管线作为碰撞概率的快速数值近似器；用碰撞球近似机器人并在每个球处放置重合的球形相机；在概率碰撞模型下从相机渲染管线推导每个球最大方向碰撞概率的上界；在GPU上评估该上界得到模块化碰撞检测器，可与采样规划器集成且无需辅助几何地图；并通过同一管线从渲染不透明度计算碰撞代价及其梯度，扩展到轨迹优化。

Result: 在消费级GPU上实现毫秒级碰撞检测时间，所有查询均得到无碰撞轨迹，优于其他基于辐射场的规划器。

Conclusion: Collision Rendering桥接了辐射场表示与轨迹规划，提供了无需辅助地图的可靠、高效碰撞检测与优化框架。

Abstract: Although radiance fields provide detailed scene representations for robotics, they remain underexplored for trajectory planning, without a widely agreed-upon way to translate learned spatial primitives into reliable collision criteria. To bridge this gap, we introduce Collision Rendering, the first trajectory planning framework on sparse voxel radiance fields (SVR), which supports GPU-accelerated, risk-bounded collision checking and a differentiable collision cost. Our key insight is that the structured volumetric density of SVR allows its rendering pipeline to serve as a fast numerical approximator of collision probability. We approximate the robot with collision spheres and place a coinciding spherical camera at each sphere. Under a probabilistic collision model, we derive an upper bound on each sphere's maximum directional collision probability from the camera's rendering pipeline. Evaluating this bound on the GPU yields a modular collision checker that integrates with established sampling-based planners without an auxiliary geometric map or changes to the learned representation. We further compute a collision cost and its gradients from rendered opacities through the same pipeline, extending the framework to trajectory optimization. Experiments demonstrate millisecond collision checking time on a consumer-grade GPU and collision-free trajectories in all queries, outperforming other radiance field-based planners.

</details>


### [84] [A Self-Organizing Swarm Control Model for Underwater Robots Based on Turing-Type Reaction-Diffusion Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31637)
*Takeshi Ishida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需GPS、绝对坐标或领导者、仅依赖两个不同通信范围信道接收信号数量的去中心化群体控制模型，可在水下通信受限环境中实现群体自主编队与空间分散度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境无法使用GPS和高速无线电通信，通信带宽和能见度严重受限，传统Boids等群体编队方法依赖邻居相对位置和速度矢量，难以应用。

Method: 将基于图灵斑图形成原理的元胞自动机模型扩展到连续空间，依据接收信号数量差计算势场，机器人向高势区域运动；通过势值时间平均、基于迟滞的状态评估、基于时间差的步进转向以及与外部方向指令的矢量合成来获得稳定行为。

Result: 二维仿真表明可实现自主群体编队，并通过形态学参数控制群体空间分散度；在通信成功率降至约0.7的通信中断条件下，仍能保持群体维持和高极化度，具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提去中心化群体控制模型仅依赖双信道接收信号计数即可实现水下群体编队与分散度调控，且对通信丢包具有较强鲁棒性。

Abstract: In underwater environments, Global Positioning System (GPS) signals and high-speed radio communication are unavailable, and severe constraints exist on communication bandwidth and visibility; consequently, applying conventional swarm formation methods, such as the Boids model, which require the relative positions and velocity vectors of neighboring vehicles, is difficult. This study proposes a decentralized swarm control model that operates without a leader or absolute coordinates, relying solely on the number of received signals from two communication channels with different transmission ranges (inner and outer neighborhoods). The proposed model extends a cellular automaton model based on Turing pattern formation principles to continuous space and is grounded in the fundamental concept that swarms form as robots move toward regions of higher potential calculated from the difference in received signal counts. Specifically, stable behavior is determined through temporal averaging of potential values, hysteresis-based state evaluation, stepwise turning based on temporal differences, and vector composition with an external directional command. Two-dimensional simulation results demonstrated that autonomous swarm formation and control of swarm spatial dispersion via a morphological parameter are achievable, confirming robustness in maintaining swarms and high polarization even under communication dropouts where the communication success rate decreases to approximately 0.7.

</details>


### [85] [FabricBench: A Standardized Policy Benchmark for Bimanual Fabric Manipulation with a Contact-Driven Deformable Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31577)
*Wenkang Hu,Haoran Wang,Liu Liu,Wenchao Huang,Yiji Chen,Mengao Zhao,Xincheng Tang,Zhengjie Shu,Lai Jiang,Wei Li,Zhendong Wang,Zhizhong Su,Huamin Wang,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FabricBench：基于Newton开源求解器的双臂布料操作基准，含六个任务、万级资产与受控泛化测试，揭示现有策略在配置泛化上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布料操作面临几何变化、自遮挡与接触难题，但标准评测仍集中于刚体对象，缺乏可复现的布料操作基准。

Method: 构建FabricBench，含六个双臂布料任务（服装、丝绸、包袋）、10000个仿真就绪资产及配置/外观泛化受控测试；基于Newton开源求解器，通过接触力实现稳定双臂抓取、保持与释放，无需人工附着；重新设计布料-刚体接触与双向力耦合，支持机械臂、布料和自由刚体交互。

Result: 抓取-保持-释放测试中45次全部成功，基线配置为0；评测六种代表性策略（含VLA与世界动作模型），最佳平均成功率77%，长程T恤折叠无一超过36%，最严重初始形变下成功率降至6-14%，降幅大于外观变化；π0.5零样本真机部署基本保持六任务难度排序（Spearman ρ=0.94）。

Conclusion: FabricBench可作为可复现的布料操作能力评测平台，暴露配置泛化方面的局限。

Abstract: Fabric manipulation challenges robot policies with changing geometry, self-occlusion, and contact, yet standardized evaluation remains focused on rigid objects. We introduce FabricBench, a benchmark for bimanual fabric manipulation with six tasks spanning garments, silk, and bags, 10,000 simulation-ready assets, and controlled tests of configuration and appearance generalization. Built on an open-source solver in Newton, our simulator enables stable bimanual grasping, holding, and release through contact forces, without artificial attachment. Redesigned cloth-rigid contact and bidirectional force coupling support interactions among robot arms, fabric, and free rigid objects, allowing a bag to support its contents and transmit their weight to the grippers. In a controlled grasp-hold-release evaluation, our solver succeeds in all 45 trials, compared with none for the tested baseline configurations. Evaluating six representative policies, including vision-language-action models and world action models, reveals substantial remaining challenges: the best policy achieves 77% average success, while no policy exceeds 36% on long-horizon T-shirt folding. Under the most deformed initial T-shirt configurations, success falls to 6-14% across policies, with larger drops than under the evaluated appearance shifts. Zero-shot deployment of $\pi_{0.5}$ on a real robot largely preserves task difficulty ordering across six tasks (Spearman $\rho=0.94$). These results establish FabricBench as a reproducible testbed for measuring fabric-manipulation capabilities and exposing limitations in configuration generalization. The project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [86] [Sampling at Scale: Distributed Multi-Agent Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31561)
*Minchan Jung,Dimitrios S. Georgiou,Augustinos D. Saravanos,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DS-TO 分布式采样轨迹优化架构，通过为采样定制的分裂机制，使每个智能体仅在自己的控制空间内采样，从而在保持对约束非凸性和其他智能体决策感知的同时实现良好扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体轨迹优化面临探索非凸地形与大规模团队扩展两大耦合挑战；采样方法擅长探索但随维度扩展差，且直接嵌入分布式优化时存在采样无法应对约束非凸性或搜索空间随邻域规模增长的权衡。

Method: 构建基于分裂机制的 DS-TO 架构：每个智能体只在自己的控制空间采样，独立于邻域规模，同时仍暴露于约束非凸性和其他智能体决策；算法完全去中心化，仅需邻域通信，局部更新并行执行；并采用子问题内并行 Jacobi 更新与单步约束线性化等高效变体降低计算量。

Result: 在最多 1024 个智能体的 2D 和 3D 任务上，相比最先进的基于导数的分布式优化，探索能力和约束满足更好；相比现有分布式采样方法，扩展性更优。

Conclusion: DS-TO 通过采样定制的分裂机制弥合了探索非凸性与大规模扩展之间的差距，实现了完全去中心化、仅邻域通信且并行执行的分布式轨迹优化。

Abstract: Multi-agent trajectory optimization poses two coupled challenges: effectively exploring non-convex landscapes and scaling favorably to large teams of agents. Sampling-based methods are natural candidates for the exploration challenge but scale poorly with problem dimension. Moreover, their off-the-shelf integration into distributed optimization introduces a fundamental trade-off: sampling either fails to address the constraint-induced non-convexity or its search space grows with neighborhood size. We address this gap by introducing a $\underline{D}\text{istributed}$ $\underline{S}\text{ampling-based}$ $\underline{T}\text{rajectory}$ $\underline{O}\text{ptimization} \ \text{(DS-TO)}$ architecture built on a splitting mechanism tailored to sampling. Under this splitting, each agent samples only in its own control space, independent of neighborhood size, while still remaining exposed to both the constraint-induced non-convexity and the decisions of other agents. The resulting algorithm is fully decentralized, requiring only neighborhood communication, with all local updates executed in parallel across agents. Efficient variations, including parallel Jacobi updates within each agent's subproblem and single-step constraint linearization, further reduce computation. Experiments on 2D and 3D tasks with up to 1024 agents demonstrate improved exploration and constraint satisfaction over state-of-the-art derivative-based distributed optimization, and superior scalability to existing distributed sampling methods.

</details>


### [87] [Hi3R-VLA: Grounding Semantics and Actions in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31535)
*Shizhe Chen,Paul Pacaud,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Hi3R-VLA，一种将视觉、语言与动作统一在显式3D几何中的机器人操作策略，在多个仿真基准和真实机器人上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型虽引入3D几何信息，但如何有效利用3D几何实现精确且可泛化的动作预测仍是核心难题。

Method: 将空间对齐的视觉特征和语言条件融入点云表示，以细粒度语义丰富场景几何；将低层控制指令锚定在同一3D坐标系中，并利用3D-RoPE增强的动作到点注意力在由粗到细的点特征上建模动作目标与观测点之间的几何关系。

Result: 在RoboCasa、RLBench、RoboTwin2、DexJoCo和DexSuite上，覆盖单臂、双臂夹爪及灵巧手等多种操作任务与本体，以少于250M可训练参数达到最优性能；真实机器人评估进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 分析表明3D内语义融合以及观测与动作间的空间对齐至关重要，Hi3R-VLA是高效且可泛化的3D操作策略，模型、代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: While recent vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly incorporate 3D geometry, how to effectively exploit it for precise and generalizable action prediction remains a central challenge. We introduce Hi3R-VLA, a 3D policy that grounds heterogeneous modalities - vision, language, and action - in explicit 3D geometry for robotic manipulation. Our model integrates spatially-aligned visual features and language conditioning into point cloud representations, enriching scene geometry with fine-grained, task-relevant semantics. It then anchors low-level control commands in the same 3D coordinate frame and uses 3D-RoPE enhanced action-to-point attention over coarse-to-fine point features to model geometric relationships between action targets and observed points. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of semantic fusion within 3D and spatial alignment between observations and actions. With fewer than 250M trainable parameters, Hi3R-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa, RLBench, RoboTwin2, DexJoCo and DexSuite across diverse manipulation tasks and embodiments, from single-arm and dual-arm grippers to dexterous hands. Real-robot evaluations further demonstrate its effectiveness in the physical world. The model, code and dataset are publicly released at this https URL .

</details>


### [88] [Embodied Neuro-symbolic AI: Making Robot Behavior Auditable, Safe, and Scalable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31488)
*Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Jonathan Francis,Alessandro Oltramari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Embodied Neuro-Symbolic（NeSy）AI范式，将学习式感知、规划与控制同显式机器可读背景知识和形式化约束结合，以解决VLA系统在真实部署中的泛化脆弱、物理因果推理不足、细粒度控制不稳定及安全合规难审计等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作（VLA）端到端系统在分布偏移下泛化脆弱、缺乏物理与因果推理、运动控制不一致，且难以表达和审计机器人“不应做什么”，在安全关键场景中无法提供保障，限制了可扩展性、可维护性与可信度。

Method: 构建面向部署的Embodied NeSy AI范式，整合学习式感知、规划与控制，并引入显式、机器可读的背景知识与形式化约束；具体通过知识引导感知、约束感知符号规划与混合学习三方面实现。

Result: 该范式使机器人能够推理环境、适应新任务，并对安全与运行要求提供可审计、机器可检查的合规性，从而支持可扩展且可信的机器人部署。

Conclusion: 将神经符号AI与具身智能结合、以显式形式化约束补足端到端VLA的短板，是迈向可扩展、可信真实世界机器人部署的可行方向。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) end-to-end systems have driven major progress in robot learning, yet they still face persistent barriers to dependable real-world deployment. In practice, these models often exhibit brittle generalization under distribution shift (i.e., novel objects, layouts, lighting, or contact conditions), limited physical and causal reasoning, and inconsistent fine-grained motor control. Critically, many VLA pipelines offer limited ways to express and audit what a robot must not do, making it difficult to provide assurance in safety-critical settings. These limitations constrain scalability, maintainability, and trust when robots move beyond controlled demonstrations. We propose Embodied Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) AI: a deployment-oriented paradigm that integrates learned perception, planning and control with explicit, machine-readable background knowledge and formal constraints. By combining knowledge-guided perception, constraint-aware symbolic planning, and hybrid learning, Embodied NeSy AI enables robots to reason about their environment, adapt to new tasks, and provide auditable, machine-checkable compliance with safety and operational requirements, thus supporting scalable, trustworthy robot deployment.

</details>


### [89] [CRAB-H: Task-Centric Certification for Healthcare Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31470)
*Tord Johan Eide,Einar Holt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAB H 是一种以任务为中心的医疗自主系统认证方法，通过任务需求配置文件（TRP H）将任务作为一等治理对象，认证即比较已证明能力与任务所需能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证侧重基准分数是否足够高，而非机器人能力是否满足具体医疗任务的安全执行要求，需要一种任务驱动、保留不可靠维度治理的认证框架。

Method: 扩展 ARC 治理架构，引入 TRP H 定义认知能力、治理约束、证据要求和运行假设；采用可靠性门控的保证分配机制，将无法可复现评估的认知维度路由到 LEA 运行要求；以基于阈值的逐维合规合取定义能力裕度。

Result: 认证转化为已证明能力与所需能力的比较；不可靠维度被治理而非丢弃，治理完整性得以保持；Capability Margin 以非补偿式逻辑表达各维度合规判定。

Conclusion: CRAB H 为安全关键医疗部署提供任务中心、非补偿式的认证逻辑，确保不可复现评估的维度仍受治理，从而维持治理完整性。

Abstract: This paper introduces CRAB H as a task-centric certification methodology for healthcare autonomous systems. CRAB H extends the ARC governance architecture by introducing the task as a first-class governance object, represented through Task Requirement Profiles TRP H. Each profile defines the cognitive capabilities, governance constraints, evidentiary requirements, and operational assumptions necessary for the safe execution of a specific healthcare activity. Rather than asking whether a robot achieves a sufficiently high benchmark score, CRAB H asks whether demonstrated capabilities satisfy the requirements of the task. Certification becomes a comparison between demonstrated capability and required capability. A reliability-gated assurance allocation mechanism routes cognitive dimensions that cannot be reproducibly evaluated to LEA operational requirements rather than discarding them. Governance integrity is preserved: unreliable dimensions are governed, not ignored. The Capability Margin is defined as the threshold-based conjunction of per-dimension compliance determinations, not as a subtracted scalar preserving non compensatory governance logic appropriate for safety-critical healthcare deployment

</details>


### [90] [Geometric Control Analysis of an Internally Actuated Flying Wing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31463)
*Jonathan Becker,Ella M. Atkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对仅由内部移动质量、动量轮和外部推力驱动的固定翼无人机进行了严格的几何可达性与可控性分析，并通过非线性MPC在扰动流场中验证了轨迹跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除传统控制面可减少高保真CFD建模所需的研究组合，因此需要从几何控制理论角度严格证明该非常规驱动配置的可达性与可控性。

Method: 采用李括号计算与强李代数秩条件分析全非线性动力学的局部可达性，用Sussmann条件分析姿态子系统在平衡点的小时间局部可控性，并通过Huygens-Steiner定理显式处理时变惯性张量；随后设计考虑飞行包线约束的非线性MPC。

Result: 全非线性动力学在一般情形下是小时间局部可达的，奇异构型可被显式刻画并可通过直接质量驱动立即逃逸；姿态子系统在平衡点满足小时间局部可控性；动量轮提供直接偏航权限，与移动质量引起的惯性变化共同生成满秩可达李代数；MPC在无扰动与扰动流场条件下均实现轨迹跟踪。

Conclusion: 该无控制面驱动配置在几何意义上具备良好的可达性与可控性，所设计的非线性MPC能够有效应对流场扰动，为非常规驱动固定翼UAS的建模与控制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a rigorous geometric accessibility and controllability analysis for a fixed-wing Uncrewed Aircraft System (UAS) actuated solely by an internal moving mass, a momentum wheel, and external thrust. This configuration eliminates traditional control surfaces, which in turn reduces the combinatorial set of computational fluid dynamics (CFD) studies necessary for high-fidelity modeling of flight through unsteady flow fields. Using Lie bracket computations and the strong Lie Algebra Rank Condition, it is shown that the full nonlinear dynamics are generically small-time locally accessible, with singular configurations explicitly characterized and shown to be immediately escapable by direct mass actuation. The stronger property of small-time local controllability is shown to hold for the attitude subsystem at equilibrium points, using Sussmann's condition. A key contribution is the explicit treatment of the time-varying inertia tensor via the Huygens-Steiner Theorem, which introduces nontrivial coupling between the control vector fields. The momentum wheel provides direct yaw authority that, combined with inertia variation from the moving mass, generates a full-rank accessibility Lie algebra. A nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (MPC) is derived with consideration of flight envelope constraints and is applied to track different trajectories under unperturbed and perturbed flow field conditions.

</details>


### [91] [Learning from Human Corrections through Temporal Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31446)
*Anjiabei Wang,Tesca Fitzgerald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将人类纠正机器人时的干预前和干预后行为转化为偏好对，用于学习价值函数，并验证其在目标推断与纠正预测任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于纠正的学习方法仅比较新轨迹与机器人原定行为，忽略了干预前机器人行为所提供的上下文信息，导致信息利用不充分。

Method: 从干预后行为（纠正行为与被替换行为的比较）构建后干预偏好，从干预前状态间的时间关系构建前干预偏好，用于学习近似人类潜在奖励的价值函数，并通过目标推断和纠正预测两个下游任务进行评估。

Result: 后干预偏好对目标推断特别有效，前干预偏好在纠正预测上表现强；结合两种信息在两项任务上均表现良好，且能泛化到训练中未见的参与者。

Conclusion: 人类何时干预与如何纠正提供了互补信息，二者共同有助于从纠正交互中学习价值表示。

Abstract: When people intervene to correct a robot, they provide information not only through how they correct its behavior, but also through when they choose to intervene. Existing approaches to Learning from Corrections focus on comparing the new trajectory to the robot's originally intended behavior, thus underutilizing the context of the robot's behavior before the intervention. In this work, we investigate how both pre- and post-intervention behavior can be translated into preference pairs for learning value functions that approximate a human's latent reward. We construct post-intervention preferences by comparing corrected behavior with the behavior it replaces, and pre-intervention preferences from temporal relationships among states leading up to an intervention. We evaluate the learned value representations through two downstream tasks: goal inference and correction forecasting. Our results show that post-intervention preferences are particularly effective for goal inference, while pre-intervention preferences provide strong correction-forecasting performance. Combining both sources of information performs strongly across both tasks, even when generalizing to participants unseen during training. These findings demonstrate that both when and how humans correct provide complementary information for learning value representations from corrective interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Learning World Models through Contrastive Identification of Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32776)
*Hayeong Lee,Youngjun Suh. Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出对比动作识别（CIA），通过从历史条件行为提议中采样替代动作来增强逆动力学监督，保留更多动作相关信息，从而提升世界模型规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型虽能基于离线视觉数据进行目标导向规划，但仅靠预测无法明确表示应保留哪些动作相关区分；传统的逆动力学平方误差动作回归只通过条件动作均值变化奖励额外信息，信息量有限。

Method: 提出CIA方法，在历史条件行为提议采样的替代动作中识别实际执行动作，通过对比同一上下文下的合理动作，捕获下一潜在状态提供的超出历史的信息；理论证明在精确行为匹配下，CIA能奖励不改变最优逆回归误差的动作信息，并用受控学习示例验证。

Result: 在五个视觉控制基准上，CIA在不修改规划器的情况下，其规划器平均成功率高于基础世界模型和回归逆动力学基线。

Conclusion: 对比动作识别能有效增强世界模型的动作相关表示，提升规划性能，且无需改动规划器。

Abstract: Latent world models enable goal-directed planning from offline visual data, but prediction alone does not specify which action-related distinctions their representations should preserve. Inverse dynamics provides auxiliary supervision, yet squared-error action regression rewards additional information only through changes in the conditional action mean. We propose Contrastive Identification of Actions (CIA), which identifies the executed action among alternatives sampled from a history-conditioned behavior proposal. Comparing actions plausible in the same context targets information supplied by the next latent beyond history. Under exact behavior matching, we show that CIA can reward action information that leaves optimal inverse-regression error unchanged, and verify this distinction through a controlled learning example. CIA achieves higher planner-averaged mean success than the base world model and a regression inverse-dynamics baseline across five visual control benchmarks, without modifying the planner.

</details>


### [93] [EviCraft: Can Minecraft Agents Find What They Cannot See?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32765)
*Wenyou Yi,Lantian Zhang,Guanlin Chen,Xingqiu Xu,Haiyang Sun,Minrui Huang,Tianle Liang,Hao Chen,Haorui Xu,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出证据闭包要求，并构建EviCraft运行时与基准，强制Agent所有决策仅依赖其拥有证据的世界事实；在Minecraft中对比感知接地（G）与加载状态（L）两种制度，发现L制度下分数虚高主要源于对未见埋藏资源的直接访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Minecraft具身Agent基准中，mineBlock等语义工具可直接从模拟器已加载世界状态选取目标而无需可见性检验，导致Agent可获取从未见过的埋藏矿石，分数无法反映其是否真正搜索。

Method: 将缺失需求形式化为证据闭包：Agent系统（含工具内部）的每个决策只能通过Agent持有证据的事实依赖世界。EviCraft在Agent之下强制执行闭包，并将准入规则设为实验变量：感知接地制度G仅允许视线内或Agent自身动作返回的事实，加载状态制度L复现标准工具栈，同时固定Agent、工具与世界。

Result: 在Survival任务中，制度效应超过最强Agent的领先幅度：G下三个中等规模Agent在32%-40%的运行中到达钻石，L下为76%-94%，且G下最强Agent得分低于L下每个Agent。两个模型中仅移除视线检验即弥合93%-96%的分数差距，仅在工具内部加载状态可恢复70%-98%，说明对观测与查询的保护仍留下大部分泄漏。在开放式获取中，L的增益集中于依赖埋藏资源或生物的物品。

Conclusion: 语义工具绕过可见性检验会显著虚增具身Agent基准分数；证据闭包应作为运行时强制要求，仅在观测层加保护不足以堵住泄漏，需在工具层强制执行感知接地。

Abstract: In Minecraft, a common testbed for embodied agents, semantic tools such as mineBlock pick targets from the simulator's loaded world state without a visibility test, so a buried ore the agent has never seen is one call away, and scores cannot show whether the agent searched. We formalize the missing requirement as evidence closure: every decision of an agent system, including those inside its tools, may depend on the world only through facts for which the agent holds evidence. EviCraft is a runtime and benchmark that enforces closure below the agent and makes the admission rule an experimental variable: a perception-grounded regime (G) admits only what is in line of sight or returned by the agent's own actions, and a loaded-state regime (L) reproduces standard tool stacks, with agents, tools, and worlds held fixed. On Survival, the regime effect exceeds the strongest agent's lead: under G, three mid-sized agents reach the diamond in 32%-40% of runs, against 76%-94% under L, and the strongest agent under G scores below each of them under L. In two models, removing only the line-of-sight test closes 93%-96% of the score gap, and loading state only inside tools recovers 70%-98%, so safeguards on observations and queries leave most of the leak open. In open-ended acquisition, the gain from L concentrates in items that depend on buried resources or mobs. Code and benchmark are available at this https URL

</details>


### [94] [Sparse Poison, Strong Hijack: Efficient Trajectory-Level Backdoors in Vision-Language-Action Models with Only 1\% Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32304)
*Simin Huang,Jiawei Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Qiwei Liang,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型的后门威胁，提出视觉-语言-动作引导的两阶段协同优化框架，在仅1%投毒率下实现轨迹级定向后门，兼顾目标成功率与干净任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型依赖示范数据易受后门攻击；轨迹级定向后门比破坏行为更具安全风险，但现有方法需高投毒率获取目标轨迹监督，高投毒率损害干净任务性能，低投毒率则监督稀缺且易被干净任务更新干扰。

Method: 两阶段协同优化框架：第一阶段冻结策略，用可微渲染与多模态监督优化3D触发器的纹理、平移与尺度；第二阶段基于优化触发器进行源选择性双LoRA适配以解耦干净与后门更新，并采用逐列校准与解析融合为统一策略。

Result: 在OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT、π0、π0.5及四个LIBERO套件上一致优于基线；仅1%投毒数据下，OpenVLA-OFT在LIBERO-Spatial达86.4%目标成功率与95.2%干净任务成功率；真实机器人实验亦实现轨迹级劫持。

Conclusion: 该框架在低投毒率下有效解决轨迹级定向后门监督稀缺与干净任务干扰的权衡，实现高目标成功率并保持干净任务性能，且可迁移至真实机器人。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models advance robotic manipulation, but their reliance on demonstrations exposes them to backdoor threats. Among these threats, trajectory-level targeted backdoors are particularly challenging and may pose greater safety risks than behavior-disrupting attacks: rather than merely causing task failures or local action deviations, they must sustain closed-loop redirection to complete an attacker-specified multi-step task. To reliably induce such behaviors, existing methods typically rely on high poisoning rates to obtain sufficient target-trajectory supervision. However, high poisoning rates may compromise clean-task performance, whereas low rates leave target-trajectory supervision scarce and backdoor learning vulnerable to interference from dominant clean-task updates. To resolve this trade-off at low poisoning rates, we propose a two-stage co-optimization framework guided by vision, language, and action. Stage I freezes the policy and uses differentiable rendering and multimodal supervision to optimize a 3D trigger's texture, translation, and scale, strengthening trigger-target associations from limited poisoned demonstrations. Building on the optimized trigger, Stage II applies source-selective dual-LoRA adaptation to decouple clean and backdoor updates and mitigate their interference, followed by learned column-wise calibration and analytic fusion into a unified policy. Experiments on OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, $\pi_0$, and $\pi_{0.5}$ across four LIBERO suites show consistent gains over all evaluated attack baselines. With only 1% poisoned data, our method achieves 86.4% target success and 95.2% clean-task success on OpenVLA-OFT in LIBERO-Spatial. Real-robot trials also demonstrate trajectory-level hijacking.

</details>


### [95] [PoseVLN: Learning Geometric State Evolution for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32218)
*Pengyu Hou,Mingfei Han,Haihong Hao,Xiaolong Wu,Bo Dong,Xiaojun Chang,Salman Khan,Bin Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PoseVLN，用相机位姿统一表示过去、现在与未来状态，联合学习导航决策与几何状态演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法分别建模长时记忆、状态推理与未来预测，导致导航状态在时间上的演化连接不足。

Method: 将视觉记忆与当前观测组织在回合相对坐标系中，联合预测未来K步动作及同视野局部ΔPose，并与当前轨迹级位姿复合得到未来位姿，递归传播形成连续几何状态演化。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE Val-Unseen上，仅用单RGB观测，成功率分别比最强基线高2.6和2.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式建模几何状态演化对VLN有效，相机位姿是桥接过去、现在与未来的自然几何表示。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to relate past experience, current spatial state, and future motion while following natural-language instructions. Recent approaches improve these capabilities through long-horizon memory, state-aware reasoning, and future prediction, yet these components are often modeled separately, leaving navigation state evolution insufficiently connected across time. We argue that camera pose provides a natural geometric representation for bridging past, present, and future states. Based on this insight, we propose PoseVLN, a pose-aware VLN framework that jointly learns navigation decisions and geometric state evolution. PoseVLN organizes visual memory and the current observation in an episode-relative coordinate frame, and jointly predicts the next K navigation actions together with a local $\Delta$Pose over the same horizon. The predicted $\Delta$Pose is composed with the current trajectory-level pose to obtain the future pose state, which is recursively propagated throughout navigation, forming a continuous geometric state evolution from past memory to future motion. Experiments on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE Val-Unseen splits demonstrate the effectiveness of explicitly modeling geometric state evolution, with PoseVLN outperforming the strongest compared baselines by 2.6 and 2.9 percentage points in success rate, respectively, using only single RGB observations.

</details>


### [96] [XMind: Thinking Twice via Cross-Memory Reasoning for LLM-Based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32209)
*Zuyi Guo,Xianjie Dai,Lei Yang,Xiaocong Zhao,Jingguang Li,Kitae Jang,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架XMind，通过跨记忆推理让LLM驾驶智能体“三思而后行”，提升交通条件变化下的决策成功率并降低风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强LLM驾驶智能体主要记录观测结果，缺乏关于替代行动的证据，导致学到的修正难以在交通条件变化时迁移。

Method: XMind整合近期交通状态的工作记忆与关键风险事件的情景记忆；智能体先做初步决策，再与检索到的历史经验交叉核对，冲突时依据证据做二次决策；通过扰动交通条件下的反事实推演评估替代行动，将验证结果存为结构化风险胶囊，并联合匹配空间风险模式和场景语义进行检索。

Result: 闭环高速公路仿真中成功率达83.3-93.3%，平均超过强基线6.6个百分点；在K-Risk上对未见真实高风险交互的决策风险更低；消融实验证实工作记忆、风险文本检索和反事实验证的互补收益。

Conclusion: 跨记忆推理与反事实验证可有效增强LLM驾驶智能体在动态交通下的经验迁移和风险规避能力。

Abstract: Memory-augmented large language model (LLM) driving agents can reuse past experience, yet previously learned corrections often fail to transfer when traffic conditions change. A key limitation is that existing memories mainly record observed outcomes, providing little evidence about alternative actions. We introduce XMind, a training-free framework that enables LLM-based driving to "Think Twice" through cross-memory reasoning. XMind integrates working memory of recent traffic states with episodic memory of critical risk events. The agent first makes an initial decision from the current context and then cross-checks it against retrieved historical experience. If a conflict arises, it makes a second decision guided by the retrieved evidence. To construct reliable episodic memory, XMind evaluates alternative actions through counterfactual rollouts under perturbed traffic conditions and stores the verified outcomes as structured risk capsules. These capsules are retrieved by jointly matching spatial risk patterns and scenario semantics. Across closed-loop highway simulations, XMind achieves success rates of 83.3-93.3%, exceeding the strong baseline by 6.6 percentage points on average. Evaluation on K-Risk further demonstrates lower decision risk on unseen real-world high-risk interactions. Ablation studies confirm the complementary benefits of working memory, risk-text retrieval, and counterfactual verification. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [97] [CoverMem: Coverage-Constrained Visual Memory for Budgeted Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32119)
*Ruijie Ma,Haoyue Liu,Mingda Zhang,Dongxu Yue,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoverMem，通过覆盖约束的记忆构建、增量维护与预算内互补帧选择，在有限视觉预算下提升情节记忆具身问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体观测历史不断增长、不同视角质量不一且计算资源有限，难以在构建紧凑视觉记忆的同时检索足够证据回答问题。

Method: CoverMem包含三部分：覆盖约束的记忆构建（选取满足物体质量与代表性场景覆盖的紧凑全帧集合）、保持覆盖的增量维护策略（仅局部重选受影响帧以满足更新后的覆盖要求）、预算受限的互补帧选择（在固定预算内平衡问题相关性、额外支持与冗余）。

Result: 在覆盖多样环境与问题类型的三个基准上，CoverMem在有限视觉预算下提升了问答性能。

Conclusion: 覆盖约束与预算感知的帧选择可在紧凑记忆下有效支持情节记忆具身问答。

Abstract: Episodic-memory embodied question answering enables agents to answer questions based on previously recorded visual observations of the surrounding scenes. However, growing observation histories, varying observation quality across viewpoints, and limited computational resources make it challenging to construct and maintain compact visual memories while retrieving sufficient evidence for question answering. To address these challenges, we introduce CoverMem, which incorporates three key components: (1) a coverage-constrained memory construction method that selects a compact set of informative full frames satisfying object-quality and representative-scene coverage requirements; (2) a coverage-preserving incremental maintenance strategy that locally reselects only the affected frames to satisfy updated coverage requirements as observations arrive; and (3) a budgeted complementary frame selection method that balances question relevance, additional support for question requirements, and redundancy with selected frames within a fixed budget. Experimental results on three benchmarks covering diverse environments and question types demonstrate that CoverMem improves question-answering performance under limited visual budgets.

</details>


### [98] [EffectWAM: Learning Action-Grounded Effects for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32053)
*Yuan Zhang,Hao Cui,Jingfei Ni,Meiyi Wang,Xin Nie,Chi Liu,Wenjie Xu,Lin Gao,Hao Li,Xianhe Zhang,Jiajia Wu,Diyuan Liu,Jia Pan,Chao Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EffectWAM通过Effect、CaPE和Action Grounding三个组件连接视觉预测与机器人控制，实现无需等待视频生成的实时动作执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型需要将视觉预测与可执行机器人控制有效连接，现有方法可能存在视觉表示与动作执行脱节、依赖视频合成导致控制延迟等问题。

Method: 提出EffectWAM，包含：Effect（以独立编码的未来观测与共享当前观测锚点之差表示视觉变化）；CaPE（在可靠外参可用时基于相对相机位姿条件化端点池化，并与无位姿池化共享参数）；Action Grounding（训练视觉表示保留末端执行器位姿和夹爪状态的测量变化）；使用观测编码器的指数移动平均提供Effect目标；共享生成专家通过流匹配联合预测Effect和可执行动作块，并按需复用采样对生成未来视频；训练于超过20,000小时异构具身数据，适配数据集特定的关节、末端执行器和手部位姿表示。

Result: 在全部五个基准上使用Small（8B）配置评估，并在RoboDojo上额外评估Large（26B）配置；闭环操作实验及与多个基线策略的比较显示Action Grounding带来一致改进。

Conclusion: EffectWAM能够在不等待视频合成的情况下执行动作，验证了Effect、CaPE与Action Grounding组合在闭环机器人操作中的有效性。

Abstract: World-action models connect visual prediction with executable robot control. We present EffectWAM, which develops this connection through three complementary components: Effect, camera positional encoding (CaPE), and Action Grounding. Effect represents visual change as the difference between independently encoded future observations and a shared current-observation anchor. CaPE conditions endpoint pooling on relative camera pose when reliable extrinsics are available, while sharing the same learned parameters with pose-free pooling for uncalibrated trajectories. Action Grounding trains the visual representation to retain measured changes in end-effector pose and gripper state. An exponential moving average of the observation encoder supplies Effect targets. A shared generation expert learns to jointly predict Effect and an executable action chunk through flow matching, and reuses the sampled pair to generate future video on demand. This allows the controller to execute actions without waiting for video synthesis. We train on more than 20,000 hours of heterogeneous embodied data, excluding unused chain-of-thought data, and accommodate dataset-specific joint, end-effector, and hand-pose representations. Our evaluation uses the Small (8B) configuration on all five benchmarks and additionally evaluates the Large (26B) configuration on RoboDojo. Experiments examine closed-loop manipulation across complementary benchmarks, and comparisons across multiple baseline policies show consistent improvements from Action Grounding.

</details>


### [99] [A Generative AI Agent to Emulate Interactive Human-driven Vehicles for Automated Driving Testing in Urban Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31797)
*Jia Hu,Changjian Yu,Panqu Wang,Jaehyun So,Yang Chang,Xuerun Yan,Jintao Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种生成式AI智能体，通过视野建模、混合专家网络和残差强化学习来仿真交互式人类驾驶车辆，在INTERACTION数据集上将五项交互特征的JSD降低39.9%–87.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市自动驾驶安全测试中的人类驾驶车辆交互建模方法难以复现真实的安全关键交互，原因包括：假设完全可观测而忽略遮挡引起的交互、驾驶风格主要由车辆机动刻画导致安全关键行为多样性不足、以及非关键驾驶行为稀释了安全关键交互的学习信号。

Method: 构建生成式AI智能体：引入视野建模以刻画遮挡下的驾驶员可见性；用涵盖初始、中间和最终阶段的五项指标刻画交互过程；通过动态混合专家网络自动学习多样化交互风格；再借助残差强化学习精炼生成轨迹，使其匹配真实世界中安全关键交互特征的分布。

Result: 在INTERACTION数据集上，相比最先进基线，该方法在五项交互特征上将JSD降低39.9%–87.1%；最终阶段交互指标提升最高达87.1%，表明可减少长时程轨迹生成的误差累积；消融实验验证了各组件有效性，尤其残差精炼作用显著；泛化实验显示在未见交叉口仅需一个epoch微调即可接近完整训练的性能。

Conclusion: 该生成式智能体能够更真实地复现安全关键的人类驾驶车辆交互，提升长时程轨迹生成精度并具备良好泛化能力，为城市自动驾驶安全测试提供有效支持。

Abstract: Realistic modeling of human-driven vehicle (HDV) interactions is essential for automated driving safety testing in urban areas. Existing methods struggle to reproduce realistic safety-critical interactions involving high collision risk for the following reasons: they often assume full observability, overlooking interactions arising from occlusion. Driving styles are mainly characterized by vehicle maneuvers, limiting the diversity of safety-critical interaction behaviors. Moreover, learning signals from safety-critical interactions can be diluted by non-critical driving behaviors. To address these issues, we propose a generative AI agent to emulate interactive HDVs. The agent incorporates field-of-view modeling to account for driver visibility under occlusion and characterizes the interaction process using five metrics spanning its initial, intermediate, and final stages. It automatically learns diverse interaction styles through a dynamic mixture-of-experts network. Through residual reinforcement learning, the agent further refines generated trajectories to match the real-world distributions of safety-critical interaction characteristics. Experiments on the INTERACTION dataset show that our method reduces Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) by 39.9\%--87.1\% across five interaction characteristics compared with a state-of-the-art baseline. Our method achieves greater improvements of up to 87.1\% in final-stage interaction metrics, suggesting that it can reduce error accumulation in long-horizon trajectory generation. Ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of each proposed component, particularly highlighting the importance of residual refinement. Generalization experiments show that our method approaches the performance of full training on unseen intersections with only one epoch of fine-tuning.

</details>


### [100] [Perception Is Enough, at the Right Scale: Scale-Adaptive Token Pruning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31954)
*Yifeng Lin,Liuxiang Qiu,Yunfeng Chen,Tiesong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TauPrune-VLA 提出一种免训练 token 剪枝方法，用自适应感知温度 τ 控制保留的感知表示尺度，实现最高 1.86 倍推理加速并保持接近基线的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型处理连续视觉流时计算开销大，限制实时机器人控制；现有 token 剪枝使用固定语义尺度，会忽略操作不同阶段所需的信息。

Method: 通过单一感知温度 τ 控制保留感知表示的尺度，并利用感知语义趋势在线更新 τ，由机器人状态变化和动作幅度调节调整强度；利用相邻帧语义相对稳定的连续性，将当前观测计算的保留索引用于下一次策略调用，使 token 选择与推理异步执行、降低剪枝开销。

Result: 在多种 VLA 模型和仿真基准上取得最高 1.86 倍推理加速，任务成功率接近基线；并在两个真实机器人平台上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应语义尺度的 token 选择无需依赖内部注意力或额外训练，即可在保持任务性能的同时显著提升 VLA 模型推理效率，并具备真实平台可用性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models must process continuous streams of visual observations, and the resulting computational cost limits real-time robotic control. Token pruning reduces this cost, but a fixed semantic scale of perceptual representation can overlook information needed at different stages of manipulation. We show that perceptual semantics can guide effective token selection without relying on internal attention when the selection scale adapts to the scene's evolving semantic structure. Based on this observation, we introduce TauPrune-VLA, a training-free method that controls the scale of the retained perceptual representation through a single perception temperature $\tau$. To adapt this scale as manipulation proceeds, TauPrune-VLA updates $\tau$ online using trends in perceptual semantics, with robot-state changes and action magnitude regulating the adjustment strength. While these semantics evolve over the course of manipulation, they remain relatively stable between consecutive frames. This continuity allows retention indices computed from the current observation to be applied at the next policy invocation, enabling token selection to run asynchronously with inference and reducing pruning overhead. Evaluated across diverse VLA models and simulation benchmarks, TauPrune-VLA achieves up to a $1.86\times$ inference speedup while maintaining near-baseline task success rates. We further validate its effectiveness on two real-world robotic platforms.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [CriticJEPA: World Models Are Better Critics Than Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32357)
*Xvyuan Liu,Jianjie Fang,Honglin Zhang,Zhaolu Wang,Xin Zhang,Wei Wu,Fengli Xu,Chen Gao,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CriticJEPA利用冻结的分层世界模型，从无奖励轨迹中学习目标条件流提议，并通过冻结推演选择候选，实现低计算量、可迁移的视觉规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的规划器在预测目标成本下降后仍持续优化，会利用预测误差导致执行不再改善；同时比较计划需要有效的相对评分，改进计划也需要可靠的更新方向。

Method: 提出CriticJEPA：从无奖励轨迹学习目标条件流提议，在每一层级用冻结的世界模型推演选择候选；前向选择构成完整规划器，短锚定更新在选择前精炼候选，且保持世界模型权重完全冻结。

Result: 在视觉导航和操作任务中，CriticJEPA以一小部分规划计算量提升成功率，并能在独立训练的模型、不同层级深度和未见任务间迁移共享设置。

Conclusion: 将目标导向候选与能区分其结果的预测相结合是有效规划的关键；冻结世界模型配合提议选择可在低计算下实现可迁移的目标导向规划。

Abstract: Visual world models enable goal-directed planning by predicting action outcomes. Gradient-based planners optimize actions to lower predicted goal cost, but continued optimization can exploit prediction errors after execution stops improving. Comparing plans requires informative relative scores; improving them also requires reliable update directions. Exact-state execution reveals that a frozen hierarchical world model ranks goal-conditioned proposals even when its rankings among outputs of prolonged gradient search are uninformative. Effective planning pairs goal-directed candidates with predictions that distinguish their outcomes. We introduce CriticJEPA, which learns goal-conditioned flow proposals from reward-free trajectories and uses frozen rollouts to select among them at every hierarchy level. Forward selection forms a complete planner; short anchored updates refine candidates before selection. Across visual navigation and manipulation, CriticJEPA increases success at a fraction of the planning compute, transferring shared settings across independently trained models, hierarchy depths, and unseen tasks while keeping every world-model weight fixed.

</details>


### [102] [ReJEPA: Bounded Predictive Regulation for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31910)
*Fangzhou Lin,Yuping Wang,Shuo Xing,Yang Chen,Yuwen Heng,Xin Li,Wenbin He,Yuhan Yao,Zhengzhong Tu,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReJEPA提出使用logcosh损失替代L2/L1损失来训练潜在世界模型，以平衡大误差下的有界梯度与零附近的平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准L2损失对大误差产生无界梯度，导致编码器和预测器过拟合噪声或瞬态帧并放大与正则化的梯度冲突；L1损失虽限制大梯度但零附近恒定梯度破坏规划所需的局部平滑性。

Method: 提出ReJEPA有界预测调节原则，通过逐元素logcosh损失实现：大误差时有界梯度，零附近二次平滑；作为即插即用训练替换应用于Temporal Straightening、LeWorldModel和V-JEPA 2.1，不改变架构或推理流程。

Result: logcosh持续优于L2、曲率匹配的二次控制、L1和Smooth-L1基线；增益随环境复杂度增长（从简单导航到接触丰富操作及NAVSIM真实自动驾驶）；探测和几何分析表明放松极端误差惩罚使表示更好地捕捉物理状态并对齐真实规划代价景观。

Conclusion: 强制最小化每个预测误差可能不必要且有害，采用有界且零附近平滑的logcosh损失能显著提升潜在世界模型的表示质量与规划性能。

Abstract: Latent world models learn representations by predicting future states in an abstract embedding space rather than reconstructing raw pixels. When training across multi-frame sequences, however, not all frames and spatial regions are equally informative or predictable. We argue that forcing a world model to minimize every prediction error may be unnecessary and harmful. Under standard $L_2$ loss, unbounded gradients force the encoder and predictor to overfit noisy or transient frames at the expense of core physical structure, while amplifying gradient conflict with representation regularizers. Conversely, $L_1$ loss bounds large gradients, but its constant gradient magnitude near zero disrupts the local smoothness required for planning. We resolve this tension with \textbf{ReJEPA}, a bounded predictive regulation principle that combines bounded gradients for large errors with smooth quadratic behavior near zero, instantiated simply via the element-wise $\log\cosh$ loss. As a drop-in training replacement across Temporal Straightening, LeWorldModel, and V-JEPA~2.1, $\log\cosh$ consistently outperforms $L_2$, a curvature-matched quadratic control, $L_1$, and Smooth-$L_1$ baselines without altering architectures or inference procedures. Notably, the gains grow with environmental complexity, scaling from simple navigation to contact-rich manipulation and real-world autonomous driving on NAVSIM. Probing and geometric analyses confirm that relaxing the penalty on extreme errors yields representations that better capture physical states and align with the true planning cost landscape.

</details>


### [103] [Curvature-Equilibrated Policy Optimization for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31680)
*Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EQUIP方法，通过平衡正负曲率构建有界局部度量来优化actor，在6个MuJoCo任务上样本效率优于SAC及两个二阶基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统一阶优化器未显式利用神经actor损失的局部曲率，限制策略优化效率。

Method: EQUIP利用轨迹信息构造搜索子空间，用投影Hessian构建有界局部度量实现曲率均衡，结合一阶损失匹配、梯度混合与曲率梯度选择，并给出几何性质、梯度对齐与幅值界及收敛条件；集成到SAC中不改变其actor损失、critic学习或回放机制。

Result: 在6个MuJoCo连续控制基准上，EQUIP-SAC平均样本效率高于SAC和两个二阶策略优化基线；消融实验验证曲率均衡与轨迹几何的收益。

Conclusion: 曲率均衡的梯度方法可有效提升actor优化与策略训练的样本效率，且可无缝集成到现有强化学习算法。

Abstract: Gradient-based actor optimization is central to deep reinforcement learning. However, conventional first-order optimizers do not explicitly exploit the local curvature of neural actor losses. We propose EQUIP (Curvature-Equilibrated Gradients for Policy Search), a novel approach that seeks to balance local contraction and expansion of the actor parameter space by equilibrating positive and negative curvature. EQUIP constructs a trajectory-informed search subspace and uses the projected Hessian of actor loss to build a bounded local metric for curvature equilibration. First-order loss matching preserves the approximated first-order decrease in actor loss. Meanwhile, gradient mixing and curvature-based gradient selection adjust the gradient applied for actor optimization. We establish the geometric properties of curvature equilibration, derive bounds on gradient alignment and magnitude, and identify conditions for improved actor loss reduction and convergence toward stationarity under direct gradient updates. We integrate EQUIP into Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) without modifying its actor loss, critic learning, or replay mechanism. Experiments on 6 MuJoCo continuous control benchmarks show that the resulting EQUIP-SAC algorithm achieves higher mean sample efficiency than SAC and two second-order policy optimization baselines. Ablation studies further highlight the benefits of curvature equilibration and trajectory-informed geometry for policy training.

</details>


### [104] [TeamFlowRL: Online Policy Optimization of Decentralized Flow Policies for Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32645)
*Guowei Zou,Beiwen Zhang,Haitao Wang,Hong Hong,Zhiquan Chen,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeamFlowRL是一种两阶段框架，先离线预训练多智能体流策略，再在线微调，通过优化完整去噪轨迹避免不可处理的动作似然，在多个仿真任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂协作任务需要多智能体协调行为，离线生成策略受限于固定数据集，在线强化学习可改进但流策略因动作似然难以计算而难以微调。

Method: 提出TeamFlowRL，两阶段框架：离线预训练多智能体流策略，在线微调；将团队策略优化形式化为对去中心化流策略完整去噪轨迹的优化，避免不可处理的动作似然；目标函数用共享团队优势加权每个智能体的轨迹比率，并分别裁剪每个比率，使局部策略更新与共同团队回报对齐。

Result: 在七个仿真组的51个配对配置中，TeamFlowRL改进了每个离线初始化，归一化分数平均提升47.3%；在6个双臂任务中，探索噪声下的平均成功率比高斯策略微调高33.1%。

Conclusion: TeamFlowRL有效结合离线预训练与在线微调，解决了流策略动作似然难以计算的问题，显著提升了多智能体协作任务的性能。

Abstract: Complex cooperative tasks require coordinated multi-agent behavior. Offline generative policies learn expressive cooperative behaviors from trajectories but remain limited by fixed datasets. Online reinforcement learning can improve them through environment interaction. However, fine-tuning flow policies is difficult because action likelihoods are hard to compute. We propose TeamFlowRL, a two-stage framework that pretrains multi-agent flow policies offline and fine-tunes them online. We formulate team policy optimization over the complete denoising trajectories of decentralized flow policies, avoiding intractable action likelihoods. The resulting objective weights each agent's trajectory ratio with a shared team advantage and clips each ratio separately, aligning local policy updates with the common team return. Across 51 paired configurations in seven simulation groups, TeamFlowRL improves every offline initialization, with a 47.3% average gain in normalized score. It also achieves 33.1% higher average success under exploration noise than Gaussian-policy fine-tuning across 6 bimanual tasks.

</details>


### [105] [Assessing Robustness to Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Deep Reinforcement Learning for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32496)
*Kim N. Nolle,Ivana Dusparic,Rhodri Cusack,Vinny Cahill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文比较了PPO、SAC和Drama三类强化学习算法在非平稳自动泊车环境中对抗遗忘的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在信息物理系统中广泛应用，智能体需适应环境变化以保持安全可靠；持续学习中的灾难性遗忘是关键问题，需探究不同类别RL算法的鲁棒性差异。

Method: 在非平稳自动泊车环境中，依次呈现四种不同停车位朝向的场景，评估无模型同策略算法PPO、无模型异策略算法SAC和基于模型算法Drama的鲁棒性。

Result: 异策略算法因使用经验回放比同策略算法更鲁棒；基于模型的算法相比无模型算法未表现出直接优势；实验还揭示了寻找鲁棒环境抽象和超参数过度敏感的额外挑战。

Conclusion: 异策略算法在非平稳环境中对抗遗忘更具鲁棒性，基于模型算法无直接优势，且鲁棒抽象和超参数敏感性是实际部署中的重要挑战。

Abstract: As the use of reinforcement learning (RL) becomes more widespread in cyber-physical systems, it becomes increasingly important to design RL agents that are able to adapt to changes in their environments in order to maintain safety and reliability. This paper addresses the question of whether different classes of RL algorithm are more robust against forgetting previously learned behaviours, a critical issue in continual learning, in non-stationary environments. We evaluate the robustness of representative examples of three important classes of RL algorithm in a non-stationary autonomous parking environment. These are Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), which is a model-free on-policy algorithm, Soft-Actor-Critic (SAC), which is a model-free off-policy algorithm, and Drama, which is a model-based algorithm. The agents must learn to successfully park in four different scenarios, which are presented successively, with parking spaces oriented at varying angles. We find off-policy algorithms appear to be more robust compared to on-policy algorithms due to their use of replay, while model-based algorithms do not appear to offer any direct advantage over model-free algorithms. These experiments also highlight the additional challenges of finding robust abstractions of the environment, and oversensitivity to hyperparameters.

</details>


### [106] [RetraQ: Manifold-Constrained Critic Guidance for Flow Policies in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32148)
*Xuanzhuo Liu,Wu Chen,Ruixiang Wu,Shuai Yuan,Chuankang Li,Tongxin Li,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RetraQ利用预训练flow的几何结构约束critic引导，提升策略性能并保持动作在数据支持区域内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的critic虽能拟合行为数据，但其动作梯度在数据支持区域之外约束不足，容易利用外推误差导致误导性引导。

Method: 提出RetraQ：用冻结flow的单次速度评估定义去噪映射，以该映射的雅可比过滤critic梯度，并应用映射本身校正更新后的动作，从而约束引导的方向与终点。

Result: 在受控实验中提升真实回报并保持动作接近数据区域；蒸馏目标比原始critic上升更稳定；LIBERO和OGBench离线到在线训练中最终成功率更高；真实插拔任务中测试时RetraQ将成功率从55.0%提升至76.7%，在线训练30分钟达98.3%、1小时达100%。

Conclusion: 通过预训练flow的几何约束critic引导，可有效避免外推误差，提升测试时引导、策略蒸馏与在线强化学习的稳定性和成功率。

Abstract: A learned critic can improve a pretrained flow-matching policy by steering sampled actions toward higher predicted value. However, even a critic that fits the behavior data well can provide misleading action gradients. Gradient components pointing away from the data-supported region are weakly constrained by behavior data and can lead guidance to exploit critic extrapolation errors. We introduce RetraQ (Retraction-based Q-guidance), which uses geometry encoded by the pretrained flow to constrain both the direction and the destination of critic guidance. A single velocity evaluation of the frozen flow defines a denoising map. RetraQ filters the critic gradient with the map's Jacobian and applies the map itself to correct the updated action. Our analysis characterizes when these operations approximate tangent filtering and retraction onto the behavior manifold. The same update guides actions at test time and provides targets for policy distillation and online reinforcement learning. In controlled studies, RetraQ improves true return while keeping refined actions close to the data-supported region, and its distillation targets remain more stable than those of raw critic ascent. In offline-to-online training on LIBERO and OGBench, RetraQ achieves higher mean final success than the evaluated critic-guidance baselines. On a real-world plug-insertion task, test-time RetraQ improves the success rate of a behavior-cloned $\pi_{0.5}$ policy from 55.0% to 76.7%. Online training further increases success to 98.3% in only 30 minutes and 100% within one hour. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [107] [ADE: A Zero-Training Unlearning Method for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31998)
*Sirui He,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练闭式遗忘算法ADE，仅用缓存预训练矩即可从潜在世界模型中精确剔除后门触发器与过期物理转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的潜在世界模型模拟器可能残留后门触发器或过时物理状态下的陈旧转移，而现有遗忘方法在自回归物理场景中失效：梯度更新扭曲潜在流形并累积推演漂移，简单输出归零会抑制触发时的有效控制响应，单步删除无法清除循环隐状态中的污染。

Method: ADE利用缓存预训练矩通过单遍矩阵降秩更新恢复保留协方差（无需原始保留数据），将闭式编辑级联应用于输入瓶颈、隐状态和下游头；结合正交切除、白化循环阻尼与保均值仿射校准，中和流形外后门并对齐流形内偏移而不导致方差塌缩。

Result: 在三个MuJoCo基准的四种场景下，ADE在DreamerV1上优于七个调优基线，消除HalfCheetah与Hopper速度翻转触发器比微调快4.4倍，并零样本迁移至DreamerV3。

Conclusion: ADE为潜在世界模型提供了一种确定性、零训练、闭式的遗忘方案，能有效清除自回归物理中的后门与陈旧转移，且具备跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Latent world models learn compact recurrent dynamics from offline trajectories, yet deployed simulators can retain backdoor triggers or stale transitions from outdated physical regimes. Existing unlearning methods fail in autoregressive physics: gradient updates distort latent manifolds and compound rollout drift, naive output zeroing suppresses valid control responses on trigger onset, and single-step deletion leaves corruption inside recurrent hidden states. We propose Analytical Dynamics Excision (ADE), a deterministic, zero-training closed-form unlearning algorithm for latent world models. Using only cached pretraining moments, ADE recovers retain covariances via single-pass matrix downdating without raw retain data and cascades closed-form edits across the input bottleneck, hidden states, and downstream heads. Pairing orthogonal excision with whitened recurrent damping and mean-preserving affine calibration neutralizes out-of-manifold backdoors and realigns in-manifold shifts without variance collapse. Across four regimes on three MuJoCo benchmarks, ADE outperforms seven tuned baselines on DreamerV1, eliminating HalfCheetah and Hopper velocity-flip triggers $4.4\times$ faster than fine-tuning, and transfers zero-shot to DreamerV3.

</details>


### [108] [Two-Stage Post-Training for Flow-Based Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31850)
*Jiaming Zhang,Yao Lyu,Haoning Wang,Yongjun Chen,Liping Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段后训练框架，基于条件流匹配的速度回归构建蒸馏目标，先通过多教师在线策略蒸馏整合专家能力，再结合强化学习与噪声加权蒸馏（仅对失败轨迹）超越教师表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练赋予具身模型通用能力，但可靠任务执行仍需后训练；多任务下SFT存在训练-部署分布不匹配，RL受稀疏奖励和任务间学习进度不均限制，MOPD虽能整合专家能力但难以超越教师。

Method: 构建基于条件流匹配速度回归表述的蒸馏目标；阶段1用多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）高效整合专家能力；阶段2将RL与教师蒸馏结合，按采样噪声对速度误差加权，且蒸馏仅限失败轨迹，以稠密引导补充稀疏奖励而不直接约束成功行为。

Result: 在多模型和多基准实验中，该框架优于联合SFT，并超过对应教师的聚合成功率；样本效率比较显示，以更少环境交互取得比直接RL更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 该两阶段后训练框架能有效整合并超越多教师能力，提升样本效率与任务成功率，为流式具身模型提供可靠后训练方案。

Abstract: Large-scale pretraining provides embodied models with general capabilities, but reliable task execution still requires post training. In multitask settings, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) faces distribution mismatch between training and deployment, whereas reinforcement learning (RL) is limited by sparse rewards and uneven learning progress across tasks. Multi-teacher on policy distillation (MOPD) consolidates expert capabilities through dense supervision on the model's own trajectories, but achieving performance beyond the teachers remains challenging. We propose a two-stage post training framework for flow-based embodied models, constructing distillation objectives from the velocity regression formulation of conditional flow matching. Stage 1 efficiently consolidates expert capabilities through MOPD. Stage 2 combines RL with teacher distillation that weights velocity errors according to the sampling noise, where distillation is restricted to failed trajectories to supplement sparse rewards with dense guidance without directly constraining successful behaviors. Experiments across multiple models and benchmarks show that the framework outperforms joint SFT and exceeds the corresponding teachers' aggregate success rates. Sample efficiency comparisons show higher success rates than direct RL with fewer environment interactions.

</details>


### [109] [PhysRF-WorldJEPA: Transforming Heterogeneous Large Language Models into Manifold-Decoupled Millimeter-Wave Radar World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32113)
*Yicheng Huang,Feng Ke,Linhan Chen,Ziyi Jiang,Xiu Yin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhysRF-WorldJEPA解耦连续电磁状态预测与跨模态语言对齐，在毫米波雷达-LLM任务中显著提升检索与物理状态预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准指令微调将连续雷达流视为静态文本令牌，导致全局雷达-文本检索近乎随机且传感器中断时物理漂移严重；而直接耦合成对对比损失虽改善检索，却注入O(B^2)排斥梯度破坏连续坐标流形。

Method: 提出流形解耦的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），分离连续电磁状态预测与跨模态语言对齐：采用1.0x时钟锚与并行双尺度RF编码器，驱动约3% LoRA的LLM，用两步状态空间EMA-JEPA（去噪->推演）和样本轴Batch-VICReg预测+3.0秒物理状态；再用停止梯度的对齐头将冻结提示状态映射到文本超球面并做假阴性掩码。

Result: 在4,541窗口三域（77/60.25/60 GHz）对10个基线，冻结物理主干仅训练解耦头使R->T R@10从5.95%升至15.01%，MedRank从107.0降至60.0，物理读出保持：3.37度（R^2=+0.961）中断+3.0秒躯干倾斜、1.89米（R^2=+0.679；RF前端1.19米/+0.863）杂乱无人机高度、2.04米（R^2=+0.461）穿墙NLOS距离、0.9976虚拟RIS不变性、100%跌倒召回；五个0.5B-1.8B LLM家族15个零开销模型中，World-JEPA在10/10次运行提升检索与75%丢失补全，中断倾斜在4/5（Span-Euc）、2/5（Scatter）、5/5（每家族最佳）优于SFT。

Conclusion: 解耦物理状态预测与语言对齐可避免对比损失对连续流形的破坏，在保持物理读出精度的同时大幅提升雷达-文本检索与中断鲁棒性，且跨LLM家族泛化有效。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar enables privacy-preserving, all-weather physical scene understanding across darkness, smoke, and walls. However, standard instruction tuning treats continuous radar streams as static text-like tokens, causing near-random global radar-to-text retrieval and severe physical drift under sensor blackouts. Conversely, coupling pairwise contrastive losses (e.g., SigLIP) directly into the shared backbone improves retrieval but injects $O(B^2)$ repulsive gradients that fracture continuous coordinate manifolds ($R^2 < 0$). We present PhysRF-WorldJEPA, a manifold-decoupled Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) that separates continuous electromagnetic state prediction from cross-modal language alignment. An unattenuated 1.0x clock anchor and parallel dual-scale RF encoder feed a lean ~3% LoRA LLM trained with two-step state-space EMA-JEPA (Denoise -> Rollout) and sample-axis Batch-VICReg to predict +3.0 s physical states; a stop-gradient alignment head then maps frozen prompt states onto the text hypersphere with false-negative masking. On a 4,541-window tri-domain suite (77/60.25/60 GHz) against 10 baselines, freezing the physical backbone and training only the decoupled head lifts R->T R@10 from 5.95% to 15.01% and improves MedRank from 107.0 to 60.0 while preserving identical physical readout: 3.37 deg ($R^2=+0.961$) blackout +3.0 s torso tilt, 1.89 m ($R^2=+0.679$; 1.19 m/+0.863 at the RF front-end) cluttered UAV altitude, 2.04 m ($R^2=+0.461$) behind-wall NLOS range, 0.9976 virtual-RIS invariance, and 100% fall recall. Across five 0.5B-1.8B LLM families (15 zero-overhead models), World-JEPA improves retrieval and 75%-drop completion in 10/10 runs and blackout tilt in 4/5 (Span-Euc), 2/5 (Scatter), and 5/5 (per-family best) over SFT.

</details>


### [110] [One Phase Is Enough: Sim-to-Real Transfer with Direct Visual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31588)
*John Marangola,Antonia Bronars,Younghyo Park,Stephen Andrews,Kushal Kedia,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG
