TL;DR: DRIFT 提出一种单阶段视觉强化学习配方：冻结预训练视觉编码器，仅训练控制策略，从而在真实机器人操作任务中超越两阶段蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段方法（先用特权状态训练教师策略，再通过模仿蒸馏为视觉学生策略）中学生策略性能明显低于教师；而直接用 RL 训练视觉策略虽可避免此问题，但从零学习任务相关视觉表征样本效率低，导致单阶段视觉 RL 难以实施。

Method: DRIFT 使用测试时可获得的图像观测，采用冻结的预训练基础视觉编码器，使 RL 只需在已有视觉表征上学习控制；冻结编码器避免对视觉骨干的反向传播开销，其预训练表征支持从快速光栅化仿真渲染迁移到真实世界。

Result: 在匹配架构、观测和计算预算下，DRIFT 优于两阶段蒸馏，在三个操作任务上平均真实世界成功率相对提升 58.8%；受控仿真评估显示其相对优势从分布内约 9% 增长到分布外偏移下超过 61%。

Conclusion: 更强的泛化能力有助于实现更优的真实世界迁移，冻结预训练视觉编码器的单阶段视觉 RL 是高效且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) has enabled impressive robotic behaviors, yet deploying policies directly from vision remains challenging. A common approach first trains a teacher policy with RL using privileged state observations, then distills its behavior into a vision-based student via imitation. However, the resulting student can substantially underperform its teacher. Directly training visual policies with RL offers a promising alternative, but learning task-relevant visual representations from scratch can be sample-inefficient, making one-phase visual RL intractable. We propose DRIFT, a simple recipe for efficient one-phase visual RL using image observations available at test time. Our key insight is to use frozen, pretrained foundation vision encoders, allowing RL to focus on learning control from existing visual representations. Freezing the encoder avoids costly backpropagation through the vision backbone, while its pretrained representations enable real-world transfer from fast, rasterized simulation rendering. Under matched architectures, observations, and compute budgets, DRIFT outperforms two-phase distillation and achieves a 58.8% relative improvement in average real-world success rate across three manipulation tasks. Controlled simulation evaluations demonstrate that DRIFT's relative advantage grows from roughly 9% in-distribution to over 61% under out-of-distribution shifts, suggesting that stronger generalization contributes to superior real-world transfer. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [111] [Near-Zero Forgetting Is Not Enough: Out-of-Distribution Robustness in Continual Learning of Pretrained VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31511)
*Adrià Ruiz,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Tinne Tuytelaars,Sergi Masip*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 预训练VLA模型在经验回放的持续学习中域内遗忘接近零，但对域偏移的鲁棒性尚未被探索；本研究发现OOD性能会迅速崩溃，并伴随表征漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅证明VLA在特定持续学习设置下域内遗忘近乎为零，但未考察已学任务对域偏移的鲁棒性，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus基准上，用经验回放微调Pi0模型，分别在域内和OOD数据下评估持续学习表现，并通过表征分析（漂移定位、线性探针）探究OOD性能下降的机制。

Result: 域内设置下灾难性遗忘接近零，但OOD性能在前序任务上迅速崩溃；OOD能力退化在不同域偏移间呈现共享表征模式，并在两个较简单套件的行为层面可见；表征漂移集中于多模态特征和动作特征；线性探针显示OOD特征仍保留任务相关信息但似乎不可读，与表征错位一致。

Conclusion: 即使域内稳定性保持完好，持续学习中仍可能发生严重的OOD遗忘，并伴随表征漂移，说明VLA持续学习的鲁棒性评估不能仅依赖域内指标。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit near-zero forgetting in specific continual learning settings with experience replay. However, robustness to domain shift of learned tasks remains unexplored. In this work, we study the robustness of Pi0 fine-tuned with experience replay under out-of-distribution (OOD) data using the LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. While the in-domain (ID) setup achieves near-zero catastrophic forgetting, OOD performance on previous tasks quickly collapses. Specifically, we show that during the continual learning process, the OOD capabilities of the model degrade, following a shared representational pattern across domain shifts, which is also visible behaviorally in the two simpler suites. Representational analysis shows that drift concentrates in the multimodal and action features. Linear probes indicate that OOD features still retain task-relevant information, but appear unreadable, consistent with representation misalignment. These findings suggest that even when ID stability remains intact, severe OOD forgetting can still occur, accompanied by representational drift during continual learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Embodied AI Fails the Mirror Test: How a Simple Mirror Breaks Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33010)
*Xuanchen Liu,Hejun Zhang,Boxuan Dong,Ying Wu,Heshuai Li,Wen Gao,Yonghong Tian,Weihao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoboDojo-Mirror基准，发现多种机器人操作策略在含镜面反射场景中成功率显著下降，且失败主要源于对反射物体的误定位而非碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受动物镜像自我认知测试启发，探究机器人策略能否在含自身及物体反射的场景中可靠操作物理对象。

Method: 基于RoboDojo构建基准，保持任务目标、成功标准和策略检查点不变，对比无镜基线、单镜、双镜和镜面桌面条件；测试五类策略（VLA模型、World Action Models、智能体策略），并与几何匹配的非反射面板对照，结合轨迹与接触分析及真机实验。

Result: 所有镜面条件均降低聚合成功率，三条件平均下降10.8至56.7个百分点，镜面桌面达62.5个百分点；50-88%的镜面特有失败无机器人-面板接触记录；分析显示存在对反射物体的幻影瞄准和真实物体空间误定位；π0.5与FastWAM真机实验同样成功率下降。

Conclusion: 多样化机器人策略对反射环境存在共同脆弱性，需开展反射感知的具身智能评估。

Abstract: Can a simple mirror expose the limitations of today's robotic manipulation policies? Inspired by the mirror self-recognition test in animal cognition, we investigate whether robot policies can reliably manipulate physical objects in scenes containing their reflections. We introduce RoboDojo-Mirror, a benchmark built on RoboDojo that preserves task objectives, success criteria, and policy checkpoints while comparing a mirror-free baseline with single-mirror, dual-mirror, and mirrored-tabletop conditions. Across five policies spanning Vision-Language-Action models, World Action Models, and agentic policies, every mirror condition reduces aggregate success rates: averaged over the three conditions, the drop ranges from 10.8 to 56.7 percentage points across policies, and it reaches 62.5 points on the mirrored tabletop. Because a mirror is also a physical obstacle, we compare it against geometrically matched non-reflective panels; 50--88\% of mirror-specific failures occur without recorded robot--panel contact, so direct collisions alone do not explain the degradation. Trajectory and contact analyses instead reveal recurring behaviors consistent with phantom targeting of reflected objects and spatial mislocalization of real ones. Targeted diagnostics further characterize grasping near reflections, policy-dependent pushing progress, and physical-target interaction under main-camera occlusion. Real-robot experiments with $\pi_{0.5}$ and FastWAM also show lower overall success rates under the upright-mirror and mirrored-tabletop conditions. These findings reveal a shared vulnerability to reflective environments across diverse robot policies and motivate reflection-aware evaluation of embodied intelligence. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [113] [Lost on Campus: Evaluating Embodied Spatial Reasoning of Vision-Language Models in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33145)
*Zehan Zheng,Yanyuan Chen,Deming Li,Yutao Tang,Jianwen Xie,Alan Yuille,Jieneng Chen,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Lost on Campus基准，基于3D高斯泼溅重建的大规模真实户外校园3D环境，用统一的推理-动作框架评测视觉语言模型的具身空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多在静态图像或合成室内环境中评估空间推理，语义捷径即可完成任务，难以忠实反映真实场景下的具身空间推理能力。

Method: 利用3D高斯泼溅重建真实户外3D环境，构建融合诊断性问答（孤立推理）与闭环交互导航（多模态指令下的主动推理）的统一评测框架，并从动作定位、空间预见、度量感知、目标导向规划、自我-异我中心定位、时空一致性六个能力维度进行细粒度诊断。

Result: 实验表明：(i) 真实户外环境比室内环境对空间推理要求显著更高，现有模型表现较差；(ii) VLM在细粒度视觉对齐上仍有困难，空间预见与自我-异我中心定位是主要瓶颈；(iii) 提升多模态交互与长时程空间推理对推进具身智能至关重要。

Conclusion: 忠实评估具身空间推理需要将感知、推理与动作紧耦合于真实连续环境中的基准。

Abstract: Embodied Spatial Reasoning (ESR) is central to deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in real-world embodied tasks, yet remains inadequately evaluated. Existing benchmarks primarily assess spatial reasoning in static images or synthetic indoor environments, where semantic shortcuts often suffice, making it difficult to faithfully evaluate spatial reasoning capabilities in the wild. In light of this, we present Lost on Campus, a benchmark for evaluating ESR in large-scale real-world outdoor 3D environments reconstructed by 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our benchmark introduces a unified reasoning-action evaluation framework that seamlessly integrates diagnostic QA for isolated reasoning with closed-loop interactive navigation for active reasoning under multimodal instructions. To enable fine-grained diagnosis, we evaluate six capability aspects of ESR: action grounding, spatial foresight, metric awareness, goal-directed planning, ego-allocentric localization, and spatio-temporal consistency. Extensive experiments reveal that: (i) Compared to indoor settings, real-world outdoor environments impose substantially higher demands on spatial reasoning, where existing models exhibit poor performance; (ii) VLMs still struggle with fine-grained visual alignment, with failures in spatial foresight and ego-allocentric localization emerging as primary bottlenecks; (iii) Improving multimodal interaction capabilities and long-term spatial reasoning is crucial for advancing embodied intelligence. These findings highlight that faithful evaluation of ESR demands benchmarks tightly coupling perception, reasoning and action within realistic and continuous environments.

</details>


### [114] [Physicl Scenes: An Artist-created 3D Dataset for Learning Perception and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33137)
*Yueh-Cheng Liu,Snehal Jauhri,Matthias Nießner,Angela Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 介绍了Physicl Scenes数据集：包含6,499个高质量3D场景，涵盖室内外环境，具备几何、物理材质和对象/部件级语义标注，用于视觉与具身学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据集在几何、材质和语义标注方面不够丰富，难以同时支持逆渲染、3D重建、场景理解和移动操作等多种任务，需要高质量、多标注的3D场景数据。

Method: 构建并策划6,499个艺术家创建的3D场景，包含详细几何、基于物理的材质、精心布置的环境以及对象级和部件级语义标注；在逆渲染、3D重建、场景理解和移动操作任务上训练先进方法，并引入移动操作基准，评估视觉-语言-动作模型在真实布局场景中的物理抓取放置，并利用部件标注评估抓取位置。

Result: 在多个任务上，使用该场景训练先进方法相比其他合成数据集提升了性能；移动操作基准能够评估VLA模型在多样3D场景中的抓取放置表现并评分抓取位置。

Conclusion: Physicl Scenes为视觉和具身AI研究提供了高质量、多任务联合监督的3D场景数据集，将公开释放以促进相关研究。

Abstract: We present Physicl Scenes, a high-quality artist-created and curated dataset of 6,499 3D scenes spanning household rooms, public interiors, and outdoor spaces. The dataset combines detailed geometry, physically based materials, and carefully composed environments with object- and part-level semantic annotations, providing rich, joint supervision for visual and embodied learning. We demonstrate its value as training data across inverse rendering, 3D reconstruction, scene understanding, and mobile manipulation. Across these tasks, training state-of-the-art methods with our scenes improves performance over alternative synthetic datasets. In addition, we introduce a mobile manipulation benchmark, evaluating vision-language-action models performing physical pick and place in diverse 3D scenes with realistic layouts, and using our part annotations to also score where each object is grasped. Our dataset will be released publicly to facilitate research in visual and embodied AI.

</details>


### [115] [Training Lightweight Gaze Models without Target Gaze Annotation: What Makes a Good Teacher?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33014)
*Scott J. Lewis,Di Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在注视目标估计任务中系统性比较了11种监督条件，发现不完美的模型生成标签可替代人工标注，且教师质量可在训练学生前通过验证集标签误差预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在交互中需要轻量、准确的注视目标估计模型；模型生成标签可规模化降低标注成本，但缺乏判断“何为好教师”以及能否在训练学生前预判教师质量的原则。

Method: 在注视目标估计任务中进行受控比较：固定学生模型、训练协议和36.1万训练样本，仅改变注视点标签来源，共11种监督条件；覆盖3个视频数据集、2种学生规模、3个随机种子；所有学生均以人工标注评估。

Result: 第一，Gaze-LLE虽未在目标数据集注视标注上训练，其蒸馏学生在ViT-Tiny上与人工标注训练学生相当，在ViT-Small上显著更准（平均距离误差0.187对0.196），ViT-Tiny学生CPU推理比教师快32倍。第二，InternVL3和Qwen3-VL在提示中给出他人人脸中心时分别有93.4%和81.1%的测试样本直接返回该坐标，复制坐标基线即可匹配InternVL3的标签与学生表现；去掉该坐标后两者远落后于专用模型。第三，验证集标签误差可对学生测试误差排序（Spearman ρ=0.91），仅用10个标注片段在95%的抽样中识别出两个专用教师。

Conclusion: 研究给出评估监督信号、揭示捷径行为和为轻量模型选择教师的实用原则：模型生成标签可替代人工标注，表面能力可能掩盖不可靠监督，且教师质量可在训练学生前通过验证标签误差预判。

Abstract: Model-generated labels offer a scalable route to training lightweight vision models, but what makes a model a good teacher, and can we tell before training a student? We study this in gaze target estimation, where interactive robots need models that are both accurate and efficient. In a controlled comparison of eleven supervision conditions, we vary only the source of gaze-point labels, keeping the student, training protocol and 361k training samples fixed, across three video datasets, two student sizes and three seeds; every student is evaluated against human annotation. Three findings emerge. First, imperfect model-generated labels can replace human annotation: Gaze-LLE, never trained on the target datasets' gaze annotation, trains students that match annotation-trained ones on ViT-Tiny and are significantly more accurate on ViT-Small (average distance error 0.187 vs 0.196), and the ViT-Tiny student runs 32$\times$ faster than its teacher on a laptop CPU. Second, apparent competence can conceal unreliable supervision: given another person's face centre in the prompt, InternVL3 and Qwen3-VL return it on 93.4% and 81.1% of test samples, and a copy-the-coordinate baseline matches InternVL3's labels and students. Without that coordinate, both models fall far behind the specialist. Third, good teachers can be identified before training: validation label error ranks student test error (Spearman's $\rho = 0.91$), and ten annotated clips single out the two specialist teachers in 95% of draws. These findings give practical principles for assessing supervision, exposing shortcuts and selecting teachers for lightweight models.

</details>


### [116] [RFW-W: Scaling Physical Asset Universes for Generative Embodied Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33013)
*Jiawei Weng,Rui Zhang,Saining Zhang,Changjian Pan,Yuanbo Lyu,Jiarui Guo,Yongzhi Li,Guolin Liu,Jialu Liu,Jiankun Wang,Yi Yang,Peishuo Li,Zhiheng Li,Jiaying Wan,Junhao Chen,Weiqing Xiao,Jingrun Du,Wenyi Li,Weiyi Lin,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Lin Qu,Zongzheng Zhang,Hao Zhao,Chaojian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RFW-W统一物理资产库，整合刚体、铰接体和可变形体资产，用于任务条件化的具身场景合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展具身仿真需要多样化且具有可执行物理表示的物体，现有资产来源分散且缺乏统一接口。

Method: 构建RFW-W，包含RFW-R（刚体，101万条记录中52.4万条、1212类）、RFW-A（铰接体，40.2万条、531类）、RFW-S（可变形体，8.3万条、485类）；RFW-R采用类别引导生成与碰撞几何、组合物理属性及交互标注，RFW-A采用可复用程序化生成器显式建模部件与关节，RFW-S采用结构感知仿真表示、校准与独立测试，并通过统一接口按资产类型保留物理需求以支持检索与场景装配。

Result: RFW-R在资产覆盖和场景满足能力上优于现有数据集；在五个RoboTwin任务、每任务每资产源100条演示下，RFW-R训练的策略宏成功率在干净环境为71.2%、随机化为41.8%，分别超过最强基线15.8和10.6个百分点。

Conclusion: RFW-W为生成物理 grounded、任务条件化的具身世界提供了可扩展的统一资产基础。

Abstract: Scaling embodied simulation requires diverse objects with executable physical representations. We present RFW-W, a physical asset universe connecting rigid, articulated, and deformable asset sources to task-conditioned scene composition. Its reported inventories comprise 1,010,386 asset records: 524,611 rigid assets in 1,212 categories (RFW-R), 402,410 articulated assets in 531 categories (RFW-A), and 83,365 jointly verified deformables in 485 categories (RFW-S). These taxonomies contribute 2,228 dataset--category entries. RFW-R couples category-guided generation with collision geometry, composed physical properties, and interaction annotations; RFW-A uses reusable agent-authored procedural generators with explicit part and joint structure; RFW-S uses structure-aware simulation representations, calibration, and independent testing. A common interface preserves physical requirements specific to each asset type for retrieval and scene assembly. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that RFW-R provides broader asset coverage and stronger scene-fulfillment capabilities than existing datasets. Across five RoboTwin tasks, using 100 demonstrations per task for each asset source, policies trained on RFW-R achieve macro success rates of 71.2% in clean settings and 41.8% under randomization, exceeding the strongest baseline by 15.8 and 10.6 percentage points, respectively. RFW-W provides a scalable and unified asset foundation for generating physically grounded, task-conditioned embodied worlds.

</details>


### [117] [MESSI: Multi-Expert Soccer Skill Integration for Physics-Based Character Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33011)
*Xiaogang Peng,Jiaqi Zou,Lei Zhong,Sirui Xu,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MESSI通过共享自适应实现多足球技能平滑切换，在技能切换时激活低秩适配器处理陌生进入状态，并通过退出机制恢复原策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理驱动机器人足球角色需要执行多种运动技能并平滑过渡，但单独表现良好的技能在连续执行时可能因前序行为导致角色和球处于陌生状态而失败。

Method: MESSI先利用运动先验和任务奖励训练八个任务专家，蒸馏为统一技能条件Transformer；在冻结的学生模型上通过在线强化学习训练共享上下文条件低秩适配器，技能切换时激活以帮助目标技能适应陌生进入状态，学习退出机制决定何时交还控制权；原生技能执行时禁用适配器以保留原策略。

Result: 在单项技能、技能切换和连续技能链实验中显著优于直接切换，带球到射门成功率从19.8%提升至61.8%。

Conclusion: MESSI框架通过共享适配与退出机制有效实现多足球技能平滑过渡，同时保持原生技能策略不变，验证了技能切换中共享自适应学习的有效性。

Abstract: Physics-based soccer characters must perform diverse motor skills and transition smoothly between them, such as dribbling into a shot. However, skills that perform well in isolation may fail when executed sequentially, as preceding behaviors can leave the character and ball in unfamiliar states. We present MESSI (Multi-Expert Soccer Skill Integration), a framework that enables multiple soccer skill transitions through shared adaptation while preserving the original skill policy outside transitions. MESSI first trains eight task-competent experts using motion priors and task rewards, then distills them into a unified, skill-conditioned Transformer. Rather than retraining individual experts for different transitions, we train a shared, context-conditioned low-rank adapter on the frozen student through online reinforcement learning. Activated upon skill switching, the adapter helps the target skill handle unfamiliar entry states, while a learned exit mechanism determines when to return control to the original student. By disabling the adapter during native skill execution, MESSI preserves the original student policy by construction. Experiments across individual skills, skill transitions, and continuous skill chains demonstrate substantial improvements over direct switching, including an increase in dribbling-to-shooting success from 19.8% to 61.8%.

</details>


### [118] [Benchmarking Evidence Sufficiency in Vision-Language Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32964)
*Nhat Ha Nguyen,Sudipta Chowdhury,Lokesh Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DriveAnswer基准，用匹配的强/弱证据配置联合评估驾驶VLM的答案正确性、证据充分性与定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将定位与可回答性分开测量，难以区分有充分证据支持的预测与仅定位到相关但不充分证据的预测。

Method: 构建包含7类推理、共20,151个问题的基准，每对问题保持场景事实与语义答案不变，仅改变观测是否支持回答，弱证据下应弃答；在六类评估上测试零样本基线并做受控适配。

Result: 最强零样本基线强证据准确率67.31%，但仅21.55%的匹配问题同时满足答-弃行为与完整定位；受控适配后Gemma-4-12B联合能力达63.29%，约为零样本的三倍，Qwen3-VL-8B也表现类似提升。

Conclusion: 语义正确性、证据充分性与定位是不同能力，应联合评估。

Abstract: Reliable driving VLMs must do more than answer correctly or localize relevant scene actors: they must determine whether the available visual evidence is sufficient to support an answer. Existing evaluations largely treat grounding and answerability as separate measurements, making it difficult to distinguish well-supported predictions from those localized to relevant but insufficient evidence. We introduce DriveAnswer, a benchmark of 20,151 questions across seven reasoning families, that operationalizes this distinction using matched strong- and weak-evidence configurations. Each pair preserves the underlying scene fact and semantic answer while changing whether the observations support answering, making abstention the appropriate response under weak evidence. On the six-family evaluation, the strongest zero-shot baseline reaches 67.31% strong-evidence accuracy, yet only 21.55% of matched questions satisfy both answer-abstain behavior and complete grounding. With controlled adaptation, Gemma-4-12B reaches 63.29% on this joint capability, nearly tripling its zero-shot performance. Qwen3-VL-8B exhibits the same broad improvement in evidence-sensitive answering and grounding. These results show that semantic correctness, evidence sufficiency, and grounding are distinct capabilities that should be evaluated jointly.

</details>


### [119] [HARI4D: Hand-Articulation Aware 4D Reconstruction of Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32932)
*Semin Kim,Xianghui Xie,Chuqiao Li,Jongyoo Kim,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HARI4D，一种从单目 RGB 视频重建时序一致、关节感知的 4D 人-物交互方法，显著降低物体误差并提升手部接触精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身重建工作忽略手部，导致手穿透物体或悬浮于物体外；从单目 RGB 视频进行 4D 重建（尤其涉及关节手）具有挑战性，而该能力对游戏、VR 和机器人学习很重要。

Method: 引入历史感知的物体跟踪器提供稳定跟踪；基于跟踪结果采用由粗到细的推理：ReachNet 在接触阶段将全身与手臂对齐到物体，TouchNet 依据时序上下文和局部物体几何细化手部关节。

Result: 在训练未见的 ARCTIC 数据集上，相比先前方法物体误差降低 56%，手部接触 F1 从 0.866 提升至 0.921；可恢复准确全身-物体交互与一致手部关节，并适用于在线视频。

Conclusion: HARI4D 实现了空间与时序一致、包含关节手部的 4D 人-物交互重建，性能优于现有方法，代码与预训练模型将公开。

Abstract: Reasoning about human-object interaction from RGB cameras is important for gaming, VR environments, and robot learning. Yet 4D reconstruction from a monocular RGB camera is challenging, especially when articulated hands are involved. Prior full-body works neglect hands during reconstruction, causing them to penetrate into, or float from the object. We present HARI4D, a hand-articulation-aware method for reconstructing spatially and temporally consistent 4D human-object interaction from monocular RGB video. We introduce a history-aware object tracker that provides robust and stable tracking. Based on these tracks, we improve the interaction configuration through coarse-to-fine reasoning that follows the order of object manipulation: ReachNet first brings the full body and arms to the object during contact, and TouchNet then finalizes hand articulation according to temporal context and local object geometry. On ARCTIC, unseen in training, our method reduces the object error by 56% and improves hand contact F1 from 0.866 to 0.921 over prior art. Our method recovers accurate full body-object interaction with coherent hand articulations and applies to online videos. Our code and pretrained models will be released.

</details>


### [120] [Motion Conditioned World Modeling for Embodied Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32900)
*Jiahao Zhang,Xuan Yao,Junyu Gao,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出运动条件世界模型，将预测的目标运动分布与机器人自运动融入潜在状态转移，通过几何-进度模块联合建模路径与步长，在三个具身跟踪基准上以0.5B轻量骨干提升跟踪性能与跨环境泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉跟踪中目标运动不确定且机器人视角变化共同影响观测，使未来状态预测困难。

Method: 构建运动条件世界模型：几何-进度模块以共享运动模式与路径条件进度分布联合建模几何路径和单步距离；动作诱导坐标变换将预测路径表达于下一机器人坐标系；未来视觉特征重建经条件转移分支监督运动预测器；以运动似然、特征重建和专家动作监督联合优化。

Result: 在三个具身跟踪基准上，使用0.5B轻量骨干取得更好的跟踪性能与跨环境泛化。

Conclusion: 显式运动表示与预测观测相链接，使跟踪策略能结合视觉上下文与预测运动，验证了运动条件世界建模的有效性。

Abstract: World models support embodied decision-making by predicting future environment states. In embodied visual tracking, uncertain target motion and robot-induced viewpoint changes jointly affect visual observations, making future-state prediction challenging. We propose Motion-Conditioned World Modeling, which incorporates predicted target-motion distributions and robot ego-motion into latent state transitions. A Geometry--Progress module jointly models geometric paths and single-step travel distances through shared motion modes and path-conditioned progress distributions. This formulation captures uncertainty in route selection and motion progress while preserving their dependence. Action-induced coordinate transformations express predicted paths in the next robot frame, providing geometrically grounded conditions for future visual-state prediction. Future visual feature reconstruction supervises the motion predictor through the conditioned transition branch, linking explicit motion representations to subsequent observations. The model is jointly optimized with motion likelihood, feature reconstruction, and expert-action supervision, enabling the tracking policy to combine visual context with predictive motion this http URL on three embodied tracking benchmarks demonstrate improved tracking performance and cross-environment generalization with a lightweight $0.5$B backbone.

</details>


### [121] [Biomechanically Grounded Prediction of Selective Motor Control and Clinical Outcomes from Infant Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32890)
*Divya Joshi,Ruize Yang,Colleen Peyton,R. James Cotton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BB3D是一种婴儿适配的生物力学流程，可从单摄像头视频估计关节角度轨迹，并借此预测BabyOSCAR评分，实现可扩展的婴儿选择性运动控制评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期识别脑瘫依赖需训练评分员且耗时的主观观察评估（如GMA、BabyOSCAR）；现有自动化方法基于二维关键点的手工特征，仅描述图像平面运动而非单关节的三维行为。

Method: 提出BB3D生物力学流程，从单摄像头视频估计关节角度轨迹；以17名婴儿的多视角无标记动作捕捉为参考评估精度；用1000+单摄像头记录的BB3D关节角轨迹预训练时空Transformer，并在350名婴儿的550段视频上微调，逐项预测BabyOSCAR。

Result: 关节中心中位Procrustes对齐误差17.5mm，标记点20.4mm；各BabyOSCAR条目平均组内AUC 0.79，总分Spearman相关0.86；未用诊断训练时预测总分区分脑瘫AUC 0.86，高于复现的二维特征分类器（0.67），低于专家评分员总分（0.97）；预测分数还携带功能能力与身体分布信息，但弱于专家评分。

Conclusion: 基于生物力学的单目视频表征可支持可扩展、关节层面的婴儿选择性运动控制评估。

Abstract: Early identification of cerebral palsy (CP) relies on observational assessments of spontaneous infant movement, such as the General Movement Assessment (GMA) and the Baby Observational Selective Control AppRaisal (BabyOSCAR), which require trained raters and substantial scoring time. Existing automated approaches derive hand-engineered features from two-dimensional keypoints, which describe image-plane motion rather than the three-dimensional behavior of individual joints. We present Baby Biomechanical 3D Body (BB3D), an infant-adapted, biomechanical pipeline that estimates joint-angle trajectories from single-camera video. Against a multi-view markerless motion capture reference in 17 infants, BB3D's median Procrustes-aligned error was 17.5~mm for joint centers and 20.4~mm for marker sites. A spatio-temporal transformer, pretrained on BB3D joint-angle trajectories from 1k+ single-camera recordings, and fine-tuned on 550 videos from 350 infants, predicted each individual BabyOSCAR item with a mean within-site AUC of 0.79 and the BabyOSCAR total with a Spearman correlation of 0.86 on held-out recordings. Without being trained on diagnosis, the predicted total separated infants with CP from those without CP with an AUC of 0.86, exceeding a replicated two-dimensional feature classifier (0.67) but below the expert clinician rater's total (0.97). Predicted scores also carried information about functional ability and body distribution, though less than expert clinician scores. These results show that biomechanically grounded representations of monocular video can support scalable, joint-level assessment of selective motor control in infancy

</details>


### [122] [MemHand: Memory-Enhanced World-Space Hand Motion Reconstruction from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32876)
*Junyi Ma,Zhangshuo Qi,Erhang Zhang,Mingjie Li,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemHand 是一个记忆增强框架，用于从第一人称视频中恢复双手的连续世界空间运动，通过短期事件记忆和长期尺度记忆解决固定窗口上下文不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一人称视频恢复双手的世界空间运动对机器人策略迁移和增强现实很重要；已有方法依赖固定长度窗口提取时间上下文，无法保证每帧在遮挡或手移出视野时都有足够上下文来准确重建世界空间中的手部姿态和度量尺度。

Method: 提出 MemHand，维护两种互补记忆：短期事件记忆保存遮挡或移出视野事件周围观察良好手部的上下文，提供给视觉证据弱或缺失的帧；长期尺度记忆在有限状态内保留整个视频中最可靠的观测作为尺度锚点，因为度量尺度在整个录制中保持不变。

Result: 在包含严重遮挡和频繁移出视野间隔的基准上，MemHand 达到最先进精度并泛化到未见领域；相比 StableHand，在 ARCTIC 上 W-MPJPE 降低 57%，在 HOT3D 上降低 70%，输出连续且度量准确的世界空间手部姿态和轨迹。

Conclusion: MemHand 通过短期事件记忆与长期尺度记忆的协同，有效解决了固定窗口上下文不足的问题，实现了连续、度量准确的世界空间手部运动重建。

Abstract: Recovering the continuous world-space motion of both hands from egocentric video is essential for applications such as human-to-robot policy transfer and augmented reality. Achieving this goes beyond reconstructing the hands from each individual frame, as the context that a frame needs is often spread over time, especially around occlusions or when a hand moves out of sight. Recent works have explored how to exploit temporal context, yet they mostly draw it from fixed-length windows. Such windows cannot guarantee that every frame has the context required to reconstruct the hands in world space with accurate poses and metric scale. To address this limitation, we introduce MemHand, a memory-enhanced framework for world-space hand motion reconstruction. MemHand maintains two complementary forms of memory. The Short-Term Event Memory keeps the context of the well-observed hand around each occlusion or out-of-sight event and supplies it to frames with weak or missing visual evidence. In addition, since the metric scale holds over the whole recording, the Long-Term Scale Memory retains the most reliable observations across the video as scale anchors within a bounded state. Across benchmarks featuring severe occlusions and frequent out-of-sight intervals, MemHand achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and generalizes well to unseen domains. Benefiting from the synergy of both memories, it cuts W-MPJPE by 57\% on ARCTIC and 70\% on HOT3D relative to StableHand, delivering continuous and metrically accurate world-space hand poses and trajectories. We will release our code and models at the project page: this https URL

</details>


### [123] [MemAnchorWorld: A Sparse World Model with Horizon-Conditioned Memory Retrieval for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32745)
*Xinyang Jia,Yuqi Zhang,Jiakai Wang,Yahu Yang,Guandong Pan,Chao Tong,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemAnchorWorld提出一种显式检索历史几何与语义证据的稀疏占用世界模型，用于4D语义占用预测，在Occ3D-nuScenes上达到1-3秒预测范围的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏世界模型依赖场景状态隐式携带历史信息，导致误差逐步传播累积，产生几何与语义偏差，难以支撑长时程预测。

Method: 受von Restorff效应启发，设计预测时间条件的分层双路径记忆锚点检索机制，依据历史信息的有效年龄与语义兼容性，结合查询级候选选择与点级记忆锚点检索；并引入由任务与预测时间联合门控的点级残差适配器，分别控制对语义、占用置信度与空间位置的残差修正。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes上，1-3秒预测范围的平均mIoU为13.55%，平均IoU为22.84%，达到当前最佳性能。

Conclusion: 显式检索历史证据可有效缓解稀疏世界模型中的误差累积问题，提升长时程4D语义占用预测的几何与语义精度。

Abstract: Sparse world models for 4D semantic occupancy forecasting represent scenes with a small set of evolving queries to efficiently predict future geometry and semantics for autonomous driving. However, existing sparse world models face a bottleneck in using historical temporal evidence for long-horizon forecasting. They rely on scene states to carry historical information implicitly, allowing errors to propagate and accumulate across successive forecasting steps and causing geometric and semantic deviations. To address this limitation, we present MemAnchorWorld, a sparse occupancy world model that explicitly retrieves relevant historical geometric and semantic evidence for 4D semantic occupancy forecasting. Inspired by the von Restorff effect, we introduce a forecast-time-conditioned hierarchical dual-path memory-anchor retrieval mechanism. Guided by the effective age and semantic compatibility of historical information, it combines query-level candidate selection with point-level memory-anchor retrieval to provide historical evidence for sparse points at different forecast times. To better use memory anchors and existing features, a point-level residual adapter jointly gated by the task and forecast time separately controls residual corrections to semantics, occupancy confidence, and spatial positions. On Occ3D-nuScenes, MemAnchorWorld achieves an average mIoU of 13.55% and an average IoU of 22.84% across the 1-3 s forecasting horizons, attaining state-of-the-art performance on both this http URL page: this https URL

</details>


### [124] [Diving Deeper into Dense Keypoint Localization in Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32728)
*Jiahong Chen,Jinghao Wang,Yueneng Yang,Banglei Guan,Zi Wang,Dewen Hu,Qifeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GHLAPose，一种结合混合格雷-二进制编码与局部感知细化的全局到局部6D位姿估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于密集语义关键点的方法使用二进制编码进行高效全局关键点定位，但二进制编码的不连续性阻碍高分辨率训练，且其离散和量化误差导致显著定位不确定性。

Method: GHLAPose采用全局到局部框架，首先使用混合格雷-二进制（HGB）编码缓解不连续性以实现准确的初始全局关键点定位；然后通过局部感知细化（LAR）模块估计不确定性并细化关键点定位，聚合全局和局部特征。

Result: 在LM、LM-O、YCB-V和TUD-L数据集上，GHLAPose超越最先进方法，尤其在细粒度指标上。在LM-O上，ADD(-S) 0.02d和0.05d分别比第二名方法高1.4%和1.9%；在YCB-V上，ADD(-S) 0.1d高1%。

Conclusion: GHLAPose通过HGB编码与LAR模块的协同，有效解决了二进制编码的离散和量化误差问题，实现了精确的全局到局部6D位姿估计，并在多个数据集上取得领先性能。

Abstract: 6D pose estimation of rigid objects is a fundamental and challenging task in computer vision. Recent dense semantic keypoint-based methods employ binary encoding for efficient global keypoint localization, delivering strong performance in object pose estimation. Nevertheless, the discontinuous nature of binary encoding hinders effective training at high resolutions, while its inherent discrete and quantization errors lead to significant localization uncertainty. We propose GHLAPose, a global-to-local framework that synergizes hybrid Gray-binary (HGB) encoding with local-aware refinement (LAR). Specifically, HGB encoding first alleviates discontinuity to achieve accurate initial global keypoint localization. To further address intrinsic encoding errors, the LAR module estimates uncertainty and refines keypoint localization. This synergy enables a precise global-to-local strategy by aggregating global and local features. Extensive experiments on LM, LM-O, YCB-V, and TUD-L datasets demonstrate that GHLAPose surpasses state-of-the-art methods, particularly on fine-grained metrics. On LM-O, it outperforms the second-best method by 1.4% and 1.9% on ADD(-S) 0.02d and 0.05d respectively, and on YCB-V by 1% on ADD(-S) 0.1d. The code will be available at GHLAPose.

</details>


### [125] [Knocked Off Course: Tracing Uncommanded Motion from Perception to Action in Embodied VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32705)
*Dan Zlotnikov,Nadav Alali,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在未指令自运动（如风、不平地形等扰动）下的跟踪性能，发现模型对突发目标丢失敏感，并通过针对性微调显著提升恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型用于具身跟踪平台，但平台常因环境扰动偏离指令，模型对未指令自运动的处理能力尚不明确，需系统评估并改进。

Method: 在三个数据集上基准测试三个跟踪VLA模型；从像素到动作追踪自运动，识别表征与动作两个缺口；对Uni-NaVid模型用仅占原训练集1.7%的数据进行阶梯式微调。

Result: 所有模型对突发目标丢失敏感；微调变体在目标丢失后提升5-22点且不降低干净成功率；平滑丢失后最佳模型相对提升72%，恢复76%的差距；改进部分通过文本介导的动作依赖实现。

Conclusion: 无需新架构、大规模数据收集或从头训练，针对性微调即可有效提升VLA模型对未指令自运动的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) enable simultaneous processing of egocentric video and textual target descriptions to produce action commands for embodied tracking platforms. However, these platforms rarely move exactly as commanded, in part due to unexpected environmental disturbances such as wind, unleveled terrain, and road bumps. Here we study how such models handle uncommanded egomotion. First, we explore performance across diverse disturbances by benchmarking three tracking VLAs on three datasets. We find that models differ in which environmental disturbances hurt them most, but all are sensitive to sudden target loss. Tracing egomotion from pixels to emitted actions, we identify two gaps. In representation, motion is reliably recovered through explicit relations between frames, but aggressive compression of past frames weakens small-motion readouts. In action, large rotations do reach the decision state, yet the decoded direction carries only a few percent of a mirrored rotation's effect on the turn. Targeting both gaps, we fine-tune one model, Uni-NaVid, on data amounting to just 1.7% of its original training set in a ladder of analysis-motivated variants. These gain 5-22 points after target-loss without degrading clean success; after a smooth loss, the best one improves success by 72% (relative), recovering 76% of its gap to clean. We also show that targeted fine-tuning improves recovery partly via a text-mediated action dependency, without new architecture, large-scale data collection or training from scratch.

</details>


### [126] [RestPose: Canonicalization for Open Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32696)
*Sandeep Inuganti,Weihang Li,Benjamin Busam,Didier Stricker,Jason Rambach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RestPose方法，无需参考模型或规范帧约定即可从单张图像进行开放物体位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的物体位姿估计依赖任意选择的规范帧来生成真值标注，限制了泛化能力。

Method: 从单张输入图像生成物体网格，通过几何与物理分析进行规范化，并利用基础模型的稠密匹配估计位姿。

Result: 在HouseCat6D、NOCS和Omni6DPose数据集上，绝对指标提升18.3点，尺度无关指标提升13.8点，且无需任务特定训练。

Conclusion: 可为任意物体推导规范帧，实现无需参考模型或规范帧约定的开放物体位姿估计，优于监督式最先进方法。

Abstract: In object pose estimation methods, predicted poses are expressed with respect to a canonical frame, defined by a reference model of that object. In learning-based methods, an arbitrarily chosen modeling convention defines this canonical frame, which is then used to generate the ground-truth pose-annotations for training. Object pose estimation methods are therefore bound to such arbitrary conventions, restricting generalization. In this work, we propose that such a canonical frame can instead be derived for any object, and present RestPose, an approach towards open object pose estimation that does not require reference models or canonical frame conventions during training. From a single input image, we generate an object mesh and canonicalize it through a geometric and physical analysis of the object, then use foundation model-based dense matching to estimate the pose. We choose category-level benchmarks for our experiments, which contain instances across various object categories and enable us to measure the effectiveness of our canonicalization. Across HouseCat6D, NOCS, and Omni6DPose datasets, we improve on the supervised state-of-the-art methods by 18.3 points in absolute metrics and 13.8 points in scale-agnostic metrics, without requiring task-specific training. Code will be published.

</details>


### [127] [CoReQ: Condition-Aware Cross-Modal Repair and Quality-Guided Robust Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32588)
*Ziwei Guo,Lingzhi Li,Jin Wang,Yunjiang Xu,Yupeng Ouyang,Benyuan Yang,Yujun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoReQ框架，通过条件感知的双向跨模态修复与质量引导的多车特征融合，提升恶劣天气下LiDAR与4D Radar协同感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多车协同感知可缓解遮挡与感知范围受限，但恶劣天气导致缺失回波和虚假回波，降低单车特征质量并影响协同检测；LiDAR几何信息丰富但天气适应性差，4D Radar天气适应性强但几何信息有限，二者互补有望改善退化观测下的感知，但需解决如何根据当前观测条件调整跨模态修复以及如何合理融合修复后可靠性仍存差异的多车特征两个问题。

Method: 提出CoReQ框架，包含条件引导的双向跨模态修复模块（CBCR）从点云占用率、密度和特征统计中提取车辆级观测条件并据此调整LiDAR与Radar特征的双向校正；以及任务感知质量估计模块（TaQE）利用EMA教师的分类与定位信息构建监督目标预测修复后特征的空间质量，再由条件与质量引导融合模块（CoQF）将质量信息与内容匹配并结合双方观测条件进行多车特征加权融合。

Result: 在V2X-R和V2X-Radar-C数据集上实验表明，该框架在多种观测条件下具有竞争力，在V2X-R雪天条件下AP@0.7取得显著提升，并在对比的V2X-Radar-C方法中取得最高AP。

Conclusion: CoReQ通过条件感知修复与质量引导融合有效提升了恶劣天气下多车协同感知的检测性能，验证了LiDAR与4D Radar互补及跨模态修复与可靠性加权融合的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle collaborative perception can use observations from different perspectives to alleviate the problems of occlusion and limited sensing range. However, missing echoes and false echoes caused by bad weather will reduce the quality of single-vehicle features and affect collaborative detection. LiDAR provides richer geometric information, and 4D Radar has strong weather adaptability. The complementarity of the two provides the possibility to improve perception under degraded observations. However, how to adjust cross-modal repair according to the current observation conditions and how to reasonably integrate multi-vehicle features whose reliability still differs after repair are still problems that need to be solved. To this end, we propose CoReQ, a condition-aware repair and quality-guided collaborative perception framework. For the former, the condition-guided bidirectional cross-modal repair module (CBCR) extracts vehicle-level observation conditions from point cloud occupancy, density and feature statistics, and adjusts the bidirectional correction of LiDAR and Radar features accordingly. For the latter, we combine local reliability assessment with collaborative weighting: first, the task-aware quality estimation module (TaQE) uses the classification and positioning information of the EMA teacher to construct a supervision target and predict the spatial quality of the repaired features; then, the condition and quality guided fusion module (CoQF) matches the quality information with the content, and the observation conditions of both parties are jointly used for multi-vehicle feature weighted fusion. Experiments on V2X-R and V2X-Radar-C show that the framework is competitive under a variety of observation conditions, achieving significant gains in AP@0.7 on V2X-R under snowy conditions, and achieving the highest AP among the compared V2X-Radar-C methods.

</details>


### [128] [DiffAnchor: Anchor-Space Diffusion for Monocular 3D Hand Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32513)
*Changlong Jiang,Youci Tang,Haohong Kuang,Hang Xu,Jinghong Zheng,Ran Wang,Zhenghong Yu,Chen Feng,Xiaofeng Yue,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiffAnchor 提出锚空间扩散框架，通过扩散中间锚点而非直接扩散关节坐标，以少步去噪和单假设实现高精度单目3D手部姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法直接扩散最终关节需同时探索合理姿态和恢复精确坐标，依赖额外去噪步或多假设，推理成本高。

Method: 固定上下文锚提供全局参考，带噪动态锚迭代细化至关节区域；关键点引导的锚选择与补充机制保留有效锚、剔除无效锚并探索覆盖不足区域；锚到关节偏移预测与加权聚合完成精确定位。

Result: 在HO-3D V2、NYU、ICVL上达到最优性能，在InterHand2.6M交互手估计上具竞争力，推理效率与确定性回归基线相当。

Conclusion: 将随机探索作用于中间锚而非最终输出可降低扩散精度负担，实现少步单假设的准确高效3D手部姿态估计。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient monocular 3D hand pose estimation is an important perception capability for embodied human interaction and dexterous manipulation. Recent diffusion-based methods model pose uncertainty by iteratively denoising 3D joint coordinates. However, directly diffusing the final joints requires the generative process to simultaneously explore plausible poses and recover precise coordinates, often relying on additional denoising steps or multiple hypotheses and consequently increasing inference cost. In this work, we propose DiffAnchor, a few-step anchor-space diffusion framework that diffuses anchors rather than joints. Our key insight is that stochastic exploration does not need to operate directly on the final output. Instead, diffusion searches for informative intermediate anchors that serve as local 3D regressors, while anchor-to-joint offset prediction and weighted aggregation complete precise joint localization. Specifically, fixed context anchors provide stable global references, whereas noisy dynamic anchors are iteratively refined toward informative joint regions. An importance-guided anchor selection and replenishment mechanism further retains useful anchors, removes ineffective ones, and explores insufficiently covered regions. This design reduces the precision burden of the diffusion process and enables accurate prediction using only a few denoising steps and a single hypothesis. Extensive experiments on HO-3D V2, NYU, ICVL, and InterHand2.6M demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the first three benchmarks and competitive performance on challenging interacting-hand estimation, while maintaining inference efficiency comparable to deterministic regression baselines.

</details>


### [129] [GeoVLDrive: Geometry-Grounded Vision-Language Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32510)
*JiaHui Huang,Songyan Zhang,Hanbing Li,Hanchao Leng,Kuiyuan Yang,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoVLDrive是一个基于几何的VLA框架，通过3D基础模型特征增强和多层结构化思维链实现3D时空推理与规划，在NAVSIM基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA规划器虽继承预训练视觉语言模型的语义知识，但其推理很少基于3D场景结构及其随时间演化的事实，缺乏几何基础。

Method: 提出GeoVLDrive：用门控交叉注意力将3D基础模型的多层特征注入视觉语言骨干；用结构化潜在思维链将未来场景几何、关键智能体运动和元动作组织为有序且分别监督的潜在状态；动作专家基于这些状态和多层场景特征生成轨迹提案，并用场景对齐的交互评分令牌排序；同时引入NAVSIM 3DQA数据集用于训练和评估度量3D空间与运动理解。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上达到91.8 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到90.9 EPDMS；在NAVSIM 3DQA上几何骨干总体得分89.2%，比微调的Qwen3-VL-2B基线高4.6个百分点；在LingoQA上骨干达到68.6%的Lingo-Judge准确率。

Conclusion: 将VLA规划器的推理显式建立在3D场景几何及其时序演化之上，并结合结构化潜在思维链与场景对齐的动作评分，能显著提升3D时空理解与规划性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) planners inherit semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language models, but their reasoning is rarely grounded in 3D scene structure and its evolution over time. We introduce GeoVLDrive, a geometry-grounded VLA framework for 3D spatiotemporal reasoning and planning. Multi-layer features from a 3D foundation model augment the vision-language backbone through gated cross-attention. A structured latent chain of thought (CoT) organizes future scene geometry, key-agent motion, and meta-actions into ordered, separately supervised latent states. An action expert generates trajectory proposals from these states and multi-layer scene features, and ranks them using scene-grounded, interacting score tokens. We also introduce NAVSIM 3DQA to train and evaluate metric 3D spatial and motion understanding. GeoVLDrive achieves 91.8 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2. On NAVSIM 3DQA, the geometry-grounded backbone scores 89.2% overall, 4.6 percentage points above the fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-2B baseline. On LingoQA, the backbone achieves 68.6% Lingo-Judge accuracy.

</details>


### [130] [OmniPersist: Persistent Dynamic World Modeling via Coupled Panoramic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32473)
*Zhilu Zhang,Xiaoyu Liu,Wenxi Liu,Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OmniPersist统一框架，将透视视频生成与全景演化耦合于单阶段扩散模型，解决动作条件世界模型中动态物体在视角外无法持续演化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在相机转离再转回时，视野外的运动实体在潜空间中无法合理演化，常发生变异或完全消失，根源在于有限的透视视角。

Method: 构建单阶段扩散模型，同时生成透视视频与低帧率全景流；通过几何锚定（GeoAnch）实现空间对应、时间补偿（TemComp）实现时间聚合；无需辅助模型或初始全景输入；兼容潜在级与注意力级相机控制架构。

Result: 在新建的动态基准上，于图像到视频和视频续写任务中，视觉质量、相机可控性、重访一致性和动态持续性均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: OmniPersist通过透视与全景演化的统一建模有效维持了视野外动态物体的持续性，且对相机控制架构具有通用性。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models play a vital role in robotics and autonomous driving, yet existing video diffusion models struggle to maintain dynamic object persistence. When the camera turns away and returns, out-of-view moving entities fail to evolve plausibly in latent space, often mutating or vanishing entirely due to bounded perspective views. To resolve this issue, we propose OmniPersist, a unified framework that couples perspective video generation with panoramic evolution within a single-stage diffusion model, without relying on auxiliary models or initial panoramic inputs. To maintain efficiency, we model the panoramic stream at a lower frame rate, bridging both views across space and time via Geometric Anchoring (GeoAnch) for spatial correspondence and Temporal Compensation (TemComp) for temporal aggregation. OmniPersist is agnostic to camera-control architectures, supporting both latent-level and attention-level conditioning. Extensive experiments on a newly constructed dynamic benchmark across image-to-video and video continuation tasks demonstrate consistent gains in visual quality, camera controllability, revisit consistency, and dynamic persistence.

</details>


### [131] [ObsBridge: Observation-Grounded Conditioning for Ego Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32447)
*Zhenhao Wang,Shaofeng Liang,Jiemin Wu,Sijia Chen,Keni Shi,Nan Li,Wenshuo Chen,Bowen Tian,Runwei Guan,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ObsBridge，一种基于流的轨迹预测框架，通过将恢复的历史状态与原始观测证据结合，并采用渐进式训练应对不完美的历史恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自我视角预测行人轨迹时，遮挡、有限可见性和跟踪误差会破坏观测到的运动历史，这在自主机器人导航中尤其关键。现有方法要么先填补噪声历史再预测，要么联合训练辅助重建分支，但基于干净历史训练的预测器在部署时对恢复历史中的残余误差敏感，且辅助重建仍在原始损坏坐标上计算智能体交互。

Method: ObsBridge先用冻结的估计器从智能体相对位置、自我相对位置、图像平面位置和可见性状态恢复历史运动；训练中引入按智能体课程，在上下文编码前逐步用完整估计历史替换干净教师历史；设计残差几何适配器融合编码运动与原始观测证据；通过社会注意力聚合表示以条件化基于流的轨迹生成器。

Result: 在多个基准上优于现有方法，在EgoTraj-TBD上将minADE@20降低6.5%，minFDE@20降低8.7%。

Conclusion: 将未来预测条件化于恢复的历史状态和原始观测证据，并采用渐进训练适应不完美恢复，能有效提升自我视角下的行人轨迹预测性能。

Abstract: Pedestrian forecasting from ego-view observations is challenging because occlusion, limited visibility, and tracking errors corrupt observed motion histories. Such errors are particularly consequential for autonomous robot navigation. Existing paradigms typically either impute noisy history positions before forecasting or jointly train an auxiliary history reconstruction branch. However, predictors trained on clean histories may be sensitive to residual errors in recovered histories at deployment. Meanwhile, auxiliary reconstruction still computes inter-agent interactions over raw, corrupted coordinates. We investigate conditioning future prediction on both recovered historical states and raw observation evidence, with progressive training to accommodate imperfect recovery. Building on this insight, we propose ObsBridge, a flow-based trajectory forecasting framework with observation-grounded historical bridging. Specifically, ObsBridge first employs a frozen estimator to recover historical motion from agent-relative and ego-relative positions, image-plane locations, and visibility states. During training, we introduce an agent-wise curriculum that gradually replaces clean teacher histories with complete estimated histories before context encoding. This progressively exposes the predictor to estimated histories during training. Furthermore, we design a residual geometry adapter to fuse the encoded motion with original observation evidence. We aggregate the resulting representations through social attention to condition the flow-based trajectory generator. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ObsBridge outperforms state-of-the-art methods. On EgoTraj-TBD, it reduces minADE@20 by 6.5\% and minFDE@20 by 8.7\%. The code will be provided at this https URL .

</details>


### [132] [A Mesh to Remember: Persistent Appearance for Interactive Video Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32445)
*Joonghyuk Shin,Alex Trevithick,Katarína Tóthová,Liyuan Zhu,Shengyu Huang,Jaesik Park,Shalini De Mello,Laura Leal-Taixé,Riccardo de Lutio,Haithem Turki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M2R，一种少步因果视频渲染器，利用网格作为像素级表面地址，将特征存储在物体表面并在重访时检索，实现长时程一致的实时视频世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型难以在长交互中保持一致性，而显式3D世界外观不真实；现有渲染代理只指定位置，导致外观在重访时被重新发明。

Method: 以网格标识每帧像素对应的表面，将渲染器特征存储在物体局部坐标系的表面上，通过光栅化网格在表面重现时检索特征；仅用合成数据训练。

Result: 零样本迁移到真实室内扫描和编码代理生成的世界；在静态、动态和多代理基准上一致性超越SOTA，物体永久性提升一倍以上，跨代理一致性接近翻倍，实时渲染720p视频达40 FPS。

Conclusion: M2R通过表面地址化特征记忆，实现了外观随物体持久、跨重访共享且内存随表面而非帧数增长的高效一致视频世界模型。

Abstract: Video world models synthesize photorealistic observations in response to user actions but struggle to remain consistent over long interactions. Coding agents and text-to-3D models instead generate explicit 3D worlds whose geometry and state persist by construction, but whose appearance falls far short of photorealism. Recent methods combine the strengths of both by rendering such worlds into coarse proxies that guide a video model. However, since these proxies only specify where things are, the renderer must invent what they look like as the world is explored, and anything it forgets is reinvented when a surface reappears. Our key insight is that a mesh identifies which surface each pixel shows, across frames and views, even as objects move, allowing it to serve as an address for what the renderer generates. We propose M2R (Mesh to Remember), a few-step causal video renderer that stores its own features on the surfaces they depict, in each object's frame, and retrieves them by rasterizing the mesh whenever those surfaces come back into view. Appearance thus travels with objects wherever they move, persists across revisits, and is shared by every agent that observes it, while memory grows with the surfaces seen instead of the number of frames. Trained only on synthetic data, M2R transfers zero-shot to real indoor scans and to worlds authored by coding agents. It outperforms state-of-the-art methods in consistency across static, dynamic, and multi-agent benchmarks, more than doubling object permanence and nearly doubling cross-agent consistency, while rendering 720p video in real time (40 FPS).

</details>


### [133] [Verify then Steer: Progress Hallucination-Aware Recovery for Continuous Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32400)
*Jiewen Hou,Guanxiang Qi,Meina Kan,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PHAR测试时恢复框架，通过检测指令进度幻觉并自适应回退，提升视觉语言导航智能体从路线偏离中恢复的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法在测试时难以从路线偏离中恢复，作者将其归因于“进度幻觉”：智能体将偏离路线的观测误判为任务进展证据，导致内部指令进度超前于实际阶段，无法依据早期子指令恢复。

Method: PHAR遵循“先验证后引导”范式：验证器检查VLM残差流表示以识别跨模态证据不匹配，并用分裂保形校准控制误报；触发警报后，引导模块用上下文多臂老虎机自适应选择指令进度的回退幅度进行在线恢复。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上的大量实验表明，该框架在测试时提升了多种最先进视觉语言导航方法的可靠性。

Conclusion: 进度幻觉是导致路线偏离恢复失败的关键因素，PHAR作为一种模型无关的测试时恢复框架，可有效增强现有VLN方法的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to follow language instructions to reach a target in an unseen 3D environment. However, existing methods still struggle to recover from route deviations at test time. We investigate this limitation through the lens of instruction progress, and trace it to a phenomenon we term progress hallucination: agents misinterpret off-route observations as evidence of task progress, causing their internal instruction progress to advance beyond the actual task stage and hindering recovery based on earlier sub-instructions. To address this failure mode, we propose PHAR (Progress Hallucination-Aware Recovery), a test-time recovery framework following a verify-then-steer paradigm. The verifier examines residual-stream representations of the Vision-Language Model (VLM) to identify mismatch of cross-modal evidence along the trajectories, and uses split conformal calibration to control false alarms. When an alarm is raised, the steering module uses a contextual bandit to adaptively select the rewind extent of instruction progress for online recovery. Through extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE, our framework enhances the reliability of multiple state-of-the-art vision-language navigation methods at test time.

</details>


### [134] [LookAnything: A Foundation Model for Holistic Gaze Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32351)
*Jiayu Tang,Yuchen Zhou,Hongsheng Wu,Hanjing Ye,Ke Huang,Cheng Wang,Yuqin Dai,Yufei Huang,Yonggen Ling,Chao Gou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的基础模型LookAnything，通过大规模多任务跨域训练和可学习视线先验查询，实现无需辅助输入的多功能视线跟随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视线理解方法依赖高度定制架构和辅助输入（头部裁剪、深度图），导致级联错误和推理开销；且仅在单一域训练、局限于热图输出的孤立视线跟随，缺乏灵活性和泛化性。

Method: 构建LookAnything基础模型，引入可学习视线先验查询以促进视线感知表示学习并消除辅助输入需求；支持点、框、热图和语言等多种输出；同时构建HoliGaze语料库与基准（31.1万问答对，覆盖5个能力维度和9项任务）。

Result: 在整体视线跟随任务上持续优于33个领先视觉语言模型，在5个传统目标检测基准上与专用专家模型高度竞争；在未见数据集和真实机器人部署中验证了强域外泛化和灵活适应能力。

Conclusion: LookAnything通过统一多任务跨域训练和视线先验查询，有效桥接视线理解差距，实现灵活、可泛化的整体视线跟随，适用于多样交互场景。

Abstract: Human gaze provides a rich window into attention and intent, making its understanding essential for applications ranging from social signal analysis to human-robot interaction. Existing methods typically employ highly customized architectures relying on auxiliary inputs like head crops or depth maps, causing cascading errors and inference overhead. Trained on single domains and confined to isolated gaze following with heatmap outputs, they lack flexibility and generalization for diverse interactive scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present LookAnything, a foundation model that unifies diverse gaze-centric tasks for holistic gaze following. Benefiting from large-scale multi-task and cross-domain training, LookAnything flexibly adapts to various settings, generating diverse outputs (points, boxes, heatmaps, and language) and supporting broad gaze-centric localization, description, and reasoning. At its core, we introduce learnable gaze-prior queries to promote gaze-aware representation learning, eliminating the need for auxiliary external inputs. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct HoliGaze, a comprehensive corpus and benchmark comprising 311K question-answer pairs across 5 capability dimensions and 9 distinct tasks. Experiments demonstrate that LookAnything consistently outperforms 33 leading vision-language models on holistic gaze following, while remaining highly competitive with dedicated specialists across 5 conventional target detection benchmarks. Evaluation on unseen datasets and deployment on a real robot further validate its strong out-of-domain generalization and flexible adaptation in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [135] [Relation Before Action: Explicit Relation Modeling for Latent Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32226)
*Boning Zhang,Junyu Gao,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RELACT，一个关系优先的潜动作预训练框架，先建模视觉转移关系，再抽象为紧凑潜动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜动作预训练把视觉转移建模与动作推断耦合在一起，直接将成对观测或转移线索映射为潜动作码，未能区分转移关系刻画与动作抽象两个任务。

Method: RELACT先构建结构化关系表示R，刻画视觉元素及其交互在观测间的演化；再推断紧凑、以状态为条件的随机潜动作U，并通过动作条件的潜动力学学习其预测效果。

Result: 在域内和域外图像目标导航、目标条件操作及下游策略自适应等实验中，预测性动作表示得到改善，规划与控制取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 关系优先的潜动作预训练有效且具有通用性，所得动作表示可用于模型规划或监督下游具身策略。

Abstract: Learning latent actions from unlabeled video sequences offers a promising way to acquire reusable action representations without relying on embodiment-specific control annotations. However, existing latent-action pretraining approaches commonly couple the modeling of visual transitions with action inference, directly mapping paired observations or derived transition cues into latent action codes. This formulation leaves two distinct tasks entangled: characterizing the relational structure of an observed transition and abstracting it into a compact action representation. We propose RELACT, a relation-first framework that explicitly separates transition relation modeling from predictive action abstraction. RELACT first constructs a structured relation representation $R$ that captures how visual elements and their interactions evolve across observations. It then infers a compact, state-conditioned stochastic latent action $U$, whose predictive effects are learned through action-conditioned latent dynamics. The resulting action representation can be grounded in native control spaces for model-based planning or used to supervise downstream embodied policies. Extensive experiments across in-domain and out-of-domain image-goal navigation, goal-conditioned manipulation, and downstream policy adaptation demonstrate improved predictive action representations and consistent gains in planning and control, supporting the effectiveness and generality of relation-first latent action pretraining.

</details>


### [136] [NativeDrive: Native Multimodal Foundations for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32216)
*Zehua Fan,Qi Jiang,Wei Li,Kai Tang,Yue Jiang,Yujian Li,Bailin Li,Feng Gao,Yan Wang,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NativeDrive是一个无需编码器的原生多模态驾驶框架，复用统一主干的专家接口进行策略学习，并引入驾驶语义编辑作为稠密监督任务，在NAVSIM v1/v2上取得有竞争力的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要语义理解与细粒度场景感知以进行精确动作预测；现有VLA模型受限于低维动作标签的结构监督不足，而通过视觉生成任务或视频世界动作模型扩展又会引入与规划无关的外观重建开销。

Method: 基于原生多模态模型，将统一主干适配于策略学习：Native Action Interface复用理解专家的token预测接口处理离散策略，并适配生成专家处理连续策略；Driving Semantic Editing复用生成专家的编辑能力，在训练时提供稠密场景结构监督，推理时仅运行选定的规划策略。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2上，NativeDrive在统一理解与生成模型中取得有竞争力的规划性能，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 复用统一多模态主干的专家接口并引入面向驾驶的稠密监督任务，可在避免外观重建开销的同时有效提升规划性能，验证了无需编码器框架的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires semantic understanding and fine-grained scene perception for precise action prediction. Vision-language-action models (VLAs) inherit semantic priors, but low-dimensional action labels provide limited structural supervision. Some studies extend VLAs with visual generation tasks or adopt video-based world-action models (WAMs), but both approaches introduce appearance-reconstruction overhead, which is less relevant to planning. Native multimodal models offer an alternative to unify understanding and generation within one backbone. On this basis, we propose NativeDrive, a native encoder-free multimodal driving framework adapting the unified backbone for policy learning and introduce a dense supervision task tailored to driving. The Native Action Interface reuses the understanding expert's token-prediction interface for the discrete policy and adapts the generation expert for continuous policy. Driving Semantic Editing reuses the generation expert's editing capability for dense scene-structure supervision during training, while inference runs only the selected planning policy. NativeDrive achieves competitive planning performance among unified understanding-and-generation models on NAVSIM v1 and v2, with ablations validating its components' effectiveness. Code will be released.

</details>


### [137] [Clinical3D-Bench: Evaluating and Improving Spatial Intelligence of Multimodal LLMs in Hospital Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32094)
*Yang Bai,Yingzhi Xia,Ling Lin,Jun Cheng,Lin Fu,Huazhu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Clinical3D-Bench 医院空间推理基准，发现主流多模态大模型表现远逊于医生，并提出 GeoJeV 方法让模型真正利用 3D 信息，取得最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准对医院场景所需的相似设备追踪、患者相对方向判断、通道宽度估计等空间推理能力覆盖不足，且模型可能未真正利用提供的 3D 信息。

Method: 构建含 373 个病房布局、3,368 道八类问题的仿真基准 Clinical3D-Bench（答案由 3D 场景计算、配对场景状态检验证据敏感性、十名医生审核）；用 44,750 题训练集 Clinical3D-45K 微调；提出 GeoJeV：从视频重建场景记忆，依据问题状态选择 3D 操作及参数，将其证据加入选项打分。

Result: 35 个 MLLM 中最佳开源与闭源模型宏平均准确率仅 45.7% 和 53.6%，答案先验为 40.8%，医生子集达 79.0%；32 个开源模型中 27 个在患者相对方向任务低于 40%；微调使两个 MLLM 超过最佳闭源模型 16-20 点；微调模型几乎不使用输入的 3D 地图；GeoJeV 在所有评估模型中准确率最高，缩小了与医生的差距。

Conclusion: 现有 MLLM 医院空间推理能力有限且未有效利用 3D 信息，让模型主动进行 3D 计算（GeoJeV）是提升空间推理、逼近人类医生水平的有效方向。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning in hospitals requires tracking look-alike equipment, interpreting patient-relative directions, and judging passage widths, yet existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of these abilities. We introduce Clinical3D-Bench, a simulated benchmark with 3,368 questions of eight types on egocentric walkthroughs of 373 hospital-room layouts. Layouts follow hospital design guidelines, answers are computed from the 3D scenes, and paired scene states test whether models change their answers when relevant evidence changes. Videos are photorealistic, and ten physicians screened the layouts. Among 35 multimodal large language models (MLLMs), the best open and proprietary models reach 45.7% and 53.6% macro accuracy, against 40.8% for an answer prior and 79.0% for physicians on a subset, and 27 of 32 open models score below 40% on two-way patient-relative directions. Fine-tuning on Clinical3D-45K, our 44,750-question training set, lifts two MLLMs 16-20 points above the best proprietary model. 3D maps given to the fine-tuned MLLM as input go largely unused: replacing them with another video's, even when they are ground truth, barely changes its accuracy. The proposed GeoJeV instead lets the MLLM compute with 3D: it reconstructs a scene memory from each video, selects 3D operations and their arguments from the MLLM's question state, and adds their evidence to its option scores. It achieves the highest accuracy of all evaluated models, narrowing the gap to physicians.

</details>


### [138] [SurgGenesis: A Generative Surgical World Model for Future-Aware Surgical Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32054)
*Qirui Zhong,Quande Liu,Yang Chen,Cheng Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SurgGenesis生成式手术世界模型，通过三阶段渐进LoRA将大规模视频扩散模型适配到数据稀缺的手术领域，作为可扩展手术数据引擎并验证其未来感知性与动作忠实有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术场景理解受数据稀缺和长尾类别不平衡制约：标注手术视频需稀缺临床专业知识，罕见手术事件代表性不足。

Method: 构建潜在空间手术世界模型，从视觉观察与文本程序描述学习未来手术状态转移；采用三阶段渐进LoRA策略，逐步注入内窥镜视觉先验、程序语义与空间运动线索，避免灾难性遗忘；从未来感知性（GT潜在预测差距与时间跨度分析）和动作忠实有用性（生成片段含指令手术三元组、重平衡长尾事件并提升下游分割、三元组识别、3D重建）两轴验证。

Result: 条件生成未来可补充传统长尾采样，在长尾事件重平衡与下游任务上获得动作特定增益，并展现出对未来状态的预测能力。

Conclusion: SurgGenesis可作为可扩展手术数据引擎，其条件生成未来与传统长尾采样互补，且以未来感知性和动作忠实有用性区分世界模型与通用生成器。

Abstract: Surgical scene understanding is constrained by data scarcity and long-tail class imbalance, as annotating surgical videos demands scarce clinical expertise while rare procedural events remain underrepresented. We tackle this through data generation and propose SurgGenesis, a generative surgical world model that learns future surgical state transitions in a latent space from visual observations and textual procedural descriptions, serving as a scalable surgical data engine. To adapt a large-scale video diffusion model to the data-scarce surgical domain without catastrophic forgetting, we introduce a three-stage progressive LoRA strategy that incrementally injects endoscopic visual priors, procedural semantics, and spatial motion cues. Rather than judging generation by visual fidelity alone, we validate SurgGenesis along two axes that separate a world model from a generic generator: (i) future-awareness, by comparing predicted futures against the real future through a GT-latent prediction gap and temporal-horizon analysis, and (ii) action-faithful usefulness, by checking that generated clips contain the commanded surgical triplet and that using them to rebalance long-tail events and drive downstream understanding (segmentation, triplet recognition, 3D reconstruction) yields action-specific gains. Conditioned futures therefore complement conventional long-tail sampling. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [139] [Instant LiDAR: Feed-Forward Gaussian Reconstruction for LiDAR Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32042)
*Xin Kang,Jiahui Huang,Jiawei Ren,Sipeng Zhang,Yu Chen,Weihua Zhang,Junjie Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Instant LiDAR 提出一种无需逐场景优化的前馈式管道，可在数分钟内将多帧 LiDAR 扫描重建为可复用的高斯资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LiDAR 重建需数小时的逐场景优化，导致大规模资产库构建与维护成本高昂；同时直接以 LiDAR 测量初始化高斯会因非均匀采样与有限视场导致重建质量差。

Method: Instant LiDAR 结合测量锚定的高斯预测、采样感知的高斯尺度以及多帧融合：预测器在记录的 LiDAR 回波锚点上细化高斯属性，尺度适配 LiDAR 采样以调整空间支撑，多帧融合将重叠预测合并为覆盖整个驾驶日志的紧凑资产。

Result: 相比 NuRec，在 Physical AI (PAI) 上重建速度约快 46 倍，在 Waymo 上约快 70 倍；在 Waymo 上下游 3D 检测精度相当。

Conclusion: 前馈式重建可在无逐场景优化下快速生成可复用 LiDAR 高斯资产，并保持下游任务精度，显著降低大规模资产库的构建成本。

Abstract: LiDAR reconstruction supports closed-loop autonomous driving simulation, but hours of per-scene optimization make large asset libraries costly to build and maintain. We present Instant LiDAR, a feed-forward pipeline that reconstructs multiple LiDAR scans as reusable Gaussian assets in minutes without per-scene optimization. LiDAR measurements provide strong geometric priors, but directly initializing Gaussians from them yields poor reconstruction due to nonuniform sampling and a limited field of view. Sparse returns leave surface gaps, while combining scans expands coverage but introduces redundant primitives. To address these challenges, Instant LiDAR combines measurement-anchored Gaussian prediction, sampling-aware Gaussian scales, and multi-frame consolidation. The predictor refines attributes anchored to recorded returns, while the scales adapt spatial support to LiDAR sampling. Multi-frame consolidation merges overlapping predictions into a compact asset spanning the driving log. Compared with NuRec, Instant LiDAR reconstructs scenes approximately 46 times faster on Physical AI (PAI) and 70 times faster on Waymo. It also achieves comparable downstream 3D detection accuracy on Waymo.

</details>


### [140] [Do Occupancy World Models Really Follow Actions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32029)
*Jongseong Bae,Ha Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReCast模型，通过自我轨迹引导的几何对应学习，提升占用世界模型对反事实轨迹的忠实预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占用世界模型在反事实动作下预测不能忠实跟随指定轨迹，因为驾驶数据集对其他轨迹覆盖稀疏，模型隐式学习自我运动的空间效应，导致动作效应与场景-轨迹相关性纠缠。

Method: 提出ReCast，一种自我轨迹引导的Transformer，将未来预测建立在以自我为中心的帧间动作诱导的几何对应上；解耦历史编码与未来解码，并利用相对自我变换对齐可变形交互，同时保留非刚性场景演化的灵活性；引入反事实动作忠实性基准，覆盖分布内、尾部及分布外轨迹。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes上，ReCast取得有竞争力的标准预测性能，同时改进反事实预测，在分布外轨迹下增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模动作诱导的几何对应，ReCast能更忠实地遵循指定轨迹进行未来预测，尤其在分布外反事实场景中表现优越。

Abstract: Occupancy world models predict future 3D scenes conditioned on ego actions, yet their predictions may not faithfully follow the specified trajectory under counterfactual actions. Because driving datasets provide sparse coverage of alternative trajectories for each scene, existing models learn the spatial effect of ego motion implicitly from observed scene--trajectory combinations, which may entangle action effects with scene--trajectory correlations. We propose ReCast, an ego-trajectory-guided transformer that grounds future prediction in action-induced geometric correspondence across ego-centric frames. ReCast decouples historical encoding from future decoding and aligns deformable interactions using relative ego transformations, while retaining flexibility for non-rigid scene evolution. We also introduce a counterfactual action-faithfulness benchmark covering in-distribution, tail, and out-of-distribution trajectories. On Occ3D-nuScenes, ReCast achieves competitive standard forecasting performance while improving counterfactual forecasting, with particularly clear gains under out-of-distribution trajectories.

</details>


### [141] [Learning Agent States for Latent Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32017)
*Shaoan Xie,Fan Feng,Peiyuan Zhu,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Agent-State LAM，通过联合学习紧凑的智能体状态表示与潜在动作建模，并引入空间匹配时序判别（SMTD），将背景和外观等无关信息从潜在动作中解耦，提升动作恢复、下游控制与世界模型预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型（LAMs）从无标注视频中学习动作式潜在表示时，若用于动作推断和转移预测的表示空间将智能体不断变化的物理状态与视觉背景、持续外观等上下文纠缠在一起，推断出的潜在动作会编码与动作无关的信息。

Method: 联合学习紧凑的智能体状态表示和建模其状态转移的潜在动作；该紧凑状态将密集视觉特征聚合到动作推断与前向预测共享的空间中；引入空间匹配时序判别（SMTD），利用冻结的局部视觉特征做空间灵活匹配，通过强调各帧最强局部特征匹配来区分源帧与邻近帧，使背景和持续外观因位移仍可匹配而信息量降低，从而促使表示聚焦智能体变化的物理状态。

Result: 在含干扰的控制任务和六个真实机器人视频数据集上的大量实验表明，动作恢复、下游控制与世界模型预测均有提升。

Conclusion: 将动作推断与预测建立在紧凑的智能体状态表示上，并用SMTD引导其保留的信息，可有效解耦与动作无关的视觉上下文，改善潜在动作学习和基于其的预训练效果。

Abstract: Latent action models (LAMs) learn action-like latents from unlabeled video by coupling frame-pair inference with latent-conditioned prediction, enabling policy and world-model pretraining. However, what the latent must explain depends on the representation space used for action inference and transition prediction. When this space entangles the agent's evolving physical state with visual context, such as background and persistent agent appearance, inferred latents can encode information unrelated to the agent's actions. To address this challenge, we propose Agent-State LAM, which jointly learns a compact agent-state representation and latent actions modeling its transitions. The compact state aggregates dense visual features into a shared space for both action inference and forward prediction. To guide what this state retains, we introduce spatially matched temporal discrimination (SMTD), which trains the state to distinguish its source frame from nearby frames through spatially flexible matching with frozen local visual features. Rather than comparing global frame representations, SMTD scores each frame by emphasizing its strongest local feature matches across spatial locations. This scoring allows background and persistent agent appearance to remain matched across frames despite displacement, making these shared cues less informative for identifying the source frame and encouraging the representation to capture the agent's evolving physical state. Extensive experiments on distracting control tasks and six real robot video datasets demonstrate improved action recovery, downstream control, and world-model prediction. Code and checkpoints are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [142] [CodeHOI: Code with Agents for Physically Plausible 4D Hand-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31976)
*Seonghwan Park,Inho Chang,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CodeHOI是一个代理框架，通过程序化生成物理合理的4D手-物交互，覆盖多种材质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽略物理或仅模拟有限材质，而编程代理又缺少驱动交互的手部建模。

Method: 采用Object Agent构建物体及其材质物理，HOI Agent通过接触指定手部并用物理求解器模拟材质，Evaluator Agent依据物理定律检查结果，并引入CodeHOI-Bench基准。

Result: 在100个重建与生成任务上，使用现成前沿模型，CodeHOI为所有材质生成合理交互，且迁移至独立物理引擎后仍保持合理。

Conclusion: CodeHOI使编程代理能够跨多种材质编写物理合理的手-物交互。

Abstract: Modeling 4D hand-object interaction (HOI) is fundamental to understanding manipulation. This requires physics, since hands move objects only by touch and each material responds differently. Yet existing methods ignore this physics or simulate only a narrow range of matter. Programs can instead describe any matter with its physics, but coding agents omit the hand that drives the interaction. We close this gap with CodeHOI, to our knowledge the first agentic framework that writes physically plausible 4D HOI as programs. To write one, an Object Agent first builds each object with the physics of its matter. An HOI Agent then specifies the hands by their contacts and simulates each kind of matter with an established physics solver. Finally, an Evaluator Agent checks each result against physical laws. We also introduce CodeHOI-Bench with 100 reconstruction and generation tasks across four kinds of matter. With off-the-shelf frontier models, CodeHOI writes interactions that are plausible for every kind of matter and remain so when transferred to an independent physics engine. It thereby enables coding agents to write physically plausible HOI across diverse matter.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Rigid Hands: Deforming Skin into Contact-Consistent Hand Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31950)
*Seonghwan Park,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种优化方法，将刚性的手部网格提升为与物体接触一致的网格：微调关节和手腕使手指接触物体，变形皮肤使穿透区域贴附表面，并保持时间一致性；同时训练轻量预测头引导估计手的接触位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手-物数据集和估计器将手表示为刚性铰接网格，导致网格悬空或穿透物体；现有修正方法保持网格刚性仅调整姿态，牺牲姿态准确性换取物理合理性。作者希望在不改变手模型或重训练估计器的前提下，让刚性手像皮肤一样接触物体且尽量保持原姿态。

Method: 通过优化实现：(i) 微调关节和手腕使手指接触物体；(ii) 变形皮肤使穿透区域贴合表面；(iii) 保持变形的时间一致性；由于变形仅作用于靠近物体的皮肤，进一步在提升后的标注上训练轻量预测头，预测估计手应接触的位置并引导变形。

Result: 与现有修正方法相比，该方法在三个已发表估计器上使贴附于物体表面的皮肤面积增加一倍以上，同时姿态几乎不变；学习预测头的引导通过闭合估计留下的接触间隙进一步提升了抓握稳定性。

Conclusion: 现有手-物数据集和估计器无需更改手模型或重训练估计器，即可采用接触一致的手部表示。

Abstract: Hands grasp objects with soft skin. A fingertip flattens against a mug, and a palm curves around a bottle. Yet hand-object datasets and estimators represent the hand as a rigid articulated mesh, which hovers above or penetrates the object where skin would press against it. Existing refiners keep the mesh rigid and adjust only the pose, trading pose accuracy for physical plausibility. We ask whether a rigid hand can instead touch the object as skin does while staying close to its original pose. We lift annotated or estimated rigid hands to contact-consistent meshes through an optimization that (i) slightly adjusts the joints and wrist to bring the fingers onto the object, (ii) deforms the skin so that penetrating regions settle onto the surface, and (iii) keeps this deformation consistent over time. Since deformation reaches only skin already near the object, we further train a lightweight head on the lifted annotations to predict where an estimated hand should touch and steer the deformation there. Unlike existing refiners, the lift more than doubles the skin lying on the object surface for three published estimators while barely changing the pose. Guidance from the learned head further improves grasp stability by closing contact gaps that the estimate leaves open. Existing hand-object datasets and estimators can thus adopt contact-consistent hands without changing the hand model or retraining the estimators.

</details>


### [144] [DraftKV: Protecting Persistent Worlds through Hierarchical Streaming Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31944)
*Xiaoling Zhou,Haopeng Li,Yufa Zhou,Rui Ding,Yu Li,Jun Lin,Enze Xie,Chunhua Shen,Xuan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DraftKV是一个免训练框架，通过几何锚定和查询条件相关性检索历史KV缓存，并结合CPU卸载与分块流式注意力，在保持生成质量的同时降低显存占用和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型在重访已探索位置时难以保持空间几何与外观一致性；历史KV缓存虽有助于长期一致性，但检索与注意力计算随历史增长带来显著延迟。

Method: 提出DraftKV：基于几何锚定与查询条件相关性检索历史KV缓存；理论上证明草案相关性精确复现全分辨率块相关性的均值分支，偏差仅限β加权最大分支，并以Top-K选择重叠度界定检索效用损失；指出草案相关性无法区分池化描述子相似的块，故需几何锚定；引入流式CG-Attention，通过分块流式将CPU到GPU的KV传输与注意力计算重叠，隐藏传输延迟并保持显存有界。

Result: 在多种模型族上，DraftKV生成质量与一致性更优，相比其他CPU卸载KV缓存检索方法实现3.27倍端到端加速和42.2%峰值显存降低；在单张H100 GPU上使用SolarWM-33B实现高质量交互式世界生成与历史KV检索。

Conclusion: DraftKV通过几何锚定检索与流式注意力有效解决了历史KV缓存检索的延迟与显存瓶颈，实现了高质量、高一致性的交互式视频世界生成。

Abstract: Interactive video world models enable action- and camera-controlled exploration, yet struggle to maintain consistent spatial geometry and appearance when revisiting previously explored locations. Historical KV caches help auto-regressive video diffusion models maintain long-term consistency, but their retrieval and attention computation introduce substantial latency as history grows. We propose DraftKV, a training-free framework that retrieves historical KV caches based on geometric grounding and query-conditioned relevance, while combining CPU offloading with tile-wise streaming attention to reduce GPU memory usage and inference latency. Theoretically, we show that draft relevance reproduces the mean branch of full-resolution block relevance exactly, so its deviation is confined to the $\beta$-weighted max branch, and we bound retrieval utility loss in terms of the measured Top-K selection overlap. We further show that draft relevance cannot distinguish blocks with similar pooled descriptors regardless of camera pose, motivating geometric grounding before refinement. Then, we introduce streaming CG-Attention, which overlaps CPU-to-GPU KV transfers with attention computation through tile-wise streaming, hiding transfer latency while maintaining bounded GPU memory usage. Experiments across diverse model families show that DraftKV achieves superior generation quality and consistency while achieving $3.27\times$ end-to-end speedup and 42.2% peak GPU memory reduction over other CPU-offloaded KV cache retrieval methods. Notably, DraftKV enables high-quality interactive world generation with historical KV retrieval on a single H100 GPU using SolarWM-33B.

</details>


### [145] [Decompose, Verify, Evolve: Physically Grounded Shape Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31924)
*Wenchao Ma,Rui Yu,Fan Zhu,Jiachen Liu,Sharon X. Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysDec 是一个基于预训练视觉语言模型的智能体框架，利用语义理解和常识推理从少量图像预测材质感知的基元分解，并通过几何反馈和物理验证器迭代优化，实现几何保真与物理合理性的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形状分解方法主要针对几何保真度训练，缺乏对物理合理性的显式约束，容易产生不连通部件或物理上不稳定的重建结果。

Method: 提出 PhysDec 框架，包含两个智能体流水线：PhysDec-Loop 利用参考3D点云进行几何反馈，并借助物理验证器迭代优化部件结构与材质推理；通过自演化发展出 PhysDec-Evolved，从历史修正中学习，推理时无需点云反馈和修正循环。

Result: 实验表明，PhysDec-Loop 在几何保真度、物理合理性和视觉质量上优于以几何为中心的基线方法；PhysDec-Evolved 以显著更低的推理成本接近其性能。

Conclusion: 结合视觉语言模型的语义与常识推理、物理验证及自演化机制，可在保证几何质量的同时提升分解结果的物理合理性，并实现高效推理。

Abstract: Shape decomposition represents 3D objects as a compact set of geometric primitives for interpretable modeling and simulation. However, existing decomposition methods are trained primarily for geometric fidelity, without explicit constraints on physical plausibility, and can therefore produce disconnected parts or physically unstable reconstructions. We present PhysDec, an agentic framework that draws on a pretrained vision-language model's semantic understanding and commonsense reasoning to predict material-aware primitive decompositions from a few images. Within this framework, PhysDec-Loop is an agentic pipeline that reasons about part structure and materials, using a reference 3D point cloud for geometric feedback and physical verifiers to guide iterative refinement. Through self-evolution, we further develop another agentic pipeline, PhysDec-Evolved, which learns from prior corrections to predict primitive decompositions from images without point-cloud feedback or a correction loop at inference. Experiments show that PhysDec-Loop improves geometric fidelity, physical plausibility and visual quality over geometry-focused baselines, while PhysDec-Evolved approaches its performance at substantially lower inference cost.

</details>


### [146] [TANDEM: Temporal Anchor Densification With Reliability-Aware Memory for Lightweight Sparse Depth Completion in Endoscopic Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31922)
*Weikuan Qiu,Jiale Hou,Chongzhao Xu,Jinlian Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TANDEM是一个用于内窥镜视频的因果稀疏深度补全框架，通过时序锚点致密化和可靠性感知记忆，利用过去测量重投影恢复度量证据，以轻量设计实现高精度深度感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜视频缺乏可靠度量深度，而通过跟踪、飞行时间传感或器械接触获得的度量测量仅覆盖每帧小部分像素，稀疏深度补全成为紧迫且未解决的挑战。

Method: 提出TANDEM框架，采用轻量设计，利用视频流将过去测量重投影到当前视图以恢复单帧稀疏无法提供的度量证据；显式记忆用解析重观测、年龄和运动视差因子对每个重投影测量评分且不覆盖当前测量；紧凑网络通过可靠性加权梯度积分和频率门控参考先验重建深度。

Result: 在C3VD结肠镜和StereoMIS腹腔镜数据集上评估，仅5.58M参数，相比最强基线均方根误差分别降低11.7%和32.0%，达到最先进精度，并在内窥镜姿态估计报告级别的姿态误差下保持准确。

Conclusion: TANDEM有效解决了内窥镜视频稀疏深度补全问题，以轻量设计实现高精度和鲁棒性，代码将在接收后发布。
