Abstract: For endoscopic robotic surgery, accurate dense depth perception serves as a foundational capability, which enables navigation, instrument localization, and tissue interaction. However, endoscopic video lacks reliable metric depth, while metric measurements obtained via tracking, time-of-flight sensing, or instrument contact only cover a small fraction of pixels in each frame, rendering sparse depth completion an urgent and unsolved challenge. In this work, we propose Temporal Anchor Densification with Reliability-Aware Memory (TANDEM), a causal sparse depth completion framework for endoscopic video. It adopts a lightweight design and leverages the video stream: past measurements re-projected into the current view recover the metric evidence unavailable within a single sparse frame. To keep this evidence interpretable, accurate, and efficient, an explicit memory scores each re-projected measurement with analytic re-observation, age, and motion-parallax factors without overriding current measurements. A compact network then reconstructs depth via reliability-weighted gradient integration and a frequency-gated reference prior. We evaluate TANDEM on the C3VD colonoscopy and StereoMIS laparoscopy datasets. With only 5.58 M parameters, TANDEM reduces the root mean square error by 11.7% and 32.0%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline, and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy. It also remains accurate under pose errors of the magnitude reported for endoscopic pose estimation. Code will be released at this https URL upon acceptance.

</details>


### [147] [Generalizable Feed-Forward 3D Hand-Object Reconstruction with Coarse-to-Fine Contact Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31812)
*Seonghwan Park,Yong-Hoon Kwon,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HORCRUX是一个统一的前馈Transformer，通过粗到细的接触估计，从单张图像重建未见物体的手-物体3D姿态及精确接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合估计器依赖预定义物体集，且接触仅由图像特征预测、缺乏几何定位；需泛化到未见物体并精确定位接触。

Method: 采用冻结基础模型从给定或图像重建的网格编码物体以泛化；共享Transformer联合推理物体与一/两只手保持对齐；利用重建几何在手部细区域上由粗到细解析逐关节接触预测。

Result: 在七个基准上，物体与手姿态精度达联合估计器最优，细粒度接触优于专用接触估计器。

Conclusion: 接触应与重建联合估计，因为重建几何可直接定位接触。

Abstract: Reconstructing the 3D poses of hands and objects from a single image, together with the contact between them, is fundamental to understanding manipulation. To be practical, the reconstruction must generalize to unseen objects, and the contact must be localized precisely. Yet joint feed-forward estimators often rely on predefined object sets, while contact is typically predicted from image features alone, without the explicit geometry needed to localize it. To address both limitations, we present HORCRUX, a unified feed-forward transformer with coarse-to-fine contact estimation. For unseen objects, a frozen foundation model encodes the object from a given or image-reconstructed mesh rather than from object-specific knowledge. A shared transformer then reasons jointly over the object and one or two hands to keep them aligned. For precise contact, coarse per-joint predictions from latent features are resolved over finer hand regions using the reconstructed geometry. Across seven benchmarks, HORCRUX achieves state-of-the-art object and hand pose accuracy among joint estimators and outperforms a dedicated contact estimator in fine-grained contact. These results suggest that contact is best estimated jointly with reconstruction, whose geometry directly localizes it.

</details>


### [148] [SceneMemBench: A Benchmark for Scene Memory in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31803)
*Yuheng Si,Linye Lyu,Jiawei Zhou,Lingyi Du,Nanfei Ye,Aobo Liu,Yu Liu,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SceneMemBench基准，评估交互式视频世界模型的场景记忆，区分已见视角回忆与新视角恢复，并设计受控延续生成与完整生成两种评测方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆评测多关注已见视角重访和完整生成过程，未充分探索跨视角场景恢复，且将记忆与轨迹跟随误差、累积生成退化混淆。

Method: 构建包含33个Unreal Engine场景的600条受控轨迹和60个完整生成案例，覆盖8个代表性开源与闭源模型；采用共享真实观察历史的延续生成和完整模型交互生成两种协议，从主体一致性、外观保真度和几何一致性评估场景恢复。

Result: 新视角恢复通常比已见视角回忆更具挑战性；受控历史下的模型表现可能与完整交互中的表现显著不同。

Conclusion: 仅靠已见视角重访或端到端完整生成会掩盖互补的失败模式，SceneMemBench揭示了交互式视频世界模型场景记忆的不同维度缺陷。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate navigable worlds frame by frame, but a persistent world requires previously observed scenes to remain recoverable as users move away and return. Existing memory evaluations, however, often focus on seen-view revisits and full rollouts, leaving cross-view scene recovery underexplored while entangling memory with trajectory-following errors and accumulated generation degradation. We introduce SceneMemBench, a benchmark for scene memory in interactive video world models. It evaluates two complementary dimensions: seen-view recall versus novel-view recovery, and controlled diagnosis versus end-to-end interaction. We introduce Continued Rollout from a shared ground-truth observation history, alongside Full Rollout over complete model-generated interactions. SceneMemBench contains 600 controlled trajectories across 33 Unreal Engine scenes and 60 Full Rollout cases, covering eight representative open- and closed-source models. We evaluate scene recovery along subject consistency, appearance fidelity, and geometric consistency. Results show that novel-view recovery is generally more challenging than seen-view recall, while model profiles under controlled histories can differ markedly from those observed in full interaction. Together, these findings reveal complementary failure modes obscured by seen-view revisits or end-to-end rollouts alone.

</details>


### [149] [RoMo Hands: A Large Scale Richly Organized Text to Hand Motion Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31766)
*Yizhak Ben-Shabat,Jiahao Zhang,Joseph Liu,Seonghyeon Moon,Young-Yoon Lee,Haomiao Jiang,Oren Jacob,Mubbasir Kapadia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RoMo Hands大规模野外双手动作数据集及393维双手表示，并系统评测了扩散、掩码token和自回归生成器在双手动作合成上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯手部动作生成受限于数据：现有数据集将手视为刚性末端执行器、缺乏细粒度描述、规模不足以支持泛化。

Method: 构建约542K序列（884小时、9550万帧、30fps）的文本配对数据集，用严格灵巧度过滤器筛选复杂双手动作，每段配五级粒度描述并采用层级分类；提出393维双手表示以适配高自由度手部运动学；基准测试扩散、掩码token与自回归生成器。

Result: 数据集规模比先前工作大一个数量级；基准测试揭示不同生成器在手部特定适配下的明显权衡，尤其是语义对齐与运动学合理性之间的显著分歧。

Conclusion: 该数据集、表示与基准为下一代灵巧动作合成奠定了严谨基础。

Abstract: While text-driven body motion generation has matured, pure-hand synthesis remains bottlenecked by data. Existing datasets often treat hands as rigid end-effectors, lack fine-grained captions, or are too limited in scale to support robust generalization. We introduce RoMo Hands, an in-the-wild, text-paired dataset of ~542K sequences (884 hours, 95.5M frames at 30 fps) which is an order of magnitude larger than prior work. To ensure high-quality supervision, a stringent dexterity filter discards low-articulation sequences, focusing the distribution on complex bimanual movement. Each clip is paired with five captions at increasing granularities from global semantic tags to atomic finger states, organized under a hierarchical taxonomy. To bridge the gap between body and hand architectures, we propose a novel 393-D bimanual representation that adapts redundant body-motion paradigms to the high-DoF kinematics of hands. Systematic benchmarking of diffusion, masked-token, and autoregressive generators reveals distinct trade-offs under these hand-specific adaptations, including a sharp divergence between semantic alignment and kinematic plausibility. Our dataset, representation, and benchmark establish a rigorous foundation for the next generation of dexterous motion synthesis. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [150] [Xtream: An Explicit Metric State for Large-Scale Streaming Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31757)
*Ernesto Lozano,Alberto Jaenal,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Xtream提出一种混合流式流水线，通过关键帧因子图维护显式米制状态，并以3D基础模型作为几何前端，提升大尺度在线重建的轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DFM受跨视图注意力限制，视频块几何对齐加隐式记忆递归、或位姿图扩展局部一致性时易产生漂移，主因是单目几何缺乏米制尺度可观测性。

Method: 构建混合流式流水线：以3DFM作几何前端，用关键帧因子图维护显式米制状态；通过3DFM的米制尺度预测或可用惯性测量引入米制信息。

Result: 在VBR、Oxford Spires和KITTI大尺度基准上，Xtream在基于3DFM的在线重建中达到最先进性能，相比已发表基线和同期工作降低了平均绝对轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 将3DFM前端与关键帧因子图的显式米制状态结合，可有效缓解漂移并提升大尺度在线重建的轨迹精度。

Abstract: 3D foundation models (3DFMs) enable dense reconstruction in a feed-forward pass, yet their effective span is limited by cross-view attention. Local consistency has been expanded through geometric alignment of video chunks and recursive updates of an implicit memory, and large-scale scenes with pose graphs. However, both strategies are prone to drift, largely due to the lack of metric-scale observability from monocular geometry. We present Xtream,a hybrid streaming pipeline that maintains an explicit metric state through a factor graph over keyframes, while using a 3DFM as a geometric front end. Xtream incorporates metric information in two ways: through metric scale predictions from 3DFMs; or through inertial measurements if available. Across the large-scale benchmarks of VBR, Oxford Spires and KITTI, Xtream achieves state-of-the-art performance in 3DFM-based online reconstruction, reducing average absolute trajectory errors compared with published baselines and concurrent work. this https URL

</details>


### [151] [GeoMVGen: Joint Appearance-Geometry Modeling for Multi-View Video Generation in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31689)
*Qiaoqiao Jia,Guile Wu,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoMVGen框架，基于视频扩散变换器联合生成多视角RGB与点图视频，实现机器人操作中外观与几何一致的可控多视角4D生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需预测跨视角一致且随动作与相机配置可控的视觉外观与场景几何演化，而现有视频生成模型缺乏显式几何建模，几何感知与多视角方法难以在机器人条件下实现多视角时序一致的外观-几何联合生成。

Method: 基于视频扩散变换器，采用模态特定适配模块分别捕捉外观与几何特征，并通过跨模态残差连接交换信息；引入带互补几何监督的跨视角投影机制，结合点图一致性与几何基础模型引导的特征对齐以建立跨视角几何对应。

Result: 在包含仿真与真实机器人操作场景的4D视频生成数据集上，GeoMVGen在视频质量、几何精度与跨视角一致性上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 联合外观-几何建模与跨视角投影机制可有效实现机器人操作条件下时序一致、几何一致的可控多视角RGB与点图视频生成。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires predicting how robot-object interactions evolve over time, encompassing both visual appearance and scene geometry. In multi-camera settings, such predictions need to capture the evolving 3D scene consistently across viewpoints, while supporting controllable generation under different robot actions and camera configurations. Existing video generation models provide strong priors for visual appearance and motion but typically lack explicit geometric modeling. Meanwhile, recent advances in geometry-aware and multi-view generation have explored joint appearance-geometry modeling and cross-view consistency, yet generating temporally coherent appearance and geometry across multiple viewpoints under robotic manipulation conditions remains challenging. In this work, we propose GeoMVGen, a framework for joint multi-view RGB and pointmap video generation in robotic manipulation. Built on a video diffusion transformer, our framework employs modality-specific adaptation modules to capture the distinct characteristics of appearance and geometry, while using cross-modal residual connections to facilitate information exchange between the two modalities. To establish geometric correspondences across viewpoints, we further introduce a cross-view projection mechanism with complementary geometric supervisions, combining pointmap consistency with geometry foundation model-guided feature alignment. These designs enable controllable generation of temporally evolving multi-view observations with coherent appearance and geometrically consistent scene structures. Extensive experiments on 4D video generation dataset comprising simulated and real-world robotic manipulation scenarios demonstrate that GeoMVGen outperforms state-of-the-art methods in video quality, geometric accuracy, and cross-view consistency.

</details>


### [152] [Ground, Guide, Act: Improving VLA Spatial Generalization with Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31452)
*Guangting Zheng,Shaoxuan Wang,Yu Yuan,Zhipeng Tang,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MFM-VLA选择性协作框架，通过多模态基础模型提供目标定位与空间引导，结合VLA的精细操作能力，提升视觉-语言-动作模型的空间泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型能从机器人演示中学习动作策略，但在物体位置和任务目标变化时泛化能力差；通用多模态基础模型具备互补的场景理解与空间推理能力，但直接用于精细控制慢且不可靠。

Method: 通过原子阶段干预系统性分析多种VLA架构的能力瓶颈，发现失败主要源于目标定位和全局空间路由不佳；据此提出MFM-VLA框架：MFM负责目标定位与空间引导，控制器执行自由空间运动，VLA保留精细操作；并用动作块级密集几何描述和扰动状态恢复数据微调VLA，同时采用开发校准的能力路由器在直接执行与MFM辅助执行间选择。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO、ALOHA-Sim-PRO、RoboCasa365和真实化学任务上，MFM-VLA相比对应基础VLA的成功率分别提升14.9、74.0、3.6和30.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 选择性结合多模态基础模型的空间推理能力与VLA的局部操作能力，可有效提升VLA的空间泛化性能，避免不必要的模型干预。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models effectively learn action policies from robot demonstrations, but struggle to generalize to changes in object positions and task goals. General multimodal foundation models (MFMs) offer complementary scene understanding and spatial reasoning, but remain slow and unreliable for direct fine-grained control. To understand how MFMs can improve VLA spatial generalization, we conduct a systematic capability analysis across multiple VLA architectures using atomic-stage interventions, revealing that many failures stem from poor target grounding and global spatial routing, while local manipulation skills remain largely usable. Based on these findings, we propose MFM-VLA, a selective collaboration framework in which an MFM provides target grounding and spatial guidance, controllers execute free-space motion, and the VLA retains control over fine-grained manipulation. We fine-tune the VLA with action-chunk-level dense geometric captions and perturbed-state recovery data to improve local guidance following and recovery from unfamiliar handoff states. A development-calibrated capability router selects between direct VLA execution and MFM-assisted execution to avoid unnecessary intervention. Across LIBERO-PRO, ALOHA-Sim-PRO, RoboCasa365, and real-world chemistry tasks, MFM-VLA improves success rates by 14.9, 74.0, 3.6, and 30.9 percentage points, respectively, over the corresponding base VLA. The project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [153] [VidForce: Predicting Force-Aware Articulation by Observing Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32967)
*Rafay Mohiuddin,Jiaqi Chen,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VidForce从第三人称RGB视频估计人-物交互轨迹与力曲线，借助物理原型先验利用有限力测量监督，并部署于Spot机器人实现铰接物体操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过观察他人可预判物体运动与交互所需力，但力测量需专用设备且数量有限，因此需要从视频中学习这种物理理解。

Method: 提出VidForce：以学习到的物理原型作为先验，原型在可比交互间共享力水平与时间曲线，由视觉与语义线索决定其贡献；结合几何与视觉-语言特征自适应调整先验，并用联合条件流同时精炼初始跟踪轨迹与力曲线；可选输入2D接触点与文本动作提示，配对的人体与力传感器夹爪记录提供监督，另训练扭矩模型验证可扩展性。

Result: 在Hoi!数据集留出交互上，ATE为6.6 cm，峰值力MAE与RMSE分别为4.81 N与5.92 N，力曲线RMSE为5.52 N；真实视频中运动与力推断稳定一致；部署于Spot机器人，由人类演示估计的轨迹与力曲线驱动物体操作并报告多次试验成功率。

Conclusion: VidForce能有效从视频估计交互轨迹与力，利用有限测量与原型先验实现可靠泛化，并可迁移至真实机器人操作任务。

Abstract: People can anticipate how an object moves and the effort an interaction may require by watching others. How can embodied agents acquire this understanding from video? Force measurements provide direct sensory supervision, but require specialized equipment and remain limited in quantity. We introduce VidForce, which estimates an interaction trajectory together with a force profile from a third-person RGB video; 2D contact clicks and textual object-action prompts are optional. Our approach makes limited instrumented measurements useful for interpreting human interactions through learned physical prototypes as a prior. The prototypes share learned force levels and temporal profiles across interactions of comparable effort, with visual and semantic cues determining their contribution to each observation. Geometric and vision-language features adapt this prior to the observed interaction, while a joint conditional flow refines an initial tracked trajectory together with the force profile. Paired human and instrumented-gripper recordings provide measured force references for supervising human video, and a separately trained torque model demonstrates that the formulation extends to other sensory quantities. We evaluate trajectory recovery and force estimation through baseline comparisons and controlled ablations on held-out interactions from the Hoi! dataset. On this evaluation subset, VidForce achieves 6.6 cm ATE, peak-force MAE and RMSE of 4.81 N and 5.92 N, respectively, and force-profile RMSE of 5.52 N. On real-world videos, VidForce infers motion and force reliably and consistently despite the variability in human execution. We further deploy VidForce on a Spot robot, where trajectories and force profiles estimated from human demonstrations drive real-world articulated-object manipulation, and report success rates across repeated trials. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [154] [DOPER: Deep Object Pose Estimation Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32846)
*Hugo Hadfield,Alperen Degirmenci,Jonathan Tremblay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DOPER是一个实时RGB 6D位姿估计框架，通过共享视觉骨干联合学习目标检测、分割与关键点预测，并结合多视图几何约束实现从合成到真实的适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6D位姿估计方法通常依赖深度输入或学习式位姿精化器，且难以在仅RGB条件下兼顾检测与关键点对应关系；同时合成到真实的域适应仍具挑战。

Method: 构建共享视觉骨干的多任务网络，联合执行目标检测、分割和关键点预测；从合成训练出发，利用校准多视图观测提供的几何约束和部分监督进行真实域适应，并保留合成关键点标签；推理时可选热图精化，直接对预测关键点分布拟合刚性位姿，无需深度或学习式位姿精化器；从欠定CAD模型开始逐步精化物体表示与位姿估计器。

Result: 在SPEED+、SHIRT上验证了对光照变化的适应和时间位姿估计的准确性；在HANDAL、HOPEv2、HOT3D、T-LESS的单视图与多视图配置下取得最先进检测AP，仅用RGB且实时运行（单GB300上每个物体模型35 Hz）。

Conclusion: 联合学习检测与位姿关键点、结合真实观测和几何约束是有效策略，可在仅RGB条件下实现实时且高精度的6D位姿估计。

Abstract: We present DOPER, an RGB pose estimation framework that shares a visual backbone across object detection, segmentation, and keypoint prediction to recover the 6D pose of known objects through Perspective-n-Point. Starting from synthetic training, DOPER adapts to real observations using partial supervision while retaining synthetic keypoint labels. Calibrated multi-view observations provide geometric constraints for keypoint supervision. During inference, optional heatmap refinement fits a rigid pose directly to the predicted keypoint distributions, resolving ambiguous correspondences without depth input or a learned pose refiner. Starting from underspecified CAD models, DOPER progressively refines the object representation and pose estimator. Results on SPEED+ and SHIRT further demonstrate effective adaptation to illumination changes and accurate temporal pose estimation. Across the evaluated single-view and multi-view configurations, DOPER achieves state-of-the-art detection AP results on HANDAL, HOPEv2, HOT3D, and T-LESS while using only RGB and running in real time (35 Hz per object model on one GB300). These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning detection and pose keypoints, with real observations and geometric constraints.

</details>


### [155] [LaWanle: Why Can't Leading Driving Models Make a Simple Turn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32650)
*Chengzhi Gao,Junzhe Gao,Yongzhi Liu,Weichao Zhuang,Weilong Hu,Taonian Liang,Guodong Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 领先端到端驾驶模型在NAVISIM接近人类，却在闭环仿真中无法可靠完成简单90度转弯；根因是交汇处对初始状态敏感，仅微调横向路径即可大幅提升通过率，但无法达到人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为何在NAVISIM上表现接近人类的端到端驾驶模型，在逼真闭环仿真器中连基本90度转弯都不可靠。

Method: 构建LaWanle转弯基准（来自三个仿真器，多起点驾驶）；对开源车道保持模型用闭环DAgger在其横向路径上后训练（仅训练5%参数）；并在恢复数据上进一步微调，与等时长对照组比较。

Result: 同一模型因起点不同而通过或失败，进入路口前行驶相似；入口处旋转几度即可致失败，轻微航向修正可挽救约一半失败转弯；后训练使转弯通过率从19%升至76%，增益迁移至另外两个基准且位列领先模型；恢复数据微调未在三个基准上提升转弯，所有变体仍远低于人类驾驶员。

Conclusion: 端到端驾驶模型的转弯失败源于闭环中对细微状态偏差的敏感性；针对横向路径的闭环后训练可有效且可迁移地提升转弯能力，但恢复数据微调无效，且与人类驾驶员仍有显著差距。

Abstract: We were surprised to find that leading end-to-end driving models across major paradigms, despite scoring close to human drivers on NAVSIM, cannot reliably make a simple 90-degree turn in photorealistic closed-loop simulators. To investigate why, we build LaWanle, a benchmark of turns drawn from three such simulators. Driving each turn from several starts, we find that the same model passes it from one start and fails it from another, and that the two drives look alike until they enter the junction. Rotating the car by a few degrees at the junction entry can fail a turn it otherwise passes. Conversely, a slight heading correction to the plans can rescue about half of the failed turns we tested. Such errors arise when a model drives by itself, so we post-train an open-source lane-keeping model with closed-loop DAgger on its lateral path. Training 5% of its parameters is enough to raise its turn pass rate from 19% to 76%. The gain transfers to the two other benchmarks, where the policy also scores among the leading models. Further fine-tuning on recovery data repairs the deviations it is trained on but, against a control trained equally long, improves turns on none of the three benchmarks, and every variant stays far below a human driver.

</details>


### [156] [ICLWorld: Benchmarking In-Context Learning of Agents from Digital to Physical Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32649)
*Qingle Liu,Jieming Li,Pei-lin Li,Shenghan Zheng,Ruize Yang,Sunqi Fan,Deyao Hong,Huan-ang Gao,Meng-Hao Guo,Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在线基准ICLWorld与统一测试框架UIverse，从数字与物理世界评测前沿智能体的上下文学习能力，发现电脑使用成功率约八成而机器人使用仅约两成，且“识别规则”与“执行规则”之间存在显著落差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文学习评测多为离线、依赖文本或静态图像，存在训练数据污染风险，机器人评测仅停留在方法级消融；而同一批前沿智能体已同时操作电脑与机器人，其上下文学习能力尚未被充分评估。

Method: 构建30个环境、录制630段演示视频（10小时以上）、设计160个任务，每个任务配有文本或视频形式的三版定制且常反直觉的规则；通过UIverse将各环境统一为用户界面实现在线评测；在回放演示、新实例、未见环境三个层级测试泛化，并设置无规则对照，评测六个前沿模型。

Result: Claude Opus 5.5与GPT-6 Astra在电脑使用任务上成功率为82%和76%，机器人使用任务上仅27%和26%；无规则对照成功率最高仅8%。GPT-6 Astra在三版规则下均成功的电脑任务仅38%，为单规则成功率的一半；机器人规则问答正确率98%但任务完成率仅20%。前沿智能体从视频与文本学规则效果相当且可跨环境迁移，较弱智能体依赖文本，演示情境重采样后视频学习成功率大幅下降。

Conclusion: 评测揭示了数字世界与物理世界之间、以及“识别规则”与“依据规则行动”之间的显著能力差距，凸显当前前沿智能体上下文学习的不足。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets agents acquire new rules from context and adapt to user-specific requirements at inference time. The same frontier agents now operate both computers and robots, yet their ICL remains insufficiently assessed: existing benchmarks are largely offline, rely on text or static images, and risk training-data contamination, while robot evaluations are limited to method-level ablations. We present ICLWorld, an online benchmark that evaluates the ICL of agents from digital to physical worlds, covering computer use and robot use, together with UIverse, a unified harness that turns every environment into a user interface. We build 30 environments, record 630 demonstration videos (10+ hours), and design 160 tasks with customized, often counter-intuitive rules that cannot be satisfied without learning from the context. Each task comes with three rule versions, given as text or video, and we test generalization at three levels, from replaying the demonstration to new instances and unseen environments. Across six frontier models, the strongest agents, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6 Astra, succeed on 82% and 76% of computer-use runs but only 27% and 26% of robot-use runs, and a no-rule control reduces success to at most 8%. GPT-6 Astra succeeds under all three rule versions on only 38% of computer-use tasks, half its rate under a single rule, and answers 98% of robot rule questions correctly yet completes only 20% of these tasks. Frontier agents learn rules comparably well from video and from text and transfer them across environments, whereas weaker agents rely on text and lose most of their video-based success once the demonstrated situation is resampled. These findings reveal substantial gaps between the digital and the physical world and between identifying a rule and acting on it.

</details>


### [157] [GOLD-SLAM: Gaussian-splatting Omnidirectional LiDAR-Depth SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32335)
*Weicong Zhang,Yuan Huang,Yu Zheng,Lin Chenxiao,Yingying Li,Lilong Fan,Liangdong Zhuang,Yufan Chen,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Jiaming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOLD-SLAM 是一个全景高斯泼溅 SLAM 系统，融合 LiDAR 深度先验与全景视觉，实现高质量渲染和精确几何重建，并在 UAV 数据集 WHOLE 上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景相机的球面覆盖范围与 LiDAR 视场不匹配，导致依赖 LiDAR 深度先验的全景高斯泼溅 SLAM 面临挑战，需要一种能在球面域进行跟踪与建图并弥补 LiDAR 覆盖不足的方法。

Method: 提出 GOLD-SLAM，在球面域执行跟踪与建图，利用无 LiDAR 覆盖区域的跟踪视觉特征改进位姿估计；引入深度感知地图扩展与剪枝（DAEP）处理新观测表面覆盖不足和浮空伪影；采集 WHOLE 数据集（含弱几何、高动态、全向、低光和简单场景），并在 Jetson Orin NX 上评估嵌入式配置。

Result: 在公开基准和 WHOLE 上，GOLD-SLAM 渲染质量具有竞争力，几何精度优于透视系统；相比现有全景基线，平均 PSNR 提升 5.56–9.10 dB，平均 Depth-L1 降低 71.9–83.9%；嵌入式配置可在 Jetson Orin NX（16 GB）上在线运行，但重建质量有所下降。

Conclusion: GOLD-SLAM 有效结合全景视觉与 LiDAR 深度先验，提升了全景 SLAM 的渲染与几何精度，并具备在资源受限 UAV 平台部署的可行性。

Abstract: Panoramic cameras capture nearly the entire spherical field of view in a single frame. However, the mismatch between this panoramic coverage and the field of view of LiDAR poses a substantial challenge for panoramic Gaussian splatting SLAM that relies on LiDAR depth priors. We introduce GOLD-SLAM, a Gaussian-splatting Omnidirectional LiDAR-Depth SLAM system that combines high-quality rendering with accurate geometric reconstruction. It performs tracking and mapping in the spherical domain and uses tracked visual features in regions without LiDAR coverage to improve pose estimation. To address insufficient coverage of newly observed surfaces and floating artifacts during incremental mapping, we introduce Depth-Aware Map Extension and Pruning (DAEP). To enable broader evaluation, we collect a new dataset, WHOLE, using a UAV platform across Weak-geometry, High-dynamic, Omnidirectional, Low-light, and Easy scenarios. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and WHOLE demonstrate competitive rendering quality and improved geometric accuracy relative to perspective systems. Compared with existing panoramic baselines, GOLD-SLAM improves mean PSNR by 5.56-9.10 dB and reduces mean Depth-L1 by 71.9-83.9%. To assess deployment feasibility on resource-constrained platforms such as UAVs, we evaluate an embedded configuration of GOLD-SLAM on a Jetson Orin NX (16 GB), demonstrating online operation at the cost of reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [158] [Seeing Is Not Reaching: Instruction Shortcuts Override Visual Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32206)
*Qing Zhang,Xuesong Li,Zhaoyuan Yang,Changlin Song,Xinyu Tian,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉-语言-动作模型在物体移动后仍会伸向训练时的位置，作者识别出场景捷径和指令捷径并解释其机制，提出无需训练的路径分离引导方法PSG显著提升目标到达率与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLAs本应继承视觉语言模型的视觉定位能力，但在物体被移动后往往仍伸向训练时的位置，说明定位能力可能未被有效利用或被其他通路覆盖，需要明确其机制并加以改进。

Method: 在六个VLA上识别出场景捷径与指令捷径，并在π0.5中通过分别掩蔽图像介导的定位通路和直接的指令到动作通路来判别定位能力是被闲置还是被覆盖；据此提出无需训练、无需外部目标位置的Pathway-Separated Guidance（PSG），放大定位信号并抑制指令捷径。

Result: 掩蔽定位通路会消除对移动目标的伸取，而掩蔽直接通路会增加对移动目标的伸取，表明定位已在引导动作但常被指令捷径通过直接通路覆盖。在LIBERO-PRO Object的位置扰动下，PSG将目标到达率从22.0%提升至52.6%，任务成功率从16.6%提升至40.8%，同时基本保持原任务性能。

Conclusion: VLA的定位能力并非缺失而是被指令捷径覆盖，通过分离并调节定位与直接指令到动作两条通路，可在不训练的情况下显著改善物体移动后的目标伸取与任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are expected to inherit visual grounding from vision-language models, yet often reach for an object's training location after it has been moved. Across six VLAs, we identify two shortcuts: a scene shortcut, where the arm follows the scene's original task despite a new instruction, and an instruction shortcut, where the instruction retrieves the requested object's training location. In $\pi_{0.5}$, the instruction shortcut persists even though object-name attention follows the moved target. We call this Seeing Is Not Reaching. To determine whether this grounding is unused or overridden, we separately mask an image-mediated grounding pathway and a direct instruction-to-action pathway. Masking the grounding pathway eliminates reaches to moved targets, whereas masking the direct pathway increases them. Thus, grounding already guides action but is often overridden by the instruction shortcut, which acts through the direct pathway. We introduce Pathway-Separated Guidance (PSG), which amplifies grounding and suppresses the shortcut without training or external target locations. On LIBERO-PRO Object under Position perturbations, PSG raises the target-reaching rate from 22.0% to 52.6% and task success from 16.6% to 40.8%, while largely preserving performance on the original tasks.

</details>


### [159] [RoboProbe: Benchmarking Physical Plausibility of Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32152)
*Zhuoying Yang,Jiachen Ni,Yichen Guo,Xiaoshan Wu,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套用于评估生成视频物理合理性的指标套件和基准，以支持可靠的机器人规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成的视频用于在真实执行前进行动作提议和评估，但当前视频生成器常产生物理上不可信的交互，而视觉语言模型（VLM）难以直接检测这些违规，因为物理验证需要基于证据的时空推理。

Method: 开发了五个针对特定物理定律的指标（接触保真度、物体持久性、固体性、机器人变形、物体变形），结合分割、跟踪、3D几何或外观比较的时空证据与定向VLM推理；并引入RoboProbe代理评估框架和RP-Bench基准（含761个人工标注生成视频和130个真实演示，覆盖12个机器人数据集的239个操作任务）。

Result: 在RP-Bench上，五个指标的AUROC均超过0.85，优于所评估的学习评判器、先前评估器以及仅使用帧的Gemini基线；应用于五个视频生成器揭示了不同模型在违规类型和频率上的明显模式。

Conclusion: 该指标套件和评估框架能有效检测生成视频中的物理违规，为机器人世界模型的可靠性评估提供了工具。

Abstract: Evaluating the physical plausibility of generated videos is critical for reliable robotic planning with world models. These videos inform action proposal and assessment before real-world execution, so undetected physical violations can lead to erroneous assessments of action feasibility and safety. Yet current video generators often produce physically implausible interactions. Detecting such violations directly with vision-language models (VLMs) remains challenging as physical verification requires evidence-grounded spatiotemporal reasoning beyond high-level semantic understanding. We develop five law-specific metrics for evaluating physical plausibility by combining spatiotemporal evidence from segmentation, tracking, 3D geometry, or appearance comparisons with targeted VLM reasoning: Contact fidelity, Object persistence, Solidity, Robot deformation, and Object deformation. Building on this metric suite, we introduce RoboProbe, an agentic evaluation framework that coordinates metric execution and selectively refines evaluations through constrained actions. To evaluate the physical plausibility of robot world models, we further introduce RP-Bench, with 761 human-labeled generated videos and 130 real demonstrations of 239 manipulation tasks from 12 robot datasets. On RP-Bench, all five metrics achieve AUROCs above 0.85 and outperform the evaluated learned judges, prior evaluators, and a frames-only Gemini baseline. Applying these metrics to five video generators reveals distinct patterns of physical violations across models, in both violation types and frequencies.

</details>


### [160] [View Management with Gaussian Placement and Opacity Regularization for Online 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32025)
*Dong Hwi Kim,Park Sangwon,Chaehyeon Song,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对在线3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）SLAM中地图过拟合稀疏输入的问题，从增加视图监督和控制模型复杂度两方面改进，提升了在线地图的外观与几何质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时应用需要在线建图，3DGS与SLAM结合虽能提供相机位姿和初始高斯，但现有地图优化沿用传统SLAM设计：仅在用于定位的关键帧上训练，并用不确定的前端深度正则化几何。这忽视了3DGS地图的高模型复杂度（数百万参数但每区域视图稀少），导致地图对稀疏输入过拟合；而每个新增视图和高斯都争夺同一实时预算，解决该问题具有挑战性。

Method: 从两方面入手：1）在关键帧保持地图结构的前提下，随着建图更新完成逐步接纳非关键帧图像，并根据各组帧提供的监督量将有限更新分配到不同帧组；2）采用边缘引导初始化，在图像细节需要处放置新高斯以控制模型复杂度；3）提出自参考不透明度正则化（SOR），利用累积渲染证据（无额外渲染开销）抑制持续出现在地图导出表面参考前方的高斯的不透明度，以补充不确定的前端深度。

Result: 这些设计共同在实时预算内同时改善了在线地图的外观和几何质量。

Conclusion: 通过逐步引入非关键帧监督、边缘引导初始化和自参考不透明度正则化，可在实时约束下有效缓解3DGS在线地图对稀疏输入的过拟合，提升在线建图的外观与几何表现。

Abstract: The realistic rendering of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has drawn interest from real-time applications that require maps to be built online. This led to coupling 3DGS with SLAM, providing camera poses and initial Gaussians for the map. However, the map is still optimized with designs inherited from conventional SLAM, which train only on keyframes selected for localization and regularize geometry with uncertain frontend depth. These designs overlook the model complexity of a 3DGS map: with millions of parameters but few views of each region, the map overfits to sparse inputs. Resolving this is challenging, as every view and Gaussian added to the map competes for the same real-time budget. We close this gap from both sides. To add views while keyframes preserve the structure of the map, we progressively admit non-keyframe images as mapping updates complete and allocate the limited updates across different groups of frames according to the supervision each group provides. To control the model complexity of the map, edge-guided initialization places new Gaussians where image detail requires them. To complement the uncertain frontend depth, Self-Referenced Opacity Regularization (SOR) uses accumulated rendering evidence, at no extra rendering cost, to suppress the opacity of Gaussians that persistently contribute in front of a map-derived surface reference. Together, these designs improve both the appearance and the geometry of online maps within the real-time budget.

</details>


### [161] [TACTUS: Unifying Surgical Action Understanding in Time and Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31956)
*Lurui Wang,Hao Ding,Chenhao Yu,Chenyan Jing,Jiru Xu,Ruoqi Zuo,Liwei Mei,Chang Liu,Lalithkumar Seenivasan,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TACTUS数据集与基准，统一标注手术动作的工具、动作、接触和轨迹时空信息，并给出参考网络TactusNet及多基线评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术基准多以任务特定目标定义监督，未完整刻画动作语义、器械运动与组织交互之间的关系，难以支撑手术自动化的时空理解。

Method: 构建统一标注框架，联合标注工具与动作身份、嵌套动作与交互窗口、器械关键点轨迹及组织区域跟踪；采用人工标注、点跟踪与模型生成空间提示相结合的两级流水线，重标注5个公开来源的141段视频；并提出参考网络TactusNet统一进行语义、时间与空间预测。

Result: 共产生22,995个动作片段、290万帧，含15,668个Gold片段和7,327个Silver片段；TactusNet与13个基线中，逐头编码器微调下工具-动作准确率达95.8%，最佳时间召回为46.6%（temporal IoU 0.7），接触Dice为0.351；加入Silver使默认TactusNet配对准确率从93.8%提升至95.5%。

Conclusion: TACTUS为学习和评估器械-组织交互提供了统一基础，可支撑未来手术自动化的研究。

Abstract: Advancing surgical automation requires a comprehensive spatio-temporal understanding of how instruments act on tissue throughout an action. Existing surgical benchmarks, however, largely define supervision through task-specific targets, leaving the relationships between action semantics, instrument motion, and tissue interaction incompletely specified. To address this limitation, we introduce TACTUS (Tool, Action, Contact and Trajectory Understanding in Surgery), a new dataset and benchmark that establishes a unified annotation framework for complete surgical actions. The framework jointly specifies tool and action identities, nested action and interaction windows, instrument-specific landmark trajectories, and tissue-region tracks. To construct these annotations at scale, we develop a two-tier pipeline combining human annotation, point tracking, and model-generated spatial prompts. Using this pipeline, we re-annotate 141 videos from five public sources, producing 22,995 action clips spanning 2.9 million frames. The dataset comprises 15,668 Gold clips with human spatial prompts followed by review and re-checking, and 7,327 Silver clips combining human semantic and temporal annotations with model-generated spatial prompts. We further introduce TactusNet, the companion reference network, to benchmark semantic, temporal, and spatial prediction from shared video features. Across TactusNet and 13 published baselines, tool-action accuracy reaches 95.8% with per-head encoder fine-tuning, while the best temporal recall and contact Dice reach 46.6% at temporal IoU 0.7 and 0.351, respectively. Moreover, adding Silver improves the default TactusNet's pair accuracy from 93.8% to 95.5%. Together, these contributions establish a foundation for learning and evaluating the instrument-tissue interactions underlying future surgical automation.

</details>


### [162] [VLG-Drive: Unifying Vision-Language and Geometric Priors for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31952)
*Junge Zhang,Jie Zhu,Yanqi Jia,Xiangyu Xu,Zixun Huang,Wenbin He,Xin Li,Jiachen Li,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLG-Drive通过冻结视觉语言模型与几何基础模型、仅训练融合接口和规划器，在NavHard和NavTest上取得领先的驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶规划器需同时具备场景语义（来自视觉语言模型）与空间结构（来自几何基础模型），但直接适配任一模型用于规划代价高，因此探索冻结两模型、轻量融合的替代方案。

Method: 提出VLG-Drive：保持VLM与几何模型冻结，仅从轨迹标签训练融合接口和规划器；在每个相机网格单元通过几何锚定残差将成对的VLM与几何特征融合为联合token，再由指令条件规划器读取token预测自车轨迹。

Result: 在扰动初始状态下的NavHard上达到31.78 EPDMS（对比方法中最高），仅用相机在NavTest上达到88.27 PDMS；逐点融合在两基准上均优于独立VLM流与几何流；在420个HUGSIM场景闭环驾驶中优于所有对比规划器。

Conclusion: 基于冻结视觉语言与几何基础模型融合特征的轻量规划器，是替代适配任一模型用于规划的有效方案。

Abstract: A driving planner needs both scene semantics, which vision-language models (VLMs) provide, and spatial structure, which geometric foundation models recover from visual history. We present VLG-Drive, which keeps both models frozen and trains only a fusion interface and a planner from trajectory labels. At each camera-grid cell, the interface fuses the paired VLM and geometry features into one joint token through a geometry-anchored residual, and a command-conditioned planner reads these tokens to predict the ego trajectory. VLG-Drive achieves 31.78 EPDMS on NavHard under perturbed initial states, the highest among the compared methods, and 88.27 PDMS on NavTest from cameras alone. Pointwise fusion outperforms independent VLM and geometry streams on both benchmarks. In closed-loop driving on 420 HUGSIM scenarios, VLG-Drive outperforms all compared planners. These results indicate that a lightweight planner trained on fused features of frozen vision-language and geometric foundation models is an effective alternative to adapting either model for planning.

</details>


### [163] [DUALingo: A Dual-Hand Instruction-Guided Dataset for Affordance Grounding on 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31919)
*Ming-Lun Lee,Yi-En Li,Feng-Dian Su,Ming-Han Lee,Kun-Ru Wu,Krzysztof Czarnecki,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DUALingo大规模数据集，用自动化流程生成任务条件下的双手协同3D可供性定位标注，并验证其可训练现有模型实现三倍以上性能提升与机器人操作可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D可供性数据集主要标注单一交互区域，而日常操作任务常需双手互补协作；人工标注双手协同接触区域难以规模化。

Method: 构建DUALingo数据集：将每个任务表示为两个协同的、与具体手无关的角色，每个角色在物体表面以热图形式定位；包含71K物体上的271K自由形式任务，由开放权重基础模型组成的全自动流程生成，无需人工标注，并使用专家评分和经过验证的LLM评判确认标注质量。

Result: 在DUALingo上训练后，现有3D可供性模型能从自然语言指令定位两个协同角色；最佳模型aIoU从零样本基线的10.3提升至37.8，提升超过三倍；机器人操作演示验证了预测的双手可供性在执行协同任务中的可操作性。

Conclusion: DUALingo可作为学习日常双手可供性的可扩展基础数据集，弥补双手协同3D可供性标注的空白。

Abstract: Affordance grounding localizes where an agent should interact with an object to perform a task. Many everyday manipulation tasks, however, require bimanual affordance, in which two hands assume complementary and cooperative roles to accomplish a shared goal. Existing 3D affordance datasets primarily ground single interaction regions, and manually annotating dual-hand, cooperative contacts does not scale. To address this gap, we introduce DUALingo, a large-scale dataset for task-conditioned, role-aware bimanual 3D affordance grounding on diverse everyday objects. DUALingo represents each task as two cooperative, hand-agnostic roles, each grounded as a heatmap on the object surface. It contains 271K free-form tasks on 71K objects, generated by a fully automated pipeline of open-weight foundation models without human labeling. Expert ratings and a validated LLM judge confirm the quality of the resulting annotations. Trained on DUALingo, existing 3D affordance models learn to ground both cooperative roles from natural-language instructions, with the best-performing model achieving a more than threefold improvement in aIoU over the zero-shot baseline, from 10.3 to 37.8. Robotic manipulation demonstrations illustrate the actionability of the predicted bimanual affordances in performing cooperative tasks. These results position DUALingo as a scalable foundation for learning everyday bimanual affordances. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [164] [Beyond Expert Replay: Cross-Instruction Anchoring for Continual Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31903)
*Ziyang Chen,Dehua Chen,Shaoguang Wang,Xu Yan,Hao Huang,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Cross-Instruction Anchoring (XIA)，通过在任务边界冻结旧策略作为教师，将旧指令与当前观测配对进行响应匹配，补充经验回放之外的指令条件监督，缓解VLA模型在有限回放下的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需持续学习新操作技能并保留旧指令跟随能力；经验回放仅保留少量历史专家帧，导致大量旧指令响应缺乏直接监督，出现旧指令触发新学竞争目标的结构性失效模式。

Method: 在任务边界冻结上一策略为教师，将其对“旧指令+当前观测”组合的响应作为监督信号，与专家回放数据联合训练，扩展监督范围而无需额外专家回放数据。

Result: 在多种VLA架构和三个LIBERO套件上，相同历史专家数据预算下成功率提升、遗忘减少；RoboCasa及真实世界实验进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: XIA通过指令条件响应保持有效弥补有限回放下旧指令监督不足，是一种可跨架构泛化、数据高效的持续学习方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models need to continually acquire new manipulation skills while retaining the ability to follow previously learned instructions. Experience replay (ER) can mitigate catastrophic forgetting, but retaining only a small number of historical expert frames per task leaves many old-instruction responses without direct supervision. Under limited replay, we observe a structured failure mode in which an earlier instruction can trigger a subsequently learned, competing goal. To address this gap, we propose Cross-Instruction Anchoring (XIA), which supplements expert replay with instruction-conditioned response preservation. At each task boundary, XIA freezes the previous policy as a teacher and matches its responses to old instructions paired with current observations. This extends supervision beyond stored historical examples without requiring additional expert replay data. Experiments across multiple VLA architectures and three LIBERO suites demonstrate improved success rates and reduced forgetting compared with ER under identical historical expert-data budgets. Additional RoboCasa and real-world experiments further support the effectiveness of XIA.

</details>


### [165] [G0.7: Scaling Up a Fast World Action Model with Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31822)
*Tianyuan Yuan,Baijun Ye,Shicheng Cao,Xing Nie,Xiaopeng Yu,Ke Dong,Zijie Zhao,Shenling Qiu,Yizhao Xu,Feiyang Xue,Haonan Liu,Haoyu Zhang,Haobo Jia,Zheng Liu,Anqi Yang,Jiahui Niu,Yue Sun,Tailai Cheng,Jianpeng Yu,Yang Wen,Longhao Wei,Zibin Dong,Shiduo Zhang,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G0.7是一个通用世界动作模型，将上下文窗口扩展至最多五分钟历史，并大规模预训练于机器人遥操作与人类第一视角视频，部署时跳过未来帧生成直接解码动作，实现60毫秒/16步动作块（比IDM快7.6倍）的实时闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型需在每步动作前生成未来帧，导致测试时推理过慢，难以用于闭环控制；且现有策略多局限于短观测历史。Fast-WAM表明视频预测仅在训练时塑造策略表征、部署时可跳过帧合成，本文据此扩展模型规模与能力。

Method: 在Fast-WAM基础上提出G0.7：将上下文窗口从单帧观测扩展至最多五分钟历史；将预训练扩展至大规模机器人遥操作与人类第一视角（ego）视频语料；采用直接动作解码（Fast模式），部署时跳过未来帧合成。

Result: 在公开仿真套件与真实机器人部署中，Fast模式与同一检查点的IDM推理效果相当，单张RTX 5090上每16步动作块仅需60毫秒（比IDM快7.6倍），支持无停顿实时分块；性能随预训练数据稳定提升，人类ego视频带来最大分布外泛化增益；五个仿真基准平均得分70.8，Galaxea R1 Pro与R1 Lite零样本抓放成功率48%和54%，视频条件上下文学习任务分布内96.7%、分布外93.3%，七项真实世界后训练任务平均进度86.9%（含多分钟折箱、饮品制备、产品装袋）。

Conclusion: 视频预测的作用在于训练阶段塑造策略表征而非部署时合成未来帧，据此可在保持性能的同时大幅加速推理；扩展历史上下文与人类ego视频预训练能一致提升性能并显著增强分布外泛化，使通用世界动作模型具备实时闭环控制能力。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) adapt pretrained video generators for robot control by jointly predicting future camera frames and actions, transferring physical priors learned from video into manipulation policies. However, generating future frames before every action makes test-time inference too slow for closed-loop control, while most existing policies remain restricted to short observation histories. Building on Fast-WAM, which showed that video prediction shapes policy representations during training so that future frame synthesis can be skipped at deployment, we present G0.7, a generalist world action model scaled along two axes: extending the context window from a single observation to up to five minutes of history, and scaling pretraining to a large-scale corpus of robot teleoperation and human egocentric (ego) video. Across public simulation suites and real-robot deployments, direct action decoding (Fast mode) matches imagine-then-act (IDM) inference from the same checkpoint while running in 60 ms per 16-step action chunk on a single RTX 5090 (7.6x faster than IDM) and supporting pause-free real-time chunking. Performance scales consistently with pretraining data, with human ego video driving the largest gains in out-of-distribution generalization: G0.7 achieves a 70.8 mean score across five simulation benchmark suites, 48% and 54% zero-shot pick-and-place success on Galaxea R1 Pro and R1 Lite, 96.7% in-distribution and 93.3% out-of-distribution success on video-conditioned in-context learning tasks, and 86.9% mean task progress across seven real-world post-training tasks including multi-minute box folding, drink preparation, and product bagging.

</details>


### [166] [LeanWAM: Learning Latent World Action Models from Frozen Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31740)
*Shaolong Li,Hongze Fu,Joyce Chai,Yinpei Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LeanWAM，一种两阶段训练的轻量级潜在世界动作模型，通过将互补的未来表示压缩为少量token并蒸馏到策略中，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低训练和部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）通常将大型预训练视频生成器适配为联合预测未来观测和机器人动作，训练和部署成本高昂；且尚不清楚策略实际需要哪些未来表示以及能以多紧凑的形式表示。

Method: 探测研究表明互补的未来表示可压缩为少量token并保留大部分动作解码性能；据此提出LeanWAM，两阶段训练：第一阶段由Future Extractor将观测到的转移压缩为32个预测token，这些token以动作、RGB、语义和几何监督共同约束冻结的视频生成器，并从生成器隐藏状态的紧凑读出获得补充的世界token；第二阶段将两种表示蒸馏到策略中，使其从当前观测预测这些表示并生成动作。

Result: LeanWAM以1.4B参数、无需大规模具身预训练，在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0上优于强VLA和WAM基线；在A100 GPU上25.1ms预测一个动作块，比代表性WAM快12.3–41.5倍。

Conclusion: 紧凑的预测监督可将视频先验迁移至鲁棒且高效的控制策略，表明策略所需的未来表示可以高度压缩。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) typically adapt large pretrained video generators to jointly predict future observations and robot actions, incurring substantial training and deployment costs. However, it remains unclear which future representations a policy actually needs and how compactly they can be represented. Our probing study shows that complementary future representations can be compressed into a few tokens while retaining most of their action-decoding performance. Motivated by this finding, we introduce LeanWAM, a latent WAM that transfers video priors from a frozen video generator to a lightweight policy via two-stage training. In the first stage, a Future Extractor compresses observed transitions into 32 predictive tokens. These tokens condition the frozen generator and are jointly shaped by action, RGB, semantic, and geometric supervision, while a compact readout of the generator's hidden states supplies complementary world tokens. The second stage distills both representations into the policy, which learns to predict them from current observations while generating actions. With 1.4B parameters and no large-scale embodied pretraining, LeanWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin~2.0. It predicts an action chunk in 25.1\,ms on an A100 GPU, 12.3--41.5$\times$ faster than representative WAMs. These results demonstrate that compact predictive supervision can transfer video priors to robust and efficient control.

</details>
